        tvBigPing = findViewById(R.id.tvBigPing); tvBigLoss = findViewById(R.id.tvBigLoss)
        tvGChartStats = findViewById(R.id.tvGChartStats); tvGJitter = findViewById(R.id.tvGJitter)
        tvGMinMax = findViewById(R.id.tvGMinMax); tvGBb = findViewById(R.id.tvGBb)
        bannerReady = findViewById(R.id.bannerReady); tvReadyTitle = findViewById(R.id.tvReadyTitle); tvReadySub = findViewById(R.id.tvReadySub)
        tvGameTitle = findViewById(R.id.tvGameTitle); tvGameSub = findViewById(R.id.tvGameSub)
        tvGameTitle.text = profile.name
        btnGPing = findViewById(R.id.btnGPing)
        loadLastSummary()
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGBack).setOnClickListener { finish() }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnGRowWifi).setOnClickListener { findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGDiag).performClick() }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnGRowVpn).setOnClickListener { findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGVpn).performClick() }
        sectionMain = findViewById(R.id.sectionGMain); sectionHistory = findViewById(R.id.sectionGHistory)
        tvMain.text = "پروفایل: ${profile.name}\nمنطقه: ${region.name}\n\nتست پینگ را بزن. توجه: سرور واقعی بازی Unknown است — این تست بنچمارک عمومی است."

        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGMenu).setOnClickListener { v ->
            val popup = PopupMenu(this, v)
            popup.menu.add(0, 1, 0, "داشبورد گیمینگ")
            popup.menu.add(0, 2, 0, "تاریخچه و گزارش مسابقه‌ها")
            popup.menu.add(0, 3, 0, "پروفایل: COD Mobile")
            popup.menu.add(0, 4, 0, "پروفایل: PUBG Mobile")
            popup.menu.add(0, 5, 0, "پروفایل: Warzone Mobile")
            popup.menu.add(0, 6, 0, "منطقه: Middle East")
            popup.menu.add(0, 7, 0, "منطقه: Europe")
            popup.menu.add(0, 8, 0, "منطقه: Asia")
            popup.menu.add(0, 9, 0, "منطقه: North America")
            popup.setOnMenuItemClickListener { item: MenuItem ->
                when (item.itemId) {
                    1 -> show(sectionMain)
                    2 -> {
                        val sessions = HistoryEngine.gamingSessions(this)
                        val sb = StringBuilder("جلسه‌های گیمینگ ثبت‌شده (${sessions.size}):\n")
                        for (gs in sessions.take(10)) {
                            sb.append("${History.dateTimeStr(gs.started)} — ${gs.profile} • ${gs.region} • avg ${"%.0f".format(gs.avgMs)}ms • loss ${"%.1f".format(gs.loss)}٪ • score ${gs.score ?: "—"} • Bufferbloat ${gs.bbGrade}\n")
                        }
                        val logSessions = Gaming.log(this).lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                        fun avgOf(list: List<String>): String { val avgs = list.mapNotNull { Regex("avg ([0-9.]+)ms").find(it)?.groupValues?.get(1)?.toDoubleOrNull() }; return if (avgs.isEmpty()) "—" else "%.0f".format(avgs.average()) + "ms" }
                        val byHour = logSessions.groupBy { it.substring(11, 13) }.map { (h, list) -> "ساعت $h: ${list.size} جلسه، میانگین ${avgOf(list)}" }.joinToString(" • ")
                        val byDay = logSessions.groupBy { it.substring(0, 10) }.map { (d, list) -> "$d: ${list.size} جلسه، میانگین ${avgOf(list)}" }.joinToString(" • ")
                        tvHistory.text = sb.toString() + "\nمقایسه ساعات روز: $byHour\nمقایسه روزها: $byDay\n(میانگین پینگ هر ساعت/روز از روی خط‌های بالا قابل مقایسه است — داده فقط از جلسه‌های ثبت‌شده خودت است.)" + "\n\n📈 تاریخچه کامل Ping Spikeها:\n" + History.spikes(this).ifBlank { "هنوز Spike ثبت نشده." } + "\n\nمسابقه‌ها (پینگ داخل بازی — دستی):\n" + Gaming.matches(this)
                        show(sectionHistory)
                    }
                    3, 4, 5 -> {
                        if (monitoringRunning()) { Toast.makeText(this, "اول مانیتورینگ را متوقف کن، بعد پروفایل را عوض کن.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show(); return@setOnMenuItemClickListener true }
                        profile = Gaming.profiles[item.itemId - 3]; Toast.makeText(this, profile.name, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                    6, 7, 8, 9 -> {
                        if (monitoringRunning()) { Toast.makeText(this, "اول مانیتورینگ را متوقف کن، بعد منطقه را عوض کن.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show(); return@setOnMenuItemClickListener true }
                        region = Gaming.regions[item.itemId - 6]
                    }
                }
                tvMain.text = "پروفایل: ${profile.name}\nمنطقه: ${region.name}"
                tvGameTitle.text = profile.name
                true
            }
            popup.show()
        }
        btnGPing.setOnClickListener { runPingTest() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnRange1).setOnClickListener { setRangeChip(1); Toast.makeText(this, "مدت مانیتورینگ: ۱ دقیقه واقعی", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnRange5).setOnClickListener { setRangeChip(5); Toast.makeText(this, "مدت مانیتورینگ: ۵ دقیقه واقعی", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnRange15).setOnClickListener { setRangeChip(15); Toast.makeText(this, "مدت مانیتورینگ: ۱۵ دقیقه واقعی", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnRange30).setOnClickListener { setRangeChip(30); Toast.makeText(this, "مدت مانیتورینگ: ۳۰ دقیقه واقعی", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGBuffer).setOnClickListener { runBufferbloat() }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGStability).setOnClickListener {
            if (stabFuture?.isDone == false) {
                stabStop.set(true); NetExecutor.cancel(stabFuture)
                Toast.makeText(this, "تست پایداری متوقف شد.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show(); return@setOnClickListener
            }
            stabStop.set(false)
            val stabSession = HistoryEngine.startGamingSession(this, profile.name, region.name + " (پایداری)", "1.1.1.1")
            tvMain.text = "تست پایداری ۵ دقیقه‌ای شروع شد — هر ۵ ثانیه یک پینگ. اگر بازی در حال اجراست، تست سنگین به‌صورت خودکار سبک می‌شود."
            val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
            stabFuture = NetExecutor.submit {
                val times = mutableListOf<Double>(); val stabPts = mutableListOf<Pair<Long, Double>>(); var drops = 0
                try {
                    while (!stabStop.get() && System.currentTimeMillis() - start < 300000) {
                        val tickStart = System.currentTimeMillis()
                        val p = NetworkEngine.ping("1.1.1.1", 1)
                        if (stabStop.get()) break
                        if (p.received == 0) { drops++; stabPts.add(System.currentTimeMillis() to -1.0); HistoryEngine.addGamingSample(this@GamingActivity, stabSession, -1.0, false) }
                        else for (t in p.times) { times.add(t); stabPts.add(System.currentTimeMillis() to t); HistoryEngine.addGamingSample(this@GamingActivity, stabSession, t, true) }
                        val snap = times.toList(); val sDrops = drops; val snapPts = stabPts.toList()
                        val el = (System.currentTimeMillis() - start) / 1000
                        NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                            if (isFinishing || isDestroyed) return@runOnMain
                            tvStats.text = "تست پایداری: ${el}s / 300s • نمونه‌ها: ${snap.size} • قطعی کوتاه: $sDrops • میانگین: ${if (snap.isEmpty()) "—" else "%.0f".format(snap.average())}ms"
                            graph.points = snapPts
                        }
                        val spent = System.currentTimeMillis() - tickStart
                        if (5000L - spent > 0) {
                            try { Thread.sleep(5000L - spent) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { break }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {}
                val fTimes = times.toList(); val fDrops = drops
                NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                    if (isFinishing || isDestroyed) return@runOnMain
                    if (!stabStop.get()) {
                        tvMain.text = "✅ تست پایداری ۵ دقیقه‌ای کامل شد. قطعی کوتاه: $fDrops"
                        val avg = if (fTimes.isEmpty()) 0.0 else fTimes.average()
                        HistoryEngine.finishGamingSession(this@GamingActivity, stabSession, fTimes.size + fDrops, fTimes.size, if (fTimes.isEmpty()) 100.0 else fDrops * 100.0 / (fTimes.size + fDrops), avg, fTimes.minOrNull() ?: 0.0, fTimes.maxOrNull() ?: 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0, 0, null, "-")
                    } else tvMain.text = "تست پایداری متوقف شد — ${fTimes.size} نمونه ثبت شد."
                }
            }
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGRoute).setOnClickListener {
            tvMain.text = "در حال Traceroute…"
            NetExecutor.submit {
                val tr = NetworkEngine.traceroute("8.8.8.8"); val tcp = NetworkEngine.tcp("8.8.8.8", 443)
                NetExecutor.runOnMain { if (!isFinishing && !isDestroyed) { tvDiag.text = "ICMP Traceroute تا 8.8.8.8:\n$tr\nTCP :443 به 8.8.8.8: ${if (tcp.ok) "${tcp.ms}ms" else "ناموفق (${tcp.note})"}\nتوجه: پاسخ ندادن یک Hop به ICMP لزوماً Packet Loss واقعی نیست؛ خیلی از روترها ICMP را جواب نمی‌دهند."; tvMain.text = "✅ مسیر بررسی شد." } }
            }
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGVpn).setOnClickListener {
            val vpn = Geo.vpnOn(this)
            NetExecutor.submit {
                val p = NetworkEngine.ping("1.1.1.1", 10)
                val snap = "VPN ${if (vpn) "روشن" else "خاموش"} • پینگ ${"%.0f".format(p.avgMs)}ms • جیتر ${"%.1f".format(p.jitterMs)} • قطعی ${"%.1f".format(p.lossPct)}٪ • لوکیشن: ${Geo.fetch().let { "${it.city}, ${it.country}" }}"
                NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                    if (isFinishing || isDestroyed) return@runOnMain
                    if (vpnSnapA == null) { vpnSnapA = snap; tvMain.text = "اسنپ‌شات اول ذخیره شد:\n$snap\n\nحالا VPN را عوض کن (روشن/خاموش) و دوباره همین دکمه را بزن تا مقایسه شود." }
                    else { tvMain.text = "مقایسه VPN:\nقبل: $vpnSnapA\nالان: $snap"; vpnSnapA = null }
                }
            }
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGMatch).setOnClickListener {
            val p = lastPing
            val inGame = "— (داخل بازی ببین و اینجا ثبت کن)"
            Gaming.saveMatch(this, profile.name, "Multiplayer", inGame, "بنچمارک قبل از مسابقه: ${if (p == null) "—" else "%.0f".format(p.avgMs) + "ms"}")
            Toast.makeText(this, "گزارش مسابقه ذخیره شد. پینگ داخل بازی را از صفحه‌ی بازی بخوان و در تاریخچه یادداشت کن — اپ بدون VPNService به ترافیک بازی دسترسی ندارد.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            tvHistory.text = "تست‌ها:\n" + Gaming.log(this) + "\n\nمسابقه‌ها:\n" + Gaming.matches(this)
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGChart).setOnClickListener {
            try {
                val bmp = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(graph.width.coerceAtLeast(100), graph.height.coerceAtLeast(100), android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
                val canvas = android.graphics.Canvas(bmp)
                graph.draw(canvas)
                val f = File(getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-gaming-chart.png")
                f.outputStream().use { bmp.compress(android.graphics.Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 100, it) }
                Toast.makeText(this, "نمودار ذخیره شد: ${f.absolutePath}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            } catch (e: Exception) { Toast.makeText(this, "خروجی نمودار ناموفق: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGExport).setOnClickListener {
            val f = File(getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-gaming.csv")
            f.writeText("time,profile,region,avg_ms,jitter_ms,loss_pct,p95,score\n" + Gaming.log(this).replace("\n", ";") + "\n")
            val fj = File(getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-gaming.json")
            fj.writeText("{\"log\":\"" + Gaming.log(this).replace("\"", "'").replace("\n", "\\n") + "\",\"matches\":\"" + Gaming.matches(this).replace("\"", "'").replace("\n", "\\n") + "\"}")
            Toast.makeText(this, "خروجی ذخیره شد: ${f.absolutePath}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnGDiag).setOnClickListener {
            tvMain.text = "در حال بررسی تشخیص‌ها…"
            NetExecutor.submit {
                val cap = Advanced.captivePortal().result
                val ip = Advanced.publicIpChange(this)
                val micro = Advanced.microLag()
                // NO bufferbloat here: Advanced.loadedJitter() runs a download
                // test (up to 50 MB) — it must only run from its own button,
                // after the data-use warning.
                val loaded = "Bufferbloat: تست جداگانه با دکمه Bufferbloat (تا ۵۰ مگابایت مصرف دارد)"
                val ports = Advanced.portTest("8.8.8.8", 443) + " • " + Advanced.portTest("1.1.1.1", 53)
                val diag = wifiDiag() + "\n\n" + mobileDiag() + "\n\n" + gameRunning() + "\n\n" + cap + "\n" + ip + "\n" + micro + "\n" + loaded + "\n" + ports + "\n" + Advanced.natType() + "\n" + Advanced.reordering() + "\n\nمحدودیت: اندروید اجازه‌ی دیدن آی‌پی/پورت مقصدهای بازی را بدون VPNService نمی‌دهد؛ VPNService فقط با رضایت صریح تو فعال می‌شود و در این نسخه فعال نیست، پس مقصد بازی Unknown می‌ماند."
                NetExecutor.runOnMain { if (!isFinishing && !isDestroyed) { tvDiag.text = diag; tvMain.text = "✅ تشخیص‌ها کامل شد." } }
            }
        }
        show(sectionMain)
    }

    private var vpnSnapA: String? = null
}

================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View

// Circular gauge ring (Home, mockup v2.3). UI only: progress = % of healthy sources
// from the last check (0-100). Arc runs 270° starting at 135°.
class GaugeView @JvmOverloads constructor(ctx: Context, attrs: AttributeSet? = null) : View(ctx, attrs) {
    var progress: Int = 0
        set(v) { field = v.coerceIn(0, 100); invalidate() }

    private val bgPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#1E293B"); strokeWidth = 26f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE; strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND }
    private val fgPaint = Paint().apply { strokeWidth = 26f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE; strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val pad = 20f
        val rect = RectF(pad, pad, width - pad, height - pad)
        canvas.drawArc(rect, 135f, 270f, false, bgPaint)
        val col = when {
            progress >= 90 -> "#22C55E"
            progress >= 70 -> "#2DD4BF"
            progress >= 40 -> "#F59E0B"
            else -> "#EF4444"
        }
        fgPaint.color = Color.parseColor(col)
        canvas.drawArc(rect, 135f, 270f * progress / 100f, false, fgPaint)
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/Geo.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.os.Build
import android.telephony.TelephonyManager
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

data class GeoInfo(val ip: String, val country: String, val countryCode: String, val city: String, val isp: String, val ok: Boolean)

object Geo {
    // ip-api.com رایگان و بدون کلید است؛ موقعیت آی‌پی فعلی را می‌دهد.
    // اگر VPN روشن باشد، این همان لوکیشن خروجی VPN است؛ اگر خاموش باشد، لوکیشن واقعی اپراتور.
    fun fetch(): GeoInfo {
        // Primary: ip-api.com (free, no key). Fallback: ipapi.co (free, no key)
        // when ip-api does not answer — common on filtered routes / VPN exits.
        // Both failing still shows an honest "not available", never a fake location.
        return fetchIpApi() ?: fetchIpapiCo() ?: GeoInfo("", "", "", "", "", false)
    }

    private fun fetchIpApi(): GeoInfo? {
        return try {
            val conn = URL("http://ip-api.com/json/?fields=status,country,countryCode,city,isp,query").openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
            conn.connectTimeout = 5000; conn.readTimeout = 5000
            val br = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.inputStream))
            val body = br.readText(); br.close()
            if (!body.contains("\"success\"")) return null
            fun f(k: String): String = Regex("\"$k\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"").find(body)?.groupValues?.get(1) ?: ""
            GeoInfo(f("query"), f("country"), f("countryCode"), f("city"), f("isp"), true)
        } catch (e: Exception) { null }
    }

    private fun fetchIpapiCo(): GeoInfo? {
        return try {
            val conn = URL("https://ipapi.co/json/").openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
            conn.connectTimeout = 5000; conn.readTimeout = 5000
            val br = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.inputStream))
            val body = br.readText(); br.close()
            fun f(k: String): String = Regex("\"$k\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"").find(body)?.groupValues?.get(1) ?: ""
            val ip = f("ip"); if (ip.isBlank()) return null
            GeoInfo(ip, f("country_name"), f("country_code"), f("city"), f("org"), true)
        } catch (e: Exception) { null }
    }

    fun operatorName(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            val tm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager
            val op = tm?.networkOperatorName ?: ""
            if (op.isNotBlank()) op else "—"
        } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
    }

    fun connectionType(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            val cm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
            val net = cm.activeNetwork
            val caps = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23 && net != null) cm.getNetworkCapabilities(net) else null
            when {
                caps == null -> "—"
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI) -> {
                    val freq = try {
                        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                        (ctx.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as android.net.wifi.WifiManager).connectionInfo.frequency
                    } catch (e: Exception) { 0 }
                    if (freq >= 5000) "Wi-Fi (5 GHz)" else if (freq > 0) "Wi-Fi (2.4 GHz)" else "Wi-Fi"
                }
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_CELLULAR) -> {
                    val tm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager
                    when (tm?.networkType) {
                        TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_LTE -> "4G LTE"
                        TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_NR -> "5G"
                        TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPAP, TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPA -> "3G"
                        else -> "Mobile Data"
                    }
                }
                else -> "—"
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
    }

    fun vpnOn(ctx: Context): Boolean {
        return try {
            val cm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23) {
                val net = cm.activeNetwork ?: return false
                val caps = cm.getNetworkCapabilities(net) ?: return false
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_VPN)
            } else false
        } catch (e: Exception) { false }
    }

    fun connectionInfo(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            val cm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
            val net = cm.activeNetwork
            val caps = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23 && net != null) cm.getNetworkCapabilities(net) else null
            val tm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager
            val op = try { tm?.networkOperatorName ?: "" } catch (e: Exception) { "" }
            // Underlying type: from network capabilities when available, otherwise
            // from TelephonyManager — mobile data is still determinable when the
            // active network read returns nothing (e.g. while a VPN tunnel is up).
            val underlying: String? = when {
                caps?.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI) == true -> "Wi-Fi"
                caps?.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_CELLULAR) == true -> when (tm?.networkType) {
                    TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_LTE -> "4G LTE"
                    TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_NR -> "5G"
                    TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPAP, TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPA -> "3G"
                    else -> "داده همراه"
                }
                tm?.networkType == TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_LTE -> "4G LTE"
                tm?.networkType == TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_NR -> "5G"
                tm?.networkType == TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPAP || tm?.networkType == TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPA -> "3G"
                tm != null && tm.simState == TelephonyManager.SIM_STATE_READY -> "داده همراه"
                else -> null
            }
            val vpn = vpnOn(ctx)
            val type = when {
                vpn && underlying != null -> "VPN ($underlying)"
                vpn -> "VPN"
                underlying != null -> underlying
                else -> "نامشخص"
            }
            val opSuffix = if (!vpn && op.isNotBlank()) " • $op" else ""
            val vpnSuffix = if (vpn) " • VPN روشن" else " • VPN خاموش"
            type + opSuffix + vpnSuffix
        } catch (e: Exception) { "نامشخص" }
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/GraphView.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

// Dashboard / History / Gaming latency chart (v2.6) — TIME-based, not
// "last 60 samples".
// - points: (timestampMs, ms); ms < 0 means the measurement FAILED: the line
//   breaks there (a visible gap) and a red tick marks the failure at the
//   bottom. Failed samples are never drawn as 0ms.
// - The x axis is real time (first sample time … last sample time, HH:mm).
// - The y axis uses ChartData.yMax (P95-based), so one huge spike does not
//   flatten the rest of the chart; points above it are clipped at the top.
// - pctPoints: optional second series (sources-ok %), drawn on the same
//   time axis — the two series can never be misaligned, because both carry
//   their own timestamps.
class GraphView @JvmOverloads constructor(ctx: Context, attrs: AttributeSet? = null) : View(ctx, attrs) {
    var points: List<Pair<Long, Double>> = emptyList()
        set(v) { field = v; invalidate() }
    var pctPoints: List<Pair<Long, Double>> = emptyList()
        set(v) { field = v; invalidate() }

    private val gridPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#1E293B"); strokeWidth = 2f }
    private val axisPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#7C8AA0"); textSize = 28f; isAntiAlias = true }
    private val linePaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#2DD4BF"); strokeWidth = 4f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE }
    private val fillPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#142E2B"); style = Paint.Style.FILL; alpha = 160 }
    private val failPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#EF4444"); strokeWidth = 5f; isAntiAlias = true }
    private val emptyPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#94A3B8"); textSize = 32f; isAntiAlias = true; textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER }
    private val emptySubPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#7C8AA0"); textSize = 26f; isAntiAlias = true; textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER }

    private fun fmtTime(t: Long): String = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.US).format(Date(t))

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val w = width.toFloat(); val h = height.toFloat()
        val padL = 92f; val padR = 12f; val padT = 12f; val padB = 28f
        val plotW = w - padL - padR; val plotH = h - padT - padB
        val valid = points.filter { it.second >= 0 }.map { it.second }
        val maxV = ChartData.yMax(valid)
        val topLabel = "${"%.0f".format(maxV)}ms"
        val midLabel = "${"%.0f".format(maxV / 2)}ms"
        for (i in 0..2) {
            val y = padT + plotH * i / 2
            canvas.drawLine(padL, y, w - padR, y, gridPaint)
            val label = when (i) { 0 -> topLabel; 1 -> midLabel; else -> "0ms" }
            canvas.drawText(label, 4f, y + 10f, axisPaint)
        }
        if (points.isEmpty()) {
            canvas.drawText("هنوز داده‌ای نیست", w / 2, h / 2 - 8f, emptyPaint)
            canvas.drawText("بعد از اولین بررسی، نمودار اینجا رسم می‌شود", w / 2, h / 2 + 30f, emptySubPaint)
            return
        }
        // Real time axis: first … last sample time.
        val t0 = points.minOf { it.first }
        val t1 = points.maxOf { it.first }
        val span = (t1 - t0).coerceAtLeast(1L)
        fun xOf(t: Long): Float = padL + (t - t0).toFloat() / span * plotW
        fun yOf(ms: Double): Float = padT + plotH - (ms.coerceAtMost(maxV) / maxV * plotH).toFloat()
        canvas.drawText(fmtTime(t0), padL, h - 6f, axisPaint)
        val nowLabel = fmtTime(t1)
        canvas.drawText(nowLabel, w - padR - axisPaint.measureText(nowLabel), h - 6f, axisPaint)
        // Failed samples: red ticks at the bottom (visible gaps, not zeros).
        for (p in points) {
            if (p.second < 0) {
                val x = xOf(p.first)
                canvas.drawLine(x, padT + plotH - 14f, x, padT + plotH, failPaint)
            }
        }
        // Latency line, broken at failures.
        for (seg in ChartData.segments(points)) {
            if (seg.size == 1) {
                canvas.drawCircle(xOf(seg[0].first), yOf(seg[0].second), 7f, linePaint)
                continue
            }
            val path = Path(); val fill = Path()
            for ((i, p) in seg.withIndex()) {
                val x = xOf(p.first); val y = yOf(p.second)
                if (i == 0) { path.moveTo(x, y); fill.moveTo(x, padT + plotH); fill.lineTo(x, y) }
                else { path.lineTo(x, y); fill.lineTo(x, y) }
            }
            fill.lineTo(xOf(seg.last().first), padT + plotH); fill.close()
            canvas.drawPath(fill, fillPaint)
            canvas.drawPath(path, linePaint)
        }
        val last = points.lastOrNull { it.second >= 0 }
        if (last != null) canvas.drawCircle(xOf(last.first), yOf(last.second), 7f, linePaint)
        // Sources-ok % on the same time axis (purple, right side).
        if (pctPoints.isNotEmpty()) {
            val pctPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#A855F7"); strokeWidth = 4f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE }
            val pctPath = Path()
            var started = false
            for (p in pctPoints) {
                val x = xOf(p.first)
                val y = padT + plotH - (p.second.coerceIn(0.0, 100.0) / 100.0 * plotH).toFloat()
                if (!started) { pctPath.moveTo(x, y); started = true } else pctPath.lineTo(x, y)
            }
            canvas.drawPath(pctPath, pctPaint)
            val rightPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#A855F7"); textSize = 26f; isAntiAlias = true }
            canvas.drawText("100%", w - padR - rightPaint.measureText("100%"), padT + 10f, rightPaint)
            canvas.drawText("0%", w - padR - rightPaint.measureText("0%"), padT + plotH, rightPaint)
        }
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/History.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

// History (v2.6): structured samples live in SQLite (HistoryDb); settings,
// logs and the timeline stay in SharedPreferences (append-only logs).
// Old SharedPreferences data (samples string + ping history) is migrated once
// into the database — nothing the user collected is lost.
// All DB writes are synchronized on one lock, so the service and the UI can
// never lose each other's samples.
object History {
    private const val PREF = "netwatch"
    private const val KEY_SAMPLES = "samples"
    private const val KEY_LOG = "log"
    private const val KEY_OUTAGE_START = "outage_start"
    private const val KEY_INTERVAL = "interval_sec"
    private const val KEY_CUSTOM_TARGET = "custom_target"
    private const val KEY_PING_HIST = "ping_hist"
    private const val KEY_MIGRATED = "db_migrated_v1"
    private val dbLock = Any()

    private fun prefs(ctx: Context): SharedPreferences = ctx.getSharedPreferences(PREF, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    fun intervalSec(ctx: Context): Int = prefs(ctx).getInt(KEY_INTERVAL, 30)
    fun setInterval(ctx: Context, sec: Int) { prefs(ctx).edit().putInt(KEY_INTERVAL, sec).apply() }
    fun customTarget(ctx: Context): String = prefs(ctx).getString(KEY_CUSTOM_TARGET, "") ?: ""
    fun setCustomTarget(ctx: Context, t: String) { prefs(ctx).edit().putString(KEY_CUSTOM_TARGET, t).apply() }

    fun livePingOn(ctx: Context): Boolean = prefs(ctx).getBoolean("live_ping", false)
    fun setLivePing(ctx: Context, on: Boolean) { prefs(ctx).edit().putBoolean("live_ping", on).apply() }

    fun alertThreshold(ctx: Context): Int = prefs(ctx).getInt("alert_threshold", 40)
    fun setAlertThreshold(ctx: Context, pct: Int) { prefs(ctx).edit().putInt("alert_threshold", pct).apply() }

    // ---- Service heartbeat (v2.6): the widget's Live pill is based on this,
    // not on a static boolean. lastAttempt = last cycle start, lastSuccess =
    // last cycle that produced a result, lastHeartbeat = last widget refresh
    // the service performed. ----
    fun setHeartbeat(ctx: Context) { prefs(ctx).edit().putLong("last_heartbeat", System.currentTimeMillis()).apply() }
    fun lastHeartbeat(ctx: Context): Long = prefs(ctx).getLong("last_heartbeat", 0L)
    fun setLastAttempt(ctx: Context, t: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()) { prefs(ctx).edit().putLong("last_attempt", t).apply() }
    fun lastAttempt(ctx: Context): Long = prefs(ctx).getLong("last_attempt", 0L)
    fun setLastSuccess(ctx: Context, t: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()) { prefs(ctx).edit().putLong("last_success", t).apply() }
    fun lastSuccess(ctx: Context): Long = prefs(ctx).getLong("last_success", 0L)

    // ---- Migration: old prefs strings -> SQLite, once. ----
    fun migrateIfNeeded(ctx: Context) {
        val p = prefs(ctx)
        if (p.getBoolean(KEY_MIGRATED, false)) return
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            if (p.getBoolean(KEY_MIGRATED, false)) return
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.beginTransaction()
            try {
                val old = p.getString(KEY_SAMPLES, "") ?: ""
                for (line in old.split(";").filter { it.isNotBlank() }) {
                    parseSample(line)?.let { s -> insertSampleRow(db, s.time, s.ok, s.total, s.avgMs, s.jitterMs, s.lossPct, "full") }
                }
                val ph = p.getString(KEY_PING_HIST, "") ?: ""
                for (e in ph.split(";").filter { it.isNotBlank() }) {
                    val a = e.split(",")
                    val t = a.getOrNull(0)?.toLongOrNull()
                    val ms = a.getOrNull(1)?.toDoubleOrNull()
                    if (t != null && ms != null) insertPingRow(db, t, ms, 1)
                }
                db.setTransactionSuccessful()
            } finally { db.endTransaction() }
            p.edit().putBoolean(KEY_MIGRATED, true).apply()
        }
    }

    private fun insertSampleRow(db: SQLiteDatabase, time: Long, ok: Int, total: Int, avgMs: Double, jitterMs: Double, lossPct: Double, source: String) {
        val cv = ContentValues().apply {
            put("time", time); put("ok", ok); put("total", total)
            put("avg_ms", avgMs); put("jitter_ms", jitterMs); put("loss_pct", lossPct); put("source", source)
        }
        db.insert("samples", null, cv)
    }
    private fun insertPingRow(db: SQLiteDatabase, time: Long, ms: Double, ok: Int) {
        val cv = ContentValues().apply { put("time", time); put("ms", ms); put("ok", ok) }
        db.insert("ping_points", null, cv)
    }
    private fun pruneOld(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        val cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - HistoryDb.RETENTION_MS
        db.delete("samples", "time < ?", arrayOf(cutoff.toString()))
        db.delete("ping_points", "time < ?", arrayOf(cutoff.toString()))
        db.delete("loss_points", "time < ?", arrayOf(cutoff.toString()))
    }

    // ---- Samples: one row per check. THE v2.5 BUG was here: addSample wrote
    // 6 fields but samples() only accepted exactly 3, so it always returned
    // an empty list and the widget, gauge, CSV, JSON, PDF and backup all saw
    // no data. samples() is now backed by the same rows addSample writes. ----
    data class Sample(val time: Long, val ok: Int, val total: Int, val avgMs: Double = -1.0, val jitterMs: Double = -1.0, val lossPct: Double = -1.0)
    fun parseSample(line: String): Sample? {
        val a = line.split(",")
        if (a.size < 3) return null
        val time = a[0].toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        val ok = a[1].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
        val total = a[2].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
        return Sample(time, ok, total,
            if (a.size > 3) a[3].toDoubleOrNull() ?: -1.0 else -1.0,
            if (a.size > 4) a[4].toDoubleOrNull() ?: -1.0 else -1.0,
            if (a.size > 5) a[5].toDoubleOrNull() ?: -1.0 else -1.0)
    }

    fun samplesFull(ctx: Context): List<Sample> {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            val out = mutableListOf<Sample>()
            db.query("samples", arrayOf("time", "ok", "total", "avg_ms", "jitter_ms", "loss_pct"), null, null, null, null, "time ASC", "5000").use { c ->
                while (c.moveToNext()) out.add(Sample(c.getLong(0), c.getInt(1), c.getInt(2), c.getDouble(3), c.getDouble(4), c.getDouble(5)))
            }
            return out
        }
    }
    fun samplesInRange(ctx: Context, sinceMs: Long): List<Sample> = samplesFull(ctx).filter { it.time >= sinceMs }
    // Kept for callers that only need (time, ok, total) — now returns the real rows.
    fun samples(ctx: Context): List<Triple<Long, Int, Int>> = samplesFull(ctx).map { Triple(it.time, it.ok, it.total) }

    fun addSample(ctx: Context, ok: Int, total: Int, avgMs: Double = -1.0, jitterMs: Double = -1.0, lossPct: Double = -1.0, source: String = "full") {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.beginTransaction()
            try {
                insertSampleRow(db, now, ok, total, avgMs, jitterMs, lossPct, source)
                pruneOld(db)
                db.setTransactionSuccessful()
            } finally { db.endTransaction() }
        }
        // outage start/end tracking: outage = <40% ok
        val p = prefs(ctx)
        val pct = if (total == 0) 0 else ok * 100 / total
        val start = p.getLong(KEY_OUTAGE_START, 0L)
        if (pct < 40 && start == 0L) {
            p.edit().putLong(KEY_OUTAGE_START, now).apply()
            addTimeline(ctx, "outage-start", "شروع اختلال در ${timeStr(now)} — دسترسی $pct٪ (قبل از آن سالم بود)")
        }
        if (pct >= 70 && start != 0L) {
            val durMin = (now - start) / 60000
            addLog(ctx, "پایان اختلال: از ${timeStr(start)} تا ${timeStr(now)} — حدود $durMin دقیقه")
            addTimeline(ctx, "outage-end", "پایان اختلال: شروع ${timeStr(start)} • پایان ${timeStr(now)} • مدت حدود $durMin دقیقه • دسترسی قبل از اختلال سالم، بعد از آن $pct٪ • علت احتمالی: در گزارش تشخیص نوع اختلال ببین")
            p.edit().putLong(KEY_OUTAGE_START, 0L).apply()
        }
    }

    // ---- Ping points: every measurement with its timestamp. ms < 0 = failed. ----
    // Removes samples/ping/loss rows written at or after a timestamp — used by
    // the Lab round-trip test to clean up its own test rows afterwards.
    fun deleteSamplesSince(ctx: Context, sinceMs: Long) {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.delete("samples", "time >= ?", arrayOf(sinceMs.toString()))
            db.delete("ping_points", "time >= ?", arrayOf(sinceMs.toString()))
            db.delete("loss_points", "time >= ?", arrayOf(sinceMs.toString()))
        }
    }

    fun addPing(ctx: Context, avgMs: Double) {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.beginTransaction()
            try { insertPingRow(db, System.currentTimeMillis(), avgMs, 1); pruneOld(db); db.setTransactionSuccessful() }
            finally { db.endTransaction() }
        }
    }
    fun addPingFailed(ctx: Context) {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.beginTransaction()
            try { insertPingRow(db, System.currentTimeMillis(), -1.0, 0); pruneOld(db); db.setTransactionSuccessful() }
            finally { db.endTransaction() }
        }
    }
    // Time-ordered (time, ms) pairs; failed points carry ms = -1.0 (gap, not 0).
    fun pingPoints(ctx: Context, sinceMs: Long = 0L): List<Pair<Long, Double>> {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            val out = mutableListOf<Pair<Long, Double>>()
            val sel = if (sinceMs > 0) "time >= ?" else null
            val args = if (sinceMs > 0) arrayOf(sinceMs.toString()) else null
            db.query("ping_points", arrayOf("time", "ms"), sel, args, null, null, "time ASC", "5000").use { c ->
                while (c.moveToNext()) out.add(c.getLong(0) to c.getDouble(1))
            }
            return out
        }
    }
    fun pingHistory(ctx: Context): List<Double> = pingPoints(ctx).filter { it.second >= 0 }.map { it.second }
    // Newest SUCCESSFUL ping with its timestamp (widget live tile).
    fun lastPing(ctx: Context): Pair<Long, Double>? {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            db.query("ping_points", arrayOf("time", "ms"), "ok = 1", null, null, null, "time DESC", "1").use { c ->
                return if (c.moveToFirst()) c.getLong(0) to c.getDouble(1) else null
            }
        }
    }
    // Newest ping attempt of any kind (for "last measurement" age).
    fun lastPingTime(ctx: Context): Long {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            db.query("ping_points", arrayOf("MAX(time)"), null, null, null, null, null).use { c ->
                return if (c.moveToFirst() && !c.isNull(0)) c.getLong(0) else 0L
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- Loss points (loss sparkline source). ----
    fun addLoss(ctx: Context, lossPct: Double) {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.beginTransaction()
            try {
                val cv = ContentValues().apply { put("time", System.currentTimeMillis()); put("loss", lossPct) }
                db.insert("loss_points", null, cv)
                pruneOld(db)
                db.setTransactionSuccessful()
            } finally { db.endTransaction() }
        }
    }
    fun lossHistory(ctx: Context): List<Double> {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            val out = mutableListOf<Double>()
            db.query("loss_points", arrayOf("loss"), null, null, null, null, "time ASC", "60").use { c ->
                while (c.moveToNext()) out.add(c.getDouble(0))
            }
            return out
        }
    }
    fun lossPoints(ctx: Context, sinceMs: Long = 0L): List<Pair<Long, Double>> {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            val out = mutableListOf<Pair<Long, Double>>()
            val sel = if (sinceMs > 0) "time >= ?" else null
            val args = if (sinceMs > 0) arrayOf(sinceMs.toString()) else null
            db.query("loss_points", arrayOf("time", "loss"), sel, args, null, null, "time ASC", "5000").use { c ->
                while (c.moveToNext()) out.add(c.getLong(0) to c.getDouble(1))
            }
            return out
        }
    }

    // ---- Gaming sessions (persisted, with per-sample timestamps). ----
    data class GamingSession(val id: Long, val started: Long, val ended: Long, val profile: String, val region: String, val host: String,
        val sent: Int, val received: Int, val loss: Double, val avgMs: Double, val minMs: Double, val maxMs: Double,
        val jitterMs: Double, val p95: Double, val spikes: Int, val burst: Int, val score: Int?, val bbGrade: String)
    fun startGamingSession(ctx: Context, profile: String, region: String, host: String): Long {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put("started", System.currentTimeMillis()); put("ended", 0L)
                put("profile", profile); put("region", region); put("host", host)
                put("sent", 0); put("received", 0); put("loss", 0.0)
                put("avg_ms", 0.0); put("min_ms", 0.0); put("max_ms", 0.0)
                put("jitter_ms", 0.0); put("p95", 0.0); put("spikes", 0); put("burst", 0)
                put("score", null as Int?); put("bb_grade", "-")
            }
            return db.insert("gaming_sessions", null, cv)
        }
    }
    fun addGamingSample(ctx: Context, sessionId: Long, ms: Double, ok: Boolean) {
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            val cv = ContentValues().apply { put("session_id", sessionId); put("time", System.currentTimeMillis()); put("ms", if (ok) ms else -1.0); put("ok", if (ok) 1 else 0) }
            db.insert("gaming_samples", null, cv)
        }
    }
    fun finishGamingSession(ctx: Context, sessionId: Long, sent: Int, received: Int, loss: Double, avgMs: Double, minMs: Double, maxMs: Double, jitterMs: Double, p95: Double, spikes: Int, burst: Int, score: Int?, bbGrade: String) {
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put("ended", System.currentTimeMillis()); put("sent", sent); put("received", received)
                put("loss", loss); put("avg_ms", avgMs); put("min_ms", minMs); put("max_ms", maxMs)
                put("jitter_ms", jitterMs); put("p95", p95); put("spikes", spikes); put("burst", burst)
                put("score", score); put("bb_grade", bbGrade)
            }
            db.update("gaming_sessions", cv, "id = ?", arrayOf(sessionId.toString()))
        }
    }
    fun gamingSessions(ctx: Context): List<GamingSession> {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            val out = mutableListOf<GamingSession>()
            db.query("gaming_sessions", null, null, null, null, null, "started DESC", "50").use { c ->
                while (c.moveToNext()) {
                    out.add(GamingSession(c.getLong(0), c.getLong(1), c.getLong(2), c.getString(3), c.getString(4), c.getString(5),
                        c.getInt(6), c.getInt(7), c.getDouble(8), c.getDouble(9), c.getDouble(10), c.getDouble(11),
                        c.getDouble(12), c.getDouble(13), c.getInt(14), c.getInt(15),
                        if (c.isNull(16)) null else c.getInt(16), c.getString(17)))
                }
            }
            return out
        }
    }
    fun gamingSessionSamples(ctx: Context, sessionId: Long): List<Pair<Long, Double>> {
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).readableDatabase
            val out = mutableListOf<Pair<Long, Double>>()
            db.query("gaming_samples", arrayOf("time", "ms"), "session_id = ?", arrayOf(sessionId.toString()), null, null, "time ASC").use { c ->
                while (c.moveToNext()) out.add(c.getLong(0) to c.getDouble(1))
            }
            return out
        }
    }

    // ---- Last full check, for the widget. ----
    data class LastCheck(val pingAvg: Double, val jitterMs: Double, val lossPct: Double, val intlPct: Int, val domPct: Int, val scoreTotal: Int?, val scoreStatus: String, val checkedAt: Long = 0)
    fun saveLastCheck(ctx: Context, pingAvg: Double, jitterMs: Double, lossPct: Double, intlPct: Int, domPct: Int, scoreTotal: Int?, scoreStatus: String) {
        prefs(ctx).edit()
            .putString("last_check", "$pingAvg|$jitterMs|$lossPct|$intlPct|$domPct|${scoreTotal ?: ""}|$scoreStatus|${System.currentTimeMillis()}").apply()
    }
    fun lastCheck(ctx: Context): LastCheck? {
        val t = prefs(ctx).getString("last_check", "") ?: ""
        if (t.isBlank()) return null
        val a = t.split("|")
        if (a.size < 7) return null
        return LastCheck(a[0].toDoubleOrNull() ?: -1.0, a[1].toDoubleOrNull() ?: -1.0, a[2].toDoubleOrNull() ?: -1.0, a[3].toIntOrNull() ?: 0, a[4].toIntOrNull() ?: 0, a[5].toIntOrNull(), a[6], if (a.size > 7) a[7].toLongOrNull() ?: 0 else 0)
    }
    fun saveConnType(ctx: Context, t: String) {
        if (t != "—") prefs(ctx).edit().putString("last_conn_type", t).apply()
    }
    fun connType(ctx: Context): String? {
        val t = prefs(ctx).getString("last_conn_type", "") ?: ""
        return if (t.isBlank()) null else t
    }

    // ---- Logs / timeline (append-only, stay in prefs). ----
    fun addLog(ctx: Context, line: String) {
        val p = prefs(ctx)
        val old = p.getString(KEY_LOG, "")!!
        val lines = (line + "\n" + old).lines().take(200).joinToString("\n")
        p.edit().putString(KEY_LOG, lines).apply()
    }
    fun log(ctx: Context): String = prefs(ctx).getString(KEY_LOG, "") ?: ""

    fun compareText(ctx: Context): String {
        val lines = Gaming.log(ctx).lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }
        if (lines.size < 2) return "مقایسه تست‌ها: هنوز دو تست گیمینگ ثبت نشده."
        val a = lines[0]; val b = lines[1]
        val hourlyData = hourly(ctx).split(";").filter { it.isNotBlank() }
        val avg = if (hourlyData.isEmpty()) 0 else hourlyData.mapNotNull { it.split(":").getOrNull(1)?.toIntOrNull() }.average()
        return "مقایسه دو تست آخر گیمینگ:\nآخرین: $a\nقبلی: $b\n\nمیانگین دسترسی ساعتی (ISP): ${"%.0f".format(avg)}٪ از ${hourlyData.size} نمونه ساعتی — نمودار روزانه/هفتگی از همین داده‌هاست."
    }
    fun addTimeline(ctx: Context, kind: String, line: String) {
        val p = prefs(ctx)
        val old = (p.getString("timeline", "") ?: "").lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }.toMutableList()
        old.add(0, "${timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())} [$kind] $line")
        while (old.size > 100) old.removeAt(old.size - 1)
        p.edit().putString("timeline", old.joinToString("\n")).apply()
    }
    data class TimelineEvent(val time: String, val kind: String, val text: String)
    fun parseTimeline(text: String): List<TimelineEvent> {
        return text.lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }.map { line ->
            val open = line.indexOf(" [")
            val close = if (open >= 0) line.indexOf("] ", open) else -1
            if (open < 0 || close < 0) TimelineEvent("", "", line)
            else TimelineEvent(line.substring(0, open), line.substring(open + 2, close), line.substring(close + 2))
        }
    }
    fun timeline(ctx: Context): String = prefs(ctx).getString("timeline", "") ?: ""
    fun addSpike(ctx: Context, ms: Double) {
        val p = ctx.getSharedPreferences("gaming", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val old = (p.getString("spikes", "") ?: "").lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }.toMutableList()
        old.add(0, "${timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())} — Spike ${"%.0f".format(ms)}ms")
        while (old.size > 100) old.removeAt(old.size - 1)
        p.edit().putString("spikes", old.joinToString("\n")).apply()
    }
    fun spikes(ctx: Context): String = ctx.getSharedPreferences("gaming", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).getString("spikes", "") ?: ""
    fun addHourly(ctx: Context, ok: Int, total: Int) {
        val p = prefs(ctx)
        val hour = SimpleDateFormat("HH", Locale.US).format(Date())
        val old = p.getString("hourly", "") ?: ""
        p.edit().putString("hourly", (old + ";" + hour + ":" + (if (total == 0) 0 else ok * 100 / total)).split(";").filter { it.isNotBlank() }.takeLast(96).joinToString(";")).apply()
    }
    fun hourly(ctx: Context): String = prefs(ctx).getString("hourly", "") ?: ""
    fun blackbox(ctx: Context, line: String) {
        val p = prefs(ctx)
        val old = (p.getString("blackbox", "") ?: "").lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }.toMutableList()
        old.add("${timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())} $line")
        while (old.size > 20) old.removeAt(0)
        p.edit().putString("blackbox", old.joinToString("\n")).apply()
    }
    fun blackboxLog(ctx: Context): String = prefs(ctx).getString("blackbox", "") ?: ""

    fun clearAll(ctx: Context) {
        prefs(ctx).edit().clear().apply()
        ctx.getSharedPreferences("gaming", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().clear().apply()
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.delete("samples", null, null); db.delete("ping_points", null, null)
            db.delete("loss_points", null, null); db.delete("gaming_sessions", null, null); db.delete("gaming_samples", null, null)
        }
    }

    // ---- Backup / restore: one valid JSON document (org.json — no hand-built
    // string escaping). Restore validates the structure + version first and
    // writes everything in ONE transaction, so a corrupt file can never
    // destroy the existing data. ----
    fun buildBackupJson(ctx: Context): String {
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        val root = org.json.JSONObject()
        root.put("version", 1)
        root.put("exported_at", dateTimeStr(System.currentTimeMillis()))
        val samples = org.json.JSONArray()
        for (s in samplesFull(ctx)) {
            val o = org.json.JSONObject()
            o.put("time", s.time); o.put("ok", s.ok); o.put("total", s.total)
            o.put("avg_ms", s.avgMs); o.put("jitter_ms", s.jitterMs); o.put("loss_pct", s.lossPct)
            samples.put(o)
        }
        root.put("samples", samples)
        val pings = org.json.JSONArray()
        for ((t, ms) in pingPoints(ctx)) { val o = org.json.JSONObject(); o.put("time", t); o.put("ms", ms); pings.put(o) }
        root.put("ping_points", pings)
        val losses = org.json.JSONArray()
        for ((t, l) in lossPoints(ctx)) { val o = org.json.JSONObject(); o.put("time", t); o.put("loss", l); losses.put(o) }
        root.put("loss_points", losses)
        root.put("log", log(ctx))
        root.put("timeline", timeline(ctx))
        root.put("hourly", hourly(ctx))
        root.put("blackbox", blackboxLog(ctx))
        root.put("gaming_log", Gaming.log(ctx))
        root.put("gaming_matches", Gaming.matches(ctx))
        root.put("spikes", spikes(ctx))
        val sessions = org.json.JSONArray()
        for (gs in gamingSessions(ctx)) {
            val o = org.json.JSONObject()
            o.put("started", gs.started); o.put("ended", gs.ended); o.put("profile", gs.profile); o.put("region", gs.region)
            o.put("host", gs.host); o.put("sent", gs.sent); o.put("received", gs.received); o.put("loss", gs.loss)
            o.put("avg_ms", gs.avgMs); o.put("min_ms", gs.minMs); o.put("max_ms", gs.maxMs); o.put("jitter_ms", gs.jitterMs)
            o.put("p95", gs.p95); o.put("spikes", gs.spikes); o.put("burst", gs.burst)
            if (gs.score != null) o.put("score", gs.score) else o.put("score", org.json.JSONObject.NULL)
            o.put("bb_grade", gs.bbGrade)
            val ss = org.json.JSONArray()
            for ((t, ms) in gamingSessionSamples(ctx, gs.id)) { val so = org.json.JSONObject(); so.put("time", t); so.put("ms", ms); ss.put(so) }
            o.put("samples", ss)
            sessions.put(o)
        }
        root.put("gaming_sessions", sessions)
        val lc = lastCheck(ctx)
        if (lc != null) {
            val o = org.json.JSONObject()
            o.put("ping_avg", lc.pingAvg); o.put("jitter_ms", lc.jitterMs); o.put("loss_pct", lc.lossPct)
            o.put("intl_pct", lc.intlPct); o.put("dom_pct", lc.domPct)
            if (lc.scoreTotal != null) o.put("score_total", lc.scoreTotal) else o.put("score_total", org.json.JSONObject.NULL)
            o.put("score_status", lc.scoreStatus); o.put("checked_at", lc.checkedAt)
            root.put("last_check", o)
        }
        val settings = org.json.JSONObject()
        settings.put("interval_sec", intervalSec(ctx)); settings.put("alert_threshold", alertThreshold(ctx))
        settings.put("live_ping", livePingOn(ctx)); settings.put("custom_target", customTarget(ctx))
        settings.put("dns_rounds", prefs(ctx).getInt("dns_rounds", 20))
        settings.put("alerts_on", prefs(ctx).getBoolean("alerts_on", true))
        root.put("settings", settings)
        return root.toString()
    }

    // Returns a human-readable result: "OK" or the exact reason it failed.
    fun restoreFromJson(ctx: Context, json: String): String {
        val root = try { org.json.JSONObject(json) } catch (e: Exception) { return "فایل بکاپ خراب است: JSON معتبر نیست (${e.message})" }
        if (!root.has("version")) return "فایل بکاپ خراب است: نسخه فایل مشخص نیست"
        if (root.optInt("version", -1) != 1) return "نسخه فایل بکاپ پشتیبانی نمی‌شود: ${root.optInt("version", -1)}"
        if (!root.has("samples") || !root.has("ping_points")) return "فایل بکاپ ناقص است: بخش نمونه‌ها وجود ندارد"
        migrateIfNeeded(ctx)
        synchronized(dbLock) {
            val db = HistoryDb.get(ctx).writableDatabase
            db.beginTransaction()
            try {
                db.delete("samples", null, null); db.delete("ping_points", null, null)
                db.delete("loss_points", null, null); db.delete("gaming_sessions", null, null); db.delete("gaming_samples", null, null)
                val sa = root.getJSONArray("samples")
                for (i in 0 until sa.length()) {
                    val o = sa.getJSONObject(i)
                    insertSampleRow(db, o.getLong("time"), o.getInt("ok"), o.getInt("total"),
                        o.optDouble("avg_ms", -1.0), o.optDouble("jitter_ms", -1.0), o.optDouble("loss_pct", -1.0), "restore")
                }
                val pa = root.getJSONArray("ping_points")
                for (i in 0 until pa.length()) {
                    val o = pa.getJSONObject(i)
                    insertPingRow(db, o.getLong("time"), o.getDouble("ms"), if (o.getDouble("ms") >= 0) 1 else 0)
                }
                if (root.has("loss_points")) {
                    val la = root.getJSONArray("loss_points")
                    for (i in 0 until la.length()) {
                        val o = la.getJSONObject(i)
                        val cv = ContentValues().apply { put("time", o.getLong("time")); put("loss", o.getDouble("loss")) }
                        db.insert("loss_points", null, cv)
                    }
                }
                if (root.has("gaming_sessions")) {
                    val ga = root.getJSONArray("gaming_sessions")
                    for (i in 0 until ga.length()) {
                        val o = ga.getJSONObject(i)
                        val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                            put("started", o.optLong("started", 0)); put("ended", o.optLong("ended", 0))
                            put("profile", o.optString("profile", "")); put("region", o.optString("region", ""))
                            put("host", o.optString("host", "")); put("sent", o.optInt("sent", 0)); put("received", o.optInt("received", 0))
                            put("loss", o.optDouble("loss", 0.0)); put("avg_ms", o.optDouble("avg_ms", 0.0))
                            put("min_ms", o.optDouble("min_ms", 0.0)); put("max_ms", o.optDouble("max_ms", 0.0))
                            put("jitter_ms", o.optDouble("jitter_ms", 0.0)); put("p95", o.optDouble("p95", 0.0))
                            put("spikes", o.optInt("spikes", 0)); put("burst", o.optInt("burst", 0))
                            if (o.isNull("score")) putNull("score") else put("score", o.getInt("score"))
                            put("bb_grade", o.optString("bb_grade", "-"))
                        }
                        val sid = db.insert("gaming_sessions", null, cv)
                        if (o.has("samples")) {
                            val ss = o.getJSONArray("samples")
                            for (j in 0 until ss.length()) {
                                val so = ss.getJSONObject(j)
                                val scv = ContentValues().apply {
                                    put("session_id", sid); put("time", so.getLong("time")); put("ms", so.getDouble("ms"))
                                    put("ok", if (so.getDouble("ms") >= 0) 1 else 0)
                                }
                                db.insert("gaming_samples", null, scv)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                db.setTransactionSuccessful()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                return "بازیابی ناموفق شد: ${e.javaClass.simpleName} — داده‌های قبلی دست نخورده باقی ماند"
            } finally { db.endTransaction() }
        }
        val p = prefs(ctx).edit()
        if (root.has("log")) p.putString(KEY_LOG, root.optString("log", ""))
        if (root.has("timeline")) p.putString("timeline", root.optString("timeline", ""))
        if (root.has("hourly")) p.putString("hourly", root.optString("hourly", ""))
        if (root.has("blackbox")) p.putString("blackbox", root.optString("blackbox", ""))
        if (root.has("settings")) {
            val s = root.getJSONObject("settings")
            p.putInt(KEY_INTERVAL, s.optInt("interval_sec", 30))
            p.putInt("alert_threshold", s.optInt("alert_threshold", 40))
            p.putBoolean("live_ping", s.optBoolean("live_ping", false))
            p.putString(KEY_CUSTOM_TARGET, s.optString("custom_target", ""))
            p.putInt("dns_rounds", s.optInt("dns_rounds", 20))
            p.putBoolean("alerts_on", s.optBoolean("alerts_on", true))
        }
        p.apply()
        if (root.has("gaming_log")) ctx.getSharedPreferences("gaming", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("log", root.optString("gaming_log", "")).apply()
        if (root.has("gaming_matches")) ctx.getSharedPreferences("gaming", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("matches", root.optString("gaming_matches", "")).apply()
        if (root.has("spikes")) ctx.getSharedPreferences("gaming", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("spikes", root.optString("spikes", "")).apply()
        if (root.has("last_check")) {
            val o = root.getJSONObject("last_check")
            prefs(ctx).edit().putString("last_check",
                "${o.optDouble("ping_avg", -1.0)}|${o.optDouble("jitter_ms", -1.0)}|${o.optDouble("loss_pct", -1.0)}|${o.optInt("intl_pct", 0)}|${o.optInt("dom_pct", 0)}|${if (o.isNull("score_total")) "" else o.getInt("score_total")}|${o.optString("score_status", "")}|${o.optLong("checked_at", 0)}").apply()
        }
        return "OK"
    }

    fun backup(ctx: Context): File {
        val f = File(ctx.getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-backup.json")
        f.writeText(buildBackupJson(ctx))
        return f
    }
    fun restore(ctx: Context): Boolean {
        return try {
            val f = File(ctx.getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-backup.json")
            if (!f.exists()) return false
            restoreFromJson(ctx, f.readText()) == "OK"
        } catch (e: Exception) { false }
    }
    fun backupToUri(ctx: Context, uri: android.net.Uri): Boolean {
        return try {
            ctx.contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri)?.use { it.write(buildBackupJson(ctx).toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)) }
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) { false }
    }
    // SAF restore with the exact failure reason (used by MainActivity's toast).
    fun restoreFromUriReason(ctx: Context, uri: android.net.Uri): String {
        return try {
            val text = ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.bufferedReader(Charsets.UTF_8)?.use { it.readText() }
                ?: return "فایل خوانده نشد."
            restoreFromJson(ctx, text)
        } catch (e: Exception) { e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName }
    }

    fun restoreFromUri(ctx: Context, uri: android.net.Uri): Boolean {
        return try {
            val bytes = ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.readBytes() ?: return false
            restoreFromJson(ctx, String(bytes, Charsets.UTF_8)) == "OK"
        } catch (e: Exception) { false }
    }

    // ---- Exports: full timestamps, real metrics, valid formats. ----
    fun exportCsv(ctx: Context): File {
        val f = File(ctx.getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-history.csv")
        val sb = StringBuilder("time,ok,total,pct,avg_ms,jitter_ms,loss_pct\n")
        for (s in samplesFull(ctx)) {
            val pct = if (s.total == 0) 0 else s.ok * 100 / s.total
            sb.append("\"${dateTimeStr(s.time)}\",${s.ok},${s.total},$pct,${fmtNum(s.avgMs)},${fmtNum(s.jitterMs)},${fmtNum(s.lossPct)}\n")
        }
        f.writeText(sb.toString())
        return f
    }
    private fun fmtNum(v: Double): String = if (v < 0) "" else "%.1f".format(v)

    fun exportJson(ctx: Context): File {
        val f = File(ctx.getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-history.json")
        f.writeText(buildBackupJson(ctx))
        return f
    }

    // Real Persian PDF: multi-page, RTL (text drawn from the right edge),
    // wrapped lines, full timestamps. Android PdfDocument renders Persian
    // shaping/joining correctly with the system font.
    fun exportPdf(ctx: Context): File {
        val f = File(ctx.getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-report.pdf")
        val doc = android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument()
        val paint = android.graphics.Paint().apply { textSize = 12f; color = android.graphics.Color.BLACK }
        val titlePaint = android.graphics.Paint().apply { textSize = 18f; color = android.graphics.Color.BLACK; isFakeBoldText = true }
        val headPaint = android.graphics.Paint().apply { textSize = 14f; color = android.graphics.Color.BLACK; isFakeBoldText = true }
        val pw = 595f; val ph = 842f; val margin = 40f; val rightX = pw - margin
        var pageNum = 1
        var page = doc.startPage(android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(pw.toInt(), ph.toInt(), pageNum).create())
        var canvas = page.canvas
        var y = 50f
        fun newPage() {
            doc.finishPage(page)
            pageNum++
            page = doc.startPage(android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(pw.toInt(), ph.toInt(), pageNum).create())
            canvas = page.canvas; y = 50f
        }
        fun drawRtl(t: String, p: android.graphics.Paint) {
            // Wrap a long line to the page width, right-aligned.
            var rest = t
            while (rest.isNotEmpty()) {
                var n = rest.length
                while (n > 0 && p.measureText(rest, 0, n) > pw - 2 * margin) n--
                if (n == 0) n = 1
                val part = rest.substring(0, n)
                canvas.drawText(part, rightX - p.measureText(part), y, p)
                y += 20f
                if (y > ph - 60) newPage()
                rest = rest.substring(n).trimStart()
            }
        }
        fun heading(t: String) { y += 8f; drawRtl(t, headPaint); y += 4f }
        drawRtl("NetWatch — Diagnostic Report", titlePaint)
        drawRtl("تاریخ گزارش: " + dateTimeStr(System.currentTimeMillis()), paint)
        drawRtl("Connection: local only, no server upload", paint)
        heading("تاریخچه بررسی‌ها (Ping / Jitter / Loss):")
        val samples = samplesFull(ctx)
        if (samples.isEmpty()) drawRtl("هنوز بررسی‌ای ثبت نشده است.", paint)
        for (s in samples.takeLast(200)) {
            val pct = if (s.total == 0) 0 else s.ok * 100 / s.total
            drawRtl("${dateTimeStr(s.time)} — ${s.ok}/${s.total} سالم ($pct٪) • Ping ${if (s.avgMs < 0) "—" else "%.0f".format(s.avgMs) + "ms"} • Jitter ${if (s.jitterMs < 0) "—" else "%.1f".format(s.jitterMs) + "ms"} • Loss ${if (s.lossPct < 0) "—" else "%.1f".format(s.lossPct) + "٪"}", paint)
        }
        heading("Gaming sessions:")
        val sessions = gamingSessions(ctx)
        if (sessions.isEmpty()) drawRtl("هنوز جلسه گیمینگ ثبت نشده است.", paint)
        for (gs in sessions) {
            drawRtl("${dateTimeStr(gs.started)} — ${gs.profile} • ${gs.region} • avg ${"%.0f".format(gs.avgMs)}ms • loss ${"%.1f".format(gs.loss)}٪ • score ${gs.score ?: "—"} • Bufferbloat ${gs.bbGrade}", paint)
        }
        heading("Blackbox (last 20 checks):")
        for (raw in blackboxLog(ctx).lines().take(20)) drawRtl(raw, paint)
        heading("History log:")
        for (raw in log(ctx).lines().take(100)) drawRtl(raw, paint)
        doc.finishPage(page)
        try { f.outputStream().use { doc.writeTo(it) } } finally { doc.close() }
        return f
    }

    fun timeStr(t: Long): String = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(Date(t))
    fun dateTimeStr(t: Long): String = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(Date(t))
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/HistoryDb.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper

// SQLite storage (v2.6) — replaces the single big SharedPreferences string.
// Tables:
// - samples: one row per check (full or background), with ping/jitter/loss.
// - ping_points: every ping measurement with its timestamp; ms < 0 = failed
//   (charts draw a gap for failures, never 0ms).
// - loss_points: per-check packet-loss values with timestamps (loss sparkline).
// - gaming_sessions / gaming_samples: persisted gaming sessions with
//   per-sample timestamps, so a session survives leaving the screen.
// Retention: rows older than 30 days are pruned on write.
class HistoryDb private constructor(ctx: Context) : SQLiteOpenHelper(ctx, "netwatch.db", null, 1) {
    override fun onCreate(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE samples(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, time INTEGER NOT NULL, ok INTEGER NOT NULL, total INTEGER NOT NULL, avg_ms REAL NOT NULL, jitter_ms REAL NOT NULL, loss_pct REAL NOT NULL, source TEXT NOT NULL)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE ping_points(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, time INTEGER NOT NULL, ms REAL NOT NULL, ok INTEGER NOT NULL)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE loss_points(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, time INTEGER NOT NULL, loss REAL NOT NULL)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE gaming_sessions(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, started INTEGER NOT NULL, ended INTEGER NOT NULL, profile TEXT NOT NULL, region TEXT NOT NULL, host TEXT NOT NULL, sent INTEGER NOT NULL, received INTEGER NOT NULL, loss REAL NOT NULL, avg_ms REAL NOT NULL, min_ms REAL NOT NULL, max_ms REAL NOT NULL, jitter_ms REAL NOT NULL, p95 REAL NOT NULL, spikes INTEGER NOT NULL, burst INTEGER NOT NULL, score INTEGER, bb_grade TEXT NOT NULL)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE gaming_samples(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, session_id INTEGER NOT NULL, time INTEGER NOT NULL, ms REAL NOT NULL, ok INTEGER NOT NULL)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX idx_samples_time ON samples(time)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX idx_ping_time ON ping_points(time)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX idx_loss_time ON loss_points(time)")
        db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX idx_gsamples_session ON gaming_samples(session_id)")
    }
    override fun onUpgrade(db: SQLiteDatabase, oldVersion: Int, newVersion: Int) {}

    companion object {
        const val RETENTION_MS: Long = 30L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
        @Volatile private var inst: HistoryDb? = null
        fun get(ctx: Context): HistoryDb = inst ?: synchronized(this) {
            inst ?: HistoryDb(ctx.applicationContext).also { inst = it }
        }
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/HistoryEngine.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context

// History Engine — the single API for storing, reading and analysing
// history. The implementation is History (SQLite via HistoryDb, 30-day
// retention, transactional writes, one-time migration of old data); this
// object is the stable boundary the UI, the widget and the services call, so
// no caller assembles a check record from five separate History calls any
// more. All timestamps are real (System.currentTimeMillis at measurement
// time); ranges are 24h / 7d / 30d and are applied to the query, not to the
// title.
object HistoryEngine {

    // One call records a whole check: the sample row (ok/total/ping/jitter/
    // loss) and the "last check" summary the dashboard and widget read.
    fun recordCheck(
        ctx: Context, ok: Int, total: Int,
        pingAvg: Double, jitterMs: Double, lossPct: Double,
        intlPct: Int, domPct: Int,
        scoreTotal: Int?, scoreStatus: String, source: String = "full",
        saveSummary: Boolean = true
    ) {
        History.addSample(ctx, ok, total, pingAvg, jitterMs, lossPct, source)
        // The background service only updates the "last check" summary when
        // the ping actually completed; a failed cycle still records its sample.
        if (saveSummary) History.saveLastCheck(ctx, pingAvg, jitterMs, lossPct, intlPct, domPct, scoreTotal, scoreStatus)
    }

    // One call records one ping measurement: success goes to the ping series,
    // failure is recorded as a failed point (ms < 0) so charts draw a gap,
    // and a completed burst also records its loss.
    fun recordPing(ctx: Context, ping: PingStats) {
        if (ping.ok) History.addPing(ctx, ping.avgMs) else History.addPingFailed(ctx)
        if (ping.completed) History.addLoss(ctx, ping.lossPct.toDouble())
    }

    // One live-ping measurement: success stores the value, failure stores
    // a failed point so the chart draws a gap instead of a zero.
    fun recordPingMs(ctx: Context, ms: Double?) {
        if (ms != null) History.addPing(ctx, ms) else History.addPingFailed(ctx)
    }

    fun pingHistory(ctx: Context): List<Double> = History.pingHistory(ctx)
    fun lossHistory(ctx: Context): List<Double> = History.lossHistory(ctx)
    fun samples(ctx: Context): List<Triple<Long, Int, Int>> = History.samples(ctx)
    fun addGamingSample(ctx: Context, sessionId: Long, ms: Double, ok: Boolean) =
        History.addGamingSample(ctx, sessionId, ms, ok)
    fun addSpike(ctx: Context, ms: Double) = History.addSpike(ctx, ms)

    fun pingPoints(ctx: Context, sinceMs: Long = 0L): List<Pair<Long, Double>> =
        History.pingPoints(ctx, sinceMs)

    fun samplesInRange(ctx: Context, sinceMs: Long): List<History.Sample> =
        History.samplesInRange(ctx, sinceMs)

    fun lastCheck(ctx: Context): History.LastCheck? = History.lastCheck(ctx)
    fun lastPing(ctx: Context): Pair<Long, Double>? = History.lastPing(ctx)

    fun startGamingSession(ctx: Context, profile: String, region: String, host: String): Long =
        History.startGamingSession(ctx, profile, region, host)

    fun finishGamingSession(
        ctx: Context, sessionId: Long, sent: Int, received: Int, loss: Double,
        avgMs: Double, minMs: Double, maxMs: Double, jitterMs: Double, p95: Double,
        spikes: Int, burst: Int, score: Int?, bbGrade: String
    ) = History.finishGamingSession(ctx, sessionId, sent, received, loss, avgMs, minMs, maxMs, jitterMs, p95, spikes, burst, score, bbGrade)

    fun gamingSessions(ctx: Context): List<History.GamingSession> = History.gamingSessions(ctx)
    fun gamingSessionSamples(ctx: Context, sessionId: Long): List<Pair<Long, Double>> =
        History.gamingSessionSamples(ctx, sessionId)

    fun buildBackupJson(ctx: Context): String = History.buildBackupJson(ctx)
    fun restoreFromJson(ctx: Context, json: String): String = History.restoreFromJson(ctx, json)

    fun setHeartbeat(ctx: Context) = History.setHeartbeat(ctx)
    fun lastHeartbeat(ctx: Context): Long = History.lastHeartbeat(ctx)
    fun setLastAttempt(ctx: Context) = History.setLastAttempt(ctx)
    fun setLastSuccess(ctx: Context) = History.setLastSuccess(ctx)
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/Lab.kt
