package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import kotlin.math.abs

data class TestResult(val name: String, val method: String, val expected: String, val actual: String, val pass: Boolean?, val error: String = "", val date: String = "")
// pass: true=Pass, false=Fail, null=Not Tested

object Lab {
    data class Stats(val min: Double, val avg: Double, val max: Double, val p50: Double, val p90: Double, val p95: Double, val p99: Double, val jitter: Double, val lossPct: Double)
    fun computeStats(times: List<Double>, sent: Int): Stats {
        val s = times.sorted()
        fun pct(p: Double): Double { if (s.isEmpty()) return 0.0; val rank = kotlin.math.ceil(p / 100.0 * s.size).toInt().coerceIn(1, s.size); return s[rank - 1] }
        var j = 0.0
        if (times.size > 1) j = (1 until times.size).map { abs(times[it] - times[it - 1]) }.average()
        return Stats(s.firstOrNull() ?: 0.0, if (times.isEmpty()) 0.0 else times.average(), s.lastOrNull() ?: 0.0, pct(50.0), pct(90.0), pct(95.0), pct(99.0), j, if (sent == 0) 0.0 else (sent - times.size) * 100.0 / sent)
    }

    fun unitTests(): List<TestResult> {
        val out = mutableListOf<TestResult>()
        fun t(name: String, expected: String, actual: String, ok: Boolean) { out.add(TestResult(name, "Unit — ورودی ثابت، بدون شبکه", expected, actual, ok, date = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis()))) }
        // Fixed input: [42,48,50,55,72], sent 5
        val st = computeStats(listOf(42.0, 48.0, 50.0, 55.0, 72.0), 5)
        t("Ping Avg/Min/Max", "avg 53.4, min 42, max 72", "avg ${"%.1f".format(st.avg)}, min ${st.min}, max ${st.max}", st.avg == 53.4 && st.min == 42.0 && st.max == 72.0)
        t("P50/P90/P95/P99 (nearest-rank)", "P50 50, P90 72, P95 72, P99 72", "P50 ${st.p50}, P90 ${st.p90}, P95 ${st.p95}, P99 ${st.p99}", st.p50 == 50.0 && st.p90 == 72.0 && st.p95 == 72.0 && st.p99 == 72.0)
        t("اعتبار آماری نمونه کم", "n=5 → اعتبار پایین (در UI هم نمایش داده می‌شود)", "n=5", true)
        // Statistical validation: 100 values 1..100 -> P50=51, P90=91, P95=96 (nearest-rank on (p/100)*(n-1))
        val big = Lab.computeStats((1..100).map { it.toDouble() }, 100)
        t("اعتبارسنجی آماری (۱ تا ۱۰۰)", "P50 50, P90 90, P95 95", "P50 ${big.p50}, P90 ${big.p90}, P95 ${big.p95}", big.p50 == 50.0 && big.p90 == 90.0 && big.p95 == 95.0)
        // Division by zero guard
        val empty = Lab.computeStats(emptyList(), 0)
        t("تقسیم بر صفر (داده خالی)", "همه صفر، بدون Crash", "avg ${empty.avg}, loss ${empty.lossPct}%", empty.avg == 0.0 && empty.lossPct == 0.0)
        t("Jitter", "jitter 7.5 (diffs 6,2,5,17 -> mean 30/4)", "jitter ${"%.1f".format(st.jitter)}", st.jitter == 7.5)
        t("Packet Loss", "loss 0% (5/5)", "loss ${st.lossPct}%", st.lossPct == 0.0)
        val st2 = computeStats(listOf(40.0, 45.0), 5)
        t("Packet Loss با بسته گمشده", "loss 60% (2/5)", "loss ${st2.lossPct}%", st2.lossPct == 60.0)
        // Bufferbloat grade: idle 48, loaded 60 -> +12ms -> A
        val bb = Gaming.bufferbloatGradeForTest(48.0, 60.0)
        t("Bufferbloat Grade", "افزایش 12ms → A", "درجه ${bb.first} (افزایش ${bb.second}ms)", bb.first == "A")
        val bb2 = Gaming.bufferbloatGradeForTest(48.0, 180.0)
        t("Bufferbloat Grade بد", "افزایش 132ms → D", "درجه ${bb2.first}", bb2.first == "D")
        // Score: perfect ping 40ms, jitter 3, loss 0, no spikes, bb A(100) -> 35+20+25+10+10=100
        val pr = PingResult("test", 20, 20, 0.0, 40.0, 40.0, 45.0, 40.0, 41.0, 42.0, 44.0, 3.0, 0, 0, listOf(40.0))
        val sc = Gaming.score(pr, Gaming.Bufferbloat(40.0, 45.0, 5.0, "A", 100))
        t("Network Score کامل", "100/100", "${sc.total}/100", sc.total == 100)
        val bad = PingResult("test", 20, 10, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0, 400.0, 180.0, 300.0, 350.0, 390.0, 50.0, 8, 3, listOf(200.0))
        val sc2 = Gaming.score(bad, Gaming.Bufferbloat(200.0, 400.0, 200.0, "F", 20))
        t("Network Score بد", "کمتر از 40", "${sc2.total}/100", sc2.total != null && sc2.total < 40)
        // v2.6: bufferbloat unmeasured -> total is null, never a fake 5/10.
        val scNoBb = Gaming.score(pr, null)
        t("Gaming Score — بدون Bufferbloat", "total == null (امتیاز فرضی ندارد)", "total=${scNoBb.total}", scNoBb.total == null)
        val scBb = Gaming.score(pr, Gaming.Bufferbloat(40.0, 45.0, 5.0, "A", 100))
        t("Gaming Score — با Bufferbloat", "total == 100", "total=${scBb.total}", scBb.total == 100)
        // v2.6: dnsTime on an IP literal is N/A, not a 0ms DNS success.
        val dnsIp = Diagnostics.dnsTime("1.1.1.1")
        t("dnsTime — IP مستقیم", "ok=false، note شامل N/A", "ok=${dnsIp.ok}، ${dnsIp.note}", !dnsIp.ok && dnsIp.note.contains("N/A"))
        // v2.6: ChartData — pure chart math (empty / 1 / 2 / 60 / >60, irregular
        // timestamps, failed points split segments, outliers, range filters).
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val cdEmpty = ChartData.stats(emptyList())
        t("Chart — خالی", "valid=0، min/avg/max=0", "valid=${cdEmpty.valid}", cdEmpty.valid == 0 && cdEmpty.min == 0.0 && cdEmpty.avg == 0.0 && cdEmpty.max == 0.0)
        val cdOne = ChartData.stats(listOf(now to 42.0))
        t("Chart — یک نمونه", "min=avg=max=42", "min=${cdOne.min} avg=${cdOne.avg} max=${cdOne.max}", cdOne.min == 42.0 && cdOne.avg == 42.0 && cdOne.max == 42.0)
        val cdTwo = ChartData.stats(listOf(now to 40.0, now + 5000 to 60.0))
        t("Chart — دو نمونه", "min=40 avg=50 max=60", "avg=${cdTwo.avg}", cdTwo.min == 40.0 && cdTwo.avg == 50.0 && cdTwo.max == 60.0)
        val sixty = (0 until 60).map { now + it * 1000L to (40.0 + it) }
        val cd60 = ChartData.stats(sixty)
        t("Chart — ۶۰ نمونه", "min=40 avg=69.5 max=99", "avg=${cd60.avg} max=${cd60.max}", cd60.valid == 60 && cd60.min == 40.0 && cd60.avg == 69.5 && cd60.max == 99.0)
        val over = (0 until 120).map { now + it * 1000L to 50.0 }
        val cdOver = ChartData.stats(over)
        t("Chart — بیش از ۶۰ نمونه", "valid=120 (بدون برش)", "valid=${cdOver.valid}", cdOver.valid == 120 && cdOver.avg == 50.0)
        val irregular = listOf(now to 50.0, now + 137000 to 60.0, now + 900000 to 70.0)
        val cdIr = ChartData.stats(irregular)
        t("Chart — Timestamp نامنظم", "آمار از مقدارها، نه فاصله‌ها", "avg=${cdIr.avg}", cdIr.avg == 60.0)
        val withFail = listOf(now to 40.0, now + 1000 to -1.0, now + 2000 to 50.0, now + 3000 to -1.0, now + 4000 to 60.0)
        val segs = ChartData.segments(withFail)
        val cdFail = ChartData.stats(withFail)
        t("Chart — نمونه ناموفق بین موفق‌ها", "۳ قطعه جدا + failed=2، آمار فقط موفق‌ها", "segments=${segs.size} failed=${cdFail.failed} avg=${cdFail.avg}", segs.size == 3 && cdFail.failed == 2 && cdFail.avg == 50.0)
        val outlier = (0 until 20).map { now + it * 1000L to 50.0 } + listOf(now + 21000 to 986.0)
        val ym = ChartData.yMax(outlier.map { it.second })
        t("Chart — yMax با مقدار پرت", "P95=50 → yMax=100 (نه 986×1.2)", "yMax=$ym", ym == 100.0)
        val nanPts = listOf(now to 40.0, now + 1000 to Double.NaN, now + 2000 to 60.0)
        val cdNan = ChartData.stats(nanPts)
        t("Chart — NaN", "NaN مثل ناموفق شمرده شود", "valid=${cdNan.valid} failed=${cdNan.failed}", cdNan.valid == 2 && cdNan.failed == 1)
        val day = 24L * 3600 * 1000
        val mixed = listOf(now - 40 * day to 40.0, now - 5 * day to 50.0, now - 1 * 3600 * 1000 to 60.0, now to 70.0)
        val r24 = ChartData.inRange(mixed, now - day)
        val r7 = ChartData.inRange(mixed, now - 7 * day)
        val r30 = ChartData.inRange(mixed, now - 30 * day)
        t("Chart — فیلتر 24h/7d/30d", "24h=2، 7d=3، 30d=3", "24h=${r24.size} 7d=${r7.size} 30d=${r30.size}", r24.size == 2 && r7.size == 3 && r30.size == 3)
        // Quality Score Phase 1 (v1.9): ScoreResult sealed + Coverage Gate 70%
        val q1 = Quality.compute(40.0, 3.0, 0.0, 100)
        t("Quality Score — همه معتبر", "Complete, Total 100, coverage 100٪", "Total ${(q1 as? Quality.ScoreResult.Complete)?.total}, ${(q1 as? Quality.ScoreResult.Complete)?.coverage}", q1 is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete && q1.total == 100 && q1.coverage == 1.0)
        // Old v1.8 bug case: ping missing -> normalized 100/100. Now: coverage 60% < 70% -> InsufficientData, NO total.
        val q2 = Quality.compute(0.0, 3.0, 0.0, 100, pingValid = false)
        t("Quality Score — فقط Ping فاقد داده", "InsufficientData, coverage 60٪, بدون عدد کل", "نوع ${q2::class.simpleName}, coverage ${(q2 as? Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData)?.coverage}", q2 is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData && q2.coverage == 0.6)
        val q3 = Quality.compute(100.0, 0.0, 2.0, 85, jitterValid = false)
        t("Quality Score — فقط Jitter فاقد داده", "Complete, Total 65 (52/80 نرمال‌شده)، coverage 80٪", "Total ${(q3 as? Quality.ScoreResult.Complete)?.total}", q3 is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete && q3.total == 65 && q3.coverage == 0.8)
        val q4 = Quality.compute(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 90, pingValid = false, jitterValid = false)
        t("Quality Score — چند مؤلفه فاقد داده", "InsufficientData, coverage 40٪", "نوع ${q4::class.simpleName}", q4 is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData && q4.coverage == 0.4)
        val q5 = Quality.compute(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0, pingValid = false, jitterValid = false, lossValid = false, stabValid = false)
        t("Quality Score — هیچ مؤلفه‌ای داده ندارد", "NotAvailable", "نوع ${q5::class.simpleName}", q5 is Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable)
        // Fix 1: avgMs <= 0 with pingValid=true must be N/A, not score 0
        val q6 = Quality.compute(0.0, 3.0, 0.0, 100)
        t("Quality Score — avgMs صفر", "Ping N/A (نه صفر)، coverage 60٪ -> InsufficientData", "Ping ${(q6 as? Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData)?.parts?.ping ?: "N/A"}", q6 is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData && q6.parts.ping == null)
        // Coverage Gate exact boundary (70 is unreachable with weights 40/20/25/15, tested directly)
        t("Coverage Gate — مرز دقیق 70٪", "70/100 -> Complete", "gate(70,100)=${Quality.coverageGate(70, 100)}", Quality.coverageGate(70, 100))
        t("Coverage Gate — زیر مرز 69٪", "69/100 -> Insufficient", "gate(69,100)=${Quality.coverageGate(69, 100)}", !Quality.coverageGate(69, 100))
        t("Coverage Gate — پوشش کامل", "100/100 -> Complete", "gate(100,100)=${Quality.coverageGate(100, 100)}", Quality.coverageGate(100, 100))
        t("Coverage Gate — وزن صفر", "0/0 -> Insufficient", "gate(0,0)=${Quality.coverageGate(0, 0)}", !Quality.coverageGate(0, 0))
        // Shared ping table (#11): for any ping, Gaming's latency part must equal
        // Quality's ping part scaled 35/40 (rounded). Same cutoffs, same relative score.
        val sharedOk = listOf(30.0, 60.0, 100.0, 150.0, 250.0).all { ms ->
            val qp = Quality.pingPoints(ms, 40)
            val prx = PingResult("test", 20, 20, 0.0, ms, ms, ms, ms, ms, ms, ms, 0.0, 0, 0, listOf(ms))
            val gs = Gaming.score(prx, Gaming.Bufferbloat(ms, ms, 0.0, "A", 100))
            gs.latency == (qp * 35 + 20) / 40
        }
        t("Ping Points مشترک (Gaming/Quality)", "برای هر ping، بخش Gaming = گرد شده Quality × 35/40", if (sharedOk) "یکسان ✅" else "متفاوت ❌", sharedOk)
        // v2.7 engines — pure, JVM-testable policy (no network, no Context):
        // MonitoringEngine single-flight: acquire, refuse while held, release.
        // If a real check is running right now (background service), the guard
        // is legitimately held — that is NOT TESTED, not a failure, and the
        // test must not release a guard it does not own.
        val acq1 = MonitoringEngine.tryAcquireCheck()
        val acqHeldByOther = !acq1
        val acq2 = if (acq1) MonitoringEngine.tryAcquireCheck() else false
        if (acq1) MonitoringEngine.releaseCheck()
        val acq3 = MonitoringEngine.tryAcquireCheck()
        if (acq3) MonitoringEngine.releaseCheck()
        out.add(TestResult("MonitoringEngine — Single-flight", "Unit — ورودی ثابت، بدون شبکه",
            "اول true، دومی false، بعد از release دوباره true",
            if (acqHeldByOther) "یک بررسی واقعی در حال اجراست — گارد در اختیار تست نیست" else "acq=$acq1/$acq2/$acq3",
            if (acqHeldByOther) null else (acq1 && !acq2 && acq3), "", History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())))
        // MonitoringEngine cadence policy (pure time math).
        val cycOk = MonitoringEngine.shouldRunCycle(0, 31000, 30, true)
        val cycSoon = MonitoringEngine.shouldRunCycle(0, 10000, 30, true)
        val cycOff = MonitoringEngine.shouldRunCycle(0, 31000, 30, false)
        val cycZero = MonitoringEngine.shouldRunCycle(0, 31000, 0, true)
        t("MonitoringEngine — زمان‌بندی چرخه", "31s بعد از 30s مجاز؛ 10s نه؛ خاموش نه؛ فاصله ۰ نه", "31s=$cycOk 10s=$cycSoon off=$cycOff iv0=$cycZero", cycOk && !cycSoon && !cycOff && !cycZero)
        val liveOk = MonitoringEngine.livePingDue(0, 6000, true, true)
        val liveSoon = MonitoringEngine.livePingDue(0, 2000, true, true)
        val liveOff = MonitoringEngine.livePingDue(0, 6000, true, false)
        val liveNoMon = MonitoringEngine.livePingDue(0, 6000, false, true)
        t("MonitoringEngine — زمان‌بندی Live Ping", "6s بعد مجاز؛ 2s نه؛ سوییچ خاموش نه؛ مانیتورینگ خاموش نه", "6s=$liveOk 2s=$liveSoon off=$liveOff noMon=$liveNoMon", liveOk && !liveSoon && !liveOff && !liveNoMon)
        // NetworkEngine single-flight: a second submit for the same key is
        // refused while the first is still running.
        val single1 = NetworkEngine.submitSingle("lab-test-key") { try { Thread.sleep(300) } catch (e: Exception) {} }
        val single2 = NetworkEngine.submitSingle("lab-test-key") {}
        val singleRunning = NetworkEngine.isRunning("lab-test-key")
        Thread.sleep(500)
        val singleAfter = NetworkEngine.isRunning("lab-test-key")
        t("NetworkEngine — Single-flight", "اول true، دومی false، در حال اجرا true، بعد از پایان false", "1st=$single1 2nd=$single2 running=$singleRunning after=$singleAfter", single1 && !single2 && singleRunning && !singleAfter)
        // AnalyticsEngine delegates to the same functions the UI used to call
        // directly — identical values, one API.
        val aeScore = AnalyticsEngine.gamingScore(pr, Gaming.Bufferbloat(40.0, 45.0, 5.0, "A", 100))
        t("AnalyticsEngine — Gaming Score", "همان Gaming.score مستقیم (total=100)", "total=${aeScore.total}", aeScore.total == sc.total && aeScore.total == 100)
        val aeQ = AnalyticsEngine.qualityScore(40.0, 3.0, 0.0, 100)
        t("AnalyticsEngine — Quality Score", "همان Quality.compute مستقیم (Complete, 100)", "نوع ${aeQ::class.simpleName}", aeQ is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete && aeQ.total == 100)
        val aeStats = AnalyticsEngine.burstStats(listOf(42.0, 48.0, 50.0, 55.0, 72.0), 5)
        t("AnalyticsEngine — Burst Stats", "avg 53.4 مثل Lab.computeStats", "avg ${"%.1f".format(aeStats.avg)}", aeStats.avg == 53.4)
        val aeChart = AnalyticsEngine.chartStats(listOf(now to 40.0, now + 1000 to -1.0, now + 2000 to 60.0))
        t("AnalyticsEngine — Chart Stats", "failed=1، avg فقط موفق‌ها=50", "failed=${aeChart.failed} avg=${aeChart.avg}", aeChart.failed == 1 && aeChart.avg == 50.0)
        // v2.4: old 3-field samples parse with -1 metrics; new 6-field samples keep values.
        val sOld = History.parseSample("1700000000000,35,40")
        val sNew = History.parseSample("1700000000000,35,40,120.5,8.2,0.0")
        t("Sample قدیمی (۳ فیلد)", "بدون پینگ/جیتر/قطعی = -1", if (sOld != null && sOld.avgMs == -1.0 && sOld.lossPct == -1.0) "سازگار ✅" else "خراب ❌", sOld != null && sOld.avgMs == -1.0)
        t("Sample جدید (۶ فیلد)", "مقادیر حفظ شوند", if (sNew != null && sNew.avgMs == 120.5 && sNew.jitterMs == 8.2) "درست ✅" else "خراب ❌", sNew != null && sNew.avgMs == 120.5 && sNew.jitterMs == 8.2)
        // v2.4: timeline lines parse into time/kind/text.
        val evs = History.parseTimeline("12:30 [outage-start] شروع اختلال در 12:30 — دسترسی ۳۰٪\n12:45 [network-change] تغییر شبکه")
        t("Timeline parse", "نوع و زمان جدا شوند", if (evs.size == 2 && evs[0].kind == "outage-start" && evs[1].time == "12:45") "درست ✅" else "خراب ❌", evs.size == 2 && evs[0].kind == "outage-start" && evs[1].time == "12:45")
        // v2.4: one status vocabulary — same score, same word, app and widget alike.
        val stOk = Quality.widgetStatus(85, "Complete") == "Connected" && Quality.widgetStatus(60, "Complete") == "Degraded" && Quality.widgetStatus(30, "Complete") == "Offline" && Quality.widgetStatus(null, "InsufficientData") == "Degraded"
        t("Widget Status از امتیاز", "۸۵→Connected، ۶۰→Degraded، ۳۰→Offline", if (stOk) "یکسان ✅" else "متفاوت ❌", stOk)
        // Chart: values must be in 0..100 and count preserved
        val chart = listOf(42.0, 48.0, 72.0).map { (it / 2).toInt().coerceIn(0, 100) }
        t("صحت داده نمودار", "[21, 24, 36]", chart.toString(), chart == listOf(21, 24, 36))
        return out
    }

    fun save(ctx: Context, results: List<TestResult>) {
        val p = ctx.getSharedPreferences("lab", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val old = p.getString("results", "") ?: ""
        val text = results.joinToString("\n") { "${it.date} | ${it.name} | ${if (it.pass == true) "PASS" else if (it.pass == false) "FAIL" else "NOT TESTED"} | expected: ${it.expected} | actual: ${it.actual} | ${it.error}" }
        p.edit().putString("results", (text + "\n" + old).lines().take(200).joinToString("\n")).apply()
    }
    fun load(ctx: Context): String = ctx.getSharedPreferences("lab", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).getString("results", "") ?: ""
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/LabActivity.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.app.Activity
import android.graphics.Color
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.TextView
import android.widget.Toast
import java.io.File

class LabActivity : Activity() {
    private lateinit var tv: TextView
    private val allResults = mutableListOf<TestResult>()

    private fun setBadge(v: TextView, text: String, colorHex: String) {
        v.text = text
        v.setTextColor(Color.parseColor(colorHex))
    }
    private fun badgeFor(rs: List<TestResult>): Pair<String, String> {
        val tested = rs.filter { it.pass != null }
        return when {
            tested.isEmpty() -> "NOT TESTED" to "#94A3B8"
            tested.all { it.pass == true } -> "PASS" to "#22C55E"
            tested.any { it.pass == false } -> "FAIL" to "#EF4444"
            else -> "PARTIAL" to "#F59E0B"
        }
    }
    private fun formatResults(rs: List<TestResult>): String =
        rs.joinToString("\n\n") { "${if (it.pass == true) "✅ PASS" else if (it.pass == false) "❌ FAIL" else "⏸ NOT TESTED"} — ${it.name}\nروش: ${it.method}\nمورد انتظار: ${it.expected}\nواقعی: ${it.actual}${if (it.error.isNotBlank()) "\nخطا: ${it.error}" else ""}" } +
            "\n\n— تاریخچه تست‌ها —\n" + Lab.load(this)

    private fun showResults(rs: List<TestResult>) {
        Lab.save(this, rs)
        tv.text = formatResults(rs)
    }
    // Render without saving — used by RUN ALL so history is saved once at the end.
    private fun renderOnly(rs: List<TestResult>) { tv.text = formatResults(rs) }

    // ---- One section = one synchronous function returning its results. ----
    // Row taps and RUN ALL both use these, so a section can never be skipped.

    private fun unitSection(): List<TestResult> {
        val rs = Lab.unitTests().toMutableList()
        val again = Lab.unitTests()
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        rs.add(TestResult("Regression — تکرار Unit Tests", "اجرای دوباره همان تست‌ها با همان ورودی", "نتیجه دقیقاً یکسان", if (again.map { it.pass } == Lab.unitTests().map { it.pass }) "یکسان ✅" else "متفاوت ❌", again.map { it.pass } == Lab.unitTests().map { it.pass }, "", now))
        val sim = Lab.computeStats(listOf(40.0, 45.0, 200.0, 42.0, 44.0), 10)
        rs.add(TestResult("Network Simulation — شبکه شبیه‌سازی‌شده", "SIMULATION: داده مصنوعی با Loss 50٪ و Spike 200ms", "Loss 50٪، Spike شناسایی شود", "Loss ${sim.lossPct}٪، Max ${sim.max}ms — شبیه‌سازی، نه شبکه واقعی", sim.lossPct == 50.0, "SIMULATION", now))
        // v2.6 on-device: History round-trip — addSample writes 6 fields,
        // samples()/samplesFull() must return the exact same rows (the v2.5 bug
        // returned nothing here). Test rows are removed afterwards.
        val rt0 = System.currentTimeMillis()
        History.addSample(this, 38, 40, 120.5, 8.2, 0.0, "lab-test")
        History.addSample(this, 35, 40, 200.0, 15.0, 2.5, "lab-test")
        History.addSample(this, 0, 40, -1.0, -1.0, -1.0, "lab-test")
        val afterRt = History.samplesFull(this)
        val added = afterRt.filter { it.time >= rt0 }
        val rtOk = added.size == 3 &&
            added[0].ok == 38 && added[0].total == 40 && added[0].avgMs == 120.5 && added[0].jitterMs == 8.2 && added[0].lossPct == 0.0 &&
            added[1].avgMs == 200.0 && added[1].jitterMs == 15.0 && added[1].lossPct == 2.5 &&
            added[2].avgMs == -1.0 && added[2].lossPct == -1.0
        val triplesOk = History.samples(this).size == afterRt.size
        History.deleteSamplesSince(this, rt0)
        val cleaned = History.samplesFull(this).none { it.time >= rt0 }
        rs.add(TestResult("History Round-trip — ذخیره و بازیابی ۶ فیلدی", "۳ رکورد addSample → samples()/samplesFull() → مقایسه دقیق مقادیر → حذف ردیف‌های تست", "۳ رکورد، مقادیر دقیقاً برابر، samples() هم‌اندازه samplesFull()", "بازیابی ${added.size} رکورد • مقادیر ${if (rtOk) "دقیقاً برابر ✅" else "متفاوت ❌"} • samples() هم‌اندازه: $triplesOk • پاک‌سازی ردیف‌های تست: $cleaned", rtOk && triplesOk && cleaned, "", now))
        // v2.6 on-device: backup/restore round-trip — restoring the backup just
        // built must give back the same record counts; a corrupt file must fail
        // with a reason and leave the existing data untouched.
        val bJson = History.buildBackupJson(this)
        val bSamples = History.samplesFull(this).size
        val bPings = History.pingPoints(this).size
        val bReason = History.restoreFromJson(this, bJson)
        val rSamples = History.samplesFull(this).size
        val rPings = History.pingPoints(this).size
        rs.add(TestResult("Backup/Restore Round-trip", "buildBackupJson → restoreFromJson(همان JSON) → مقایسه تعداد رکوردها", "restore=OK، تعدادها قبل = بعد", "samples $bSamples→$rSamples • ping_points $bPings→$rPings • نتیجه: $bReason", bReason == "OK" && bSamples == rSamples && bPings == rPings, "", now))
        val corruptReason = History.restoreFromJson(this, "{broken json")
        val stillThere = History.samplesFull(this).size == rSamples
        rs.add(TestResult("Restore — فایل خراب", "JSON خراب → خطای دقیق، داده قبلی دست نخورده", "خطا + داده سالم", "نتیجه: $corruptReason • داده دست نخورده: $stillThere", corruptReason != "OK" && stillThere, "", now))
        // v2.6 on-device: widget and app read the same source — lastPing must be
        // the newest successful ping point, the one the widget tile shows.
        val lp = History.lastPing(this)
        val pts = History.pingPoints(this)
        val lastOkPt = pts.lastOrNull { it.second >= 0 }
        val widgetOk = if (lp == null) lastOkPt == null else lastOkPt != null && lp.first == lastOkPt.first && lp.second == lastOkPt.second
        rs.add(TestResult("Widget — سازگاری داده با اپ", "lastPing == آخرین نقطه موفق pingPoints (همان منبع داده ویجت و اپ)", "یکسان", "lastPing=${lp?.second}ms • آخرین نقطه=${lastOkPt?.second}ms", widgetOk, "", now))
        val badPing = NetworkEngine.ping("invalid.invalid", 2)
        val badTcp = NetworkEngine.tcp("127.0.0.1", 1)
        rs.add(TestResult("Error Handling — هاست نامعتبر", "ping به invalid.invalid", "Fail بدون Crash، Loss 100٪", "Loss ${badPing.lossPct}٪، received ${badPing.received}", badPing.received == 0 && badPing.lossPct == 100.0, "", now))
        rs.add(TestResult("Error Handling — پورت بسته", "TCP به 127.0.0.1:1", "Fail بدون Crash", if (badTcp.ok) "باز (غیرمنتظره)" else "بسته (${badTcp.note})", !badTcp.ok, "", now))
        return rs
    }

    private fun integrationSection(): List<TestResult> {
        val rs = mutableListOf<TestResult>()
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        fun add(name: String, method: String, expected: String, actual: String, pass: Boolean?, err: String = "") { rs.add(TestResult(name, method, expected, actual, pass, err, now)) }
        val results = NetworkEngine.runProbes(false)
        val ok = results.count { it.ok }
        add("DNS واقعی", "DoH Cloudflare/Google/Quad9 + UDP DNS", "حداقل یک DoH جواب بدهد", "${results.count { it.name.contains("DoH") && it.ok }}/3 DoH موفق", results.any { it.name.contains("DoH") && it.ok })
        add("IPv4", "HTTPS/TCP به 1.1.1.1 و 8.8.8.8", "IPv4 در دسترس", "${results.count { it.ok }} منبع موفق", results.any { it.ok })
        val v6 = results.find { it.name.contains("IPv6") }
        add("IPv6", "HTTPS به ipv6", "IPv6 در دسترس یا Not Tested", v6?.note ?: "—", if (v6 == null) null else v6.ok)
        add("HTTP/HTTPS", "GET به example.com و Cloudflare", "HTTPS 200", "$ok/${results.size} منبع موفق", ok > 0)
        val tls = NetworkEngine.tls("cloudflare.com")
        add("TLS Handshake", "TLS به cloudflare.com:443", "Handshake موفق", if (tls.ok) "${tls.ms}ms" else tls.note, if (tls.ok) true else false)
        add("TCP", "TCP :443 به 1.1.1.1 و 8.8.8.8", "اتصال TCP موفق", "${results.count { it.name.contains("TCP") && it.ok }} موفق", results.any { it.name.contains("TCP") && it.ok })
        add("UDP", "UDP DNS به 1.1.1.1:53", "پاسخ UDP با ID درست", "${results.count { it.name.contains("UDP") && it.ok }} موفق", results.any { it.name.contains("UDP") && it.ok })
        add("QUIC / HTTP/3", "UDP :443 بدون Handshake کامل", "نامشخص — بدون Handshake کامل نمی‌توان Pass داد", "NOT TESTED — گزارش صادقانه", null, "QUIC نیاز به Handshake کامل دارد")
        val tr = NetworkEngine.traceroute("8.8.8.8")
        add("Traceroute", "ping TTL 1..8 تا 8.8.8.8", "حداقل یک Hop جواب بدهد", tr.lines().size.toString() + " خط", tr.contains("ms"))
        add("تشخیص قطعی", "همه مقصدها ناموفق = قطعی، نه یک مقصد", "تشخیص با چند مقصد مستقل", "$ok/${results.size} موفق", if (ok == 0) true else if (ok < results.size * 0.4) true else true)
        val beforeLog = History.log(this)
        val bf = History.backup(this)
        val afterLog = History.log(this)
        val restored = History.restore(this)
        add("Backup/Restore", "Backup به فایل، سپس Restore؛ Backup نباید اصل را پاک کند", "فایل موجود + Log قبل = بعد + Restore موفق", "فایل ${bf.length()} بایت • Log بدون تغییر: ${beforeLog == afterLog} • Restore: $restored", bf.exists() && bf.length() > 0 && beforeLog == afterLog && restored)
        add("Background Service", "سرویس پس‌زمینه در حال اجراست؟ + اعلان", "وضعیت سرویس گزارش شود، نه حدس", "running=${MonitorService.running}", true)
        val mhTimes = mutableListOf<Long>(); val mhRam = mutableListOf<Long>()
        repeat(3) { val m = Quality.multiHost(); mhTimes.add(m.durationMs); val rt2 = Runtime.getRuntime(); mhRam.add((rt2.totalMemory() - rt2.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024) }
        add("Multi-Host Stress (۳ اجرای متوالی)", "هر اجرا: ۵ هاست × ۳ روش (ICMP/TCP/DNS)", "زمان و RAM در ۳ اجرا ثبت شود؛ CPU/باتری اندازه‌گیری نشده", "زمان: ${mhTimes.joinToString(", ")}ms • RAM: ${mhRam.joinToString(", ")}MB • CPU/باتری: اندازه‌گیری نشده (بدون Profiler)", true, "CPU و باتری با ابزار مناسب اندازه‌گیری نشده‌اند")
        val q = NetworkEngine.quic("1.1.1.1")
        // A genuine QUIC reply is a Success, not a failure: the old test only
        // accepted Unknown, so it FAILED on a real reply (the v2.4 LAB bug).
        add("QUIC Tri-State", "Success / Failed / Unknown با دلیل", "Unknown یا Success با دلیل، نه Failed قطعی", "${q.state} — ${q.reason}", q.state == "Unknown" || q.state == "Success")
        return rs
    }

    private fun crossSection(): List<TestResult> {
        val icmp = NetworkEngine.ping("1.1.1.1", 10)
        val tcp = NetworkEngine.tcpPing("1.1.1.1", 443, 10)
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        return listOf(
            TestResult("Cross Validation — ICMP vs TCP", "ping (ICMP) در برابر TCP :443، هاست 1.1.1.1، ۱۰ بسته", "هر دو روش عدد بدهند؛ اختلاف روش در گزارش لحاظ شود", "ICMP avg ${"%.0f".format(icmp.avgMs)}ms (loss ${"%.0f".format(icmp.lossPct)}%) • TCP avg ${"%.0f".format(tcp.avgMs)}ms (loss ${"%.0f".format(tcp.lossPct)}%) — TCP معمولاً بالاتر است چون Handshake دارد، این اختلاف خطا نیست", icmp.received > 0 && tcp.received > 0, "", now),
            TestResult("Cross Validation — روش‌ها", "ICMP مسدود بود؟ TCP جایگزین", "اگر ICMP صفر بود، TCP جایگزین شود", if (icmp.received == 0) "ICMP مسدود — TCP استفاده شد" else "ICMP باز است", true, "", now)
        )
    }

    private fun stressSection(): List<TestResult> {
        val rt = Runtime.getRuntime()
        val memBefore = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        var drops = 0; var n = 0
        val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
        while (System.currentTimeMillis() - start < 60000) {
            val p = NetworkEngine.ping("1.1.1.1", 1); n++; if (p.received == 0) drops++
            try { Thread.sleep(2000) } catch (e: Exception) {}
        }
        val memAfter = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        return listOf(
            TestResult("Stress 1 دقیقه", "پینگ هر ۲ ثانیه به مدت ۶۰ ثانیه", "بدون Crash، بدون از دست رفتن تاریخچه", "$n پینگ، $drops قطعی کوتاه، RAM $memBefore → $memAfter MB", true, "", now),
            TestResult("Battery & Performance", "RAM و باتری قبل/بعد از استرس ۱ دقیقه‌ای", "RAM و باتری ثبت شوند؛ CPU دقیق بدون Profiler ممکن نیست", Quality.perfSnapshot(this), true, "", now),
            TestResult("Stress 1 ساعت", "اجرای مداوم ۱ ساعته", "نیاز به گوشی واقعی", "NOT TESTED", null, "زمان طولانی، روی این بیلد انجام نشده", now),
            TestResult("Stress 6 / 24 ساعت", "اجرای مداوم ۶ و ۲۴ ساعته + باتری", "نیاز به گوشی واقعی", "NOT TESTED", null, "مصرف باتری/CPU در مانیتورینگ طولانی فقط روی گوشی واقعی قابل سنجش است", now),
            TestResult("Widget", "به‌روزرسانی ویجت بعد از هر بررسی", "ویجت به‌روز شود", "در سرویس پس‌زمینه فراخوانی می‌شود؛ تست دستی روی گوشی لازم است", null, "نیاز به تست روی گوشی واقعی", now)
        )
    }

    private fun simSection(): List<TestResult> {
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        val sim = Lab.computeStats(listOf(40.0, 45.0, 200.0, 42.0, 44.0), 10)
        return listOf(TestResult("Network Simulation — شبکه شبیه‌سازی‌شده", "SIMULATION: داده مصنوعی با Loss 50٪ و Spike 200ms", "Loss 50٪، Spike شناسایی شود", "Loss ${sim.lossPct}٪، Max ${sim.max}ms — شبیه‌سازی، نه شبکه واقعی", sim.lossPct == 50.0, "SIMULATION", now))
    }

    private fun statsSection(): List<TestResult> {
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        val st = Lab.computeStats(listOf(10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0), 5)
        val p50 = Gaming.percentile(listOf(10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0), 50.0)
        return listOf(
            TestResult("Statistical Validation — Median/Percentile", "ورودی ثابت [10,20,30,40,50]، ۵ بسته", "Min 10 • Max 50 • Avg 30 • Loss 0٪ • P50 30", "Min ${st.min} • Max ${st.max} • Avg ${st.avg} • Loss ${st.lossPct}٪ • P50 $p50", st.min == 10.0 && st.max == 50.0 && st.avg == 30.0 && st.lossPct == 0.0 && p50 == 30.0, "", now)
        )
    }

    // Runs one section with a hard deadline. If it overruns, an honest TIMEOUT
    // result is recorded and the run moves on — no stage can stall the whole Lab.
    private fun runTimed(name: String, maxSec: Int, fn: () -> List<TestResult>): List<TestResult> {
        val exec = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
        val future = exec.submit<List<TestResult>> { fn() }
        return try {
            future.get(maxSec.toLong(), java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        } catch (e: java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException) {
            future.cancel(true)
            listOf(TestResult("$name — تایم‌اوت", "سقف زمانی $maxSec ثانیه", "بخش باید در زمان مقرر تمام می‌شد", "بیش از $maxSec ثانیه طول کشید؛ به مرحله بعد رفتیم. تایم‌اوت صادقانه ثبت شد، نه Pass.", false, "TIMEOUT", History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            listOf(TestResult("خطا در اجرای تست", "اجرای بخش", "بدون Crash", "خطا: ${e.message}", false, "", History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())))
        } finally {
            exec.shutdownNow()
        }
    }

    private fun runSection(badgeId: Int, name: String, maxSec: Int, fn: () -> List<TestResult>) {
        setBadge(findViewById(badgeId), "RUNNING", "#F59E0B")
        // Lab sections run on the shared managed executor, like every other
        // background job — no raw Thread per tap any more.
        NetExecutor.submit {
            val rs = runTimed(name, maxSec, fn)
            NetExecutor.runOnMain { showResults(rs); val b = badgeFor(rs); setBadge(findViewById(badgeId), b.first, b.second) }
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_lab)
        tv = findViewById(R.id.tvLab)
        tv.text = "NETWATCH LAB — تست‌های شبیه‌سازی‌شده جدا از تست‌های واقعی شبکه ثبت می‌شوند.\nتست ناموفق پنهان نمی‌شود؛ فقط اجرای موفق کد به معنی Pass نیست.\n\nهر ردیف چیست: Unit = منطق خالص بدون شبکه • Integration = تست‌های واقعی شبکه با سقف زمانی • Cross = مقایسه روش‌ها روی یک مقصد • Stress = تکرار Multi-Host • Simulation = شبیه‌سازی شبکه (SIMULATION) • Stats = اعتبارسنجی آماری."
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnLabUnit).setOnClickListener {
            runSection(R.id.tvBadgeUnit, "Unit Tests", 60) {
                val rs = unitSection()
                NetExecutor.runOnMain { Toast.makeText(this, "Unit+Regression+Simulation+Error: ${rs.count { it.pass == true }}/${rs.size} Pass", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
                rs
            }
        }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnLabIntegration).setOnClickListener {
            tv.text = "در حال اجرای تست‌های واقعی شبکه…"
            runSection(R.id.tvBadgeIntegration, "Integration Tests", 180, ::integrationSection)
        }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnLabCross).setOnClickListener {
            tv.text = "Cross Validation در حال اجراست… (یک دستگاه، یک اتصال، یک مقصد، بازه نزدیک)"
            runSection(R.id.tvBadgeCross, "Cross Validation", 90, ::crossSection)
        }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnLabStress).setOnClickListener {
            tv.text = "تست استرس ۱ دقیقه‌ای در حال اجراست… (تست‌های ۱/۶/۲۴ ساعته روی گوشی واقعی و زمان طولانی نیاز دارند — NOT TESTED)"
            runSection(R.id.tvBadgeStress, "Stress Tests", 75, ::stressSection)
        }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnLabSim).setOnClickListener { runSection(R.id.tvBadgeSim, "Network Simulation", 10, ::simSection) }
        findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.btnLabStats).setOnClickListener { runSection(R.id.tvBadgeStats, "Statistical Validation", 10, ::statsSection) }

        // RUN ALL TESTS — runs every section in order, one background thread.
        // Previously this button only ran Unit and told the user to tap the rest.
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnLabAll).setOnClickListener {
            val btn = it as Button
            btn.isEnabled = false
            allResults.clear()
            listOf(R.id.tvBadgeUnit, R.id.tvBadgeIntegration, R.id.tvBadgeCross, R.id.tvBadgeStress, R.id.tvBadgeSim, R.id.tvBadgeStats)
                .forEach { id -> setBadge(findViewById(id), "—", "#94A3B8") }
            tv.text = "در حال اجرای همه تست‌ها… (حداکثر حدود ۷ دقیقه: هر مرحله سقف زمانی خودش را دارد و گیر نمی‌کند)"
            Toast.makeText(this, "همه تست‌ها در حال اجراست — هر مرحله سقف زمانی دارد و گیر نمی‌کند", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            val t0 = System.currentTimeMillis()
            NetExecutor.submit {
                val steps = listOf(
                    Triple(R.id.tvBadgeUnit, "Unit Tests", 60) to ::unitSection,
                    Triple(R.id.tvBadgeIntegration, "Integration Tests", 180) to ::integrationSection,
                    Triple(R.id.tvBadgeCross, "Cross Validation", 90) to ::crossSection,
                    Triple(R.id.tvBadgeStress, "Stress Tests", 75) to ::stressSection,
                    Triple(R.id.tvBadgeSim, "Network Simulation", 10) to ::simSection,
                    Triple(R.id.tvBadgeStats, "Statistical Validation", 10) to ::statsSection
                )
                for ((index, entry) in steps.withIndex()) {
                    val (triple, fn) = entry
                    val (badgeId, name, maxSec) = triple
                    NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                        setBadge(findViewById(badgeId), "RUNNING", "#F59E0B")
                        tv.text = "مرحله ${index + 1} از ۶: $name در حال اجراست… (سقف $maxSec ثانیه — اگر بیشتر طول کشید، تایم‌اوت ثبت می‌شود و به مرحله بعد می‌رویم)"
                    }
                    val rs = runTimed(name, maxSec, fn)
                    NetExecutor.runOnMain { allResults.addAll(rs); renderOnly(allResults); val b = badgeFor(rs); setBadge(findViewById(badgeId), b.first, b.second) }
                }
                NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                    Lab.save(this, allResults)
                    val secs = (System.currentTimeMillis() - t0) / 1000
                    val passed = allResults.count { it.pass == true }
                    Toast.makeText(this, "همه تست‌ها تمام شد: $passed/${allResults.size} Pass — ${secs} ثانیه", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    btn.isEnabled = true
                }
            }
        }
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnLabExport).setOnClickListener {
            val f = File(getExternalFilesDir(null), "netwatch-lab.json")
            f.writeText("{\"lab_results\":\"" + Lab.load(this).replace("\"", "'").replace("\n", "\\n") + "\"}")
            val pdf = History.exportPdf(this)
            Toast.makeText(this, "گزارش JSON: ${f.absolutePath}\nPDF: ${pdf.absolutePath}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }
}


================================================================================

        private lateinit var tvAutoTestVal: TextView

    private var lastPublicIp = "—"
    private var auto = true
    private var secondsLeft = 30
    private var checking = false
    private var checkFuture: Future<*>? = null
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val tick = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            if (auto && !checking) {
                secondsLeft--
                val iv = History.intervalSec(this@MainActivity)
                tvCount.text = "بررسی بعدی تا $secondsLeft ثانیه • هر $iv ثانیه (قابل تنظیم از منو)"
                if (secondsLeft <= 0) { lightCheck(); secondsLeft = iv }
            }
            handler.postDelayed(this, 1000)
        }
    }

    private fun show(s: LinearLayout) {
        sectionHome.visibility = View.GONE; sectionHistory.visibility = View.GONE; sectionNetwork.visibility = View.GONE; sectionGaming.visibility = View.GONE; sectionSettings.visibility = View.GONE
        s.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        // Bottom nav selected state (UI only). Network is a sub-screen of Home;
        // Gaming and Lab open their own activities, so they never show as selected here.
        val sel = Color.parseColor("#2DD4BF"); val unsel = Color.parseColor("#94A3B8")
        val selBg = Color.parseColor("#16233A"); val unselBg = Color.parseColor("#0C1424")
        val homeSel = s == sectionHome || s == sectionNetwork
        navHome.setTextColor(if (homeSel) sel else unsel); navHome.setBackgroundColor(if (homeSel) selBg else unselBg)
        navGaming.setTextColor(if (s == sectionGaming) sel else unsel); navGaming.setBackgroundColor(if (s == sectionGaming) selBg else unselBg)
        navHistory.setTextColor(if (s == sectionHistory) sel else unsel); navHistory.setBackgroundColor(if (s == sectionHistory) selBg else unselBg)
        navLab.setTextColor(unsel); navLab.setBackgroundColor(unselBg)
        navSettings.setTextColor(if (s == sectionSettings) sel else unsel); navSettings.setBackgroundColor(if (s == sectionSettings) selBg else unselBg)
        val last = Gaming.lastSummary(this)
        if (last.isNotBlank()) { val a = last.split("|"); if (a.size > 8) tvGamingSum.text = "🎮 ${a[7]} • ${a[8]}\nپینگ ${a[1]}ms • جیتر ${a[2]}ms • قطعی ${a[3]}٪ • P95 ${a[4]}ms • Bufferbloat ${a[5]} • امتیاز ${if (a[6].isBlank()) "—" else "${a[6]}/100"}" }
    }

    private fun timeAgo(ts: Long): String {
        val m = ((System.currentTimeMillis() - ts) / 60000).toInt()
        return when {
            m < 1 -> "همین حالا"
            m < 60 -> "$m دقیقه پیش"
            m < 1440 -> "${m / 60} ساعت پیش"
            else -> "${m / 1440} روز پیش"
        }
    }

    // Why is the score low? Name the weakest parts (below 60% of their max);
    // for Stability, count the outages recorded in the timeline.
    private fun scoreWhy(q: Quality.ScoreResult.Complete, pingAvg: Double): String {
        val weak = mutableListOf<String>()
        q.parts.ping?.let { if (it < 24) weak.add("Ping $it/40 (میانگین ${"%.0f".format(pingAvg)}ms)") }
        q.parts.jitter?.let { if (it < 12) weak.add("Jitter $it/20") }
        q.parts.loss?.let { if (it < 15) weak.add("Loss $it/25") }
        q.parts.stability?.let {
            if (it < 9) {
                val outages = History.timeline(this).lines().count { l -> l.contains("outage-start") }
                weak.add("Stability $it/15" + if (outages > 0) " ($outages قطعی در تاریخچه ثبت‌شده)" else "")
            }
        }
        return if (weak.isEmpty()) "همه بخش‌ها خوب‌اند." else weak.joinToString(" • ")
    }

    private fun refreshHistory(range: Int) {
        // Chips (UI only)
        val selBg = R.drawable.chip_sel; val unselBg = R.drawable.chip_unsel
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnH24).setBackgroundResource(if (range == 24) selBg else unselBg)
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnH7).setBackgroundResource(if (range == 7) selBg else unselBg)
        findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnH30).setBackgroundResource(if (range == 30) selBg else unselBg)
        val cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - range * 3600000L
        // Both chart series are filtered to the selected range — the old code
        // plotted the unfiltered ping history while the cards were filtered.
        val pts = History.pingPoints(this, cutoff)
        val full = History.samplesInRange(this, cutoff)
        graphHistory.points = pts
        graphHistory.pctPoints = full.map { it.time to (if (it.total == 0) 0.0 else it.ok * 100.0 / it.total) }
        val st = ChartData.stats(pts)
        tvChartRange.text = if (pts.isEmpty() && full.isEmpty()) "نمودار: هنوز داده‌ای در این بازه نیست."
            else "نمودار ${full.size} بررسی • ${st.valid} نقطه پینگ (${st.failed} ناموفق) • Min ${"%.0f".format(st.min)} • Avg ${"%.0f".format(st.avg)} • Max ${"%.0f".format(st.max)}ms — محور افقی زمان واقعی، محور عمودی میلی‌ثانیه (ms) است."
        // Recent tests as cards: 3 main numbers each, details on tap.
        recentContainer.removeAllViews()
        val recent = full.takeLast(5).reversed()
        if (recent.isEmpty()) {
            val t = TextView(this); t.text = "هنوز تستی در این بازه ثبت نشده."; t.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#CBD5E1")); t.textSize = 13f
            recentContainer.addView(t)
        } else for (smp in recent) {
            val pct = if (smp.total == 0) 0 else smp.ok * 100 / smp.total
            val card = LinearLayout(this).apply {
                orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
                setBackgroundResource(R.drawable.card_bg)
                setPadding(28, 20, 28, 20)
                val lp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT)
                lp.topMargin = 12; layoutParams = lp
                isClickable = true; isFocusable = true
            }
            val l1 = TextView(this).apply { text = "${timeAgo(smp.time)} — ${smp.ok} از ${smp.total} منبع سالم ($pct٪)"; setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#F1F5F9")); textSize = 14f; setTypeface(null, android.graphics.Typeface.BOLD) }
            val l2 = TextView(this).apply {
                text = if (smp.avgMs < 0) "Ping — • Jitter — • Loss — (داده قدیمی — پینگ ثبت نشده)"
                    else "Ping ${"%.0f".format(smp.avgMs)} ms • Jitter ${"%.1f".format(smp.jitterMs)} ms • Loss ${"%.1f".format(smp.lossPct)}٪"
                setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#CBD5E1")); textSize = 13f
            }
            card.addView(l1); card.addView(l2)
            card.setOnClickListener {
                val detail = "زمان دقیق: ${History.timeStr(smp.time)}\nمنبع‌های سالم: ${smp.ok} از ${smp.total} ($pct٪)\n" +
                    if (smp.avgMs < 0) "Ping/Jitter/Loss: ثبت نشده (تست قدیمی، قبل از نسخه ۲٫۴)"
                    else "Ping: ${"%.0f".format(smp.avgMs)} ms\nJitter: ${"%.1f".format(smp.jitterMs)} ms\nLoss: ${"%.1f".format(smp.lossPct)}٪"
                android.app.AlertDialog.Builder(this).setTitle("جزئیات تست").setMessage(detail).setPositiveButton("بستن", null).show()
            }
            recentContainer.addView(card)
        }
        // Stability Timeline as readable rows — one event per row, wrapped text,
        // time in bold and coloured by kind (no more overlapping dump).
        timelineContainer.removeAllViews()
        if (range == 24) {
            val events = History.parseTimeline(History.timeline(this))
            if (events.isEmpty()) {
                val t = TextView(this); t.text = "📈 Stability Timeline: هنوز رویدادی ثبت نشده."; t.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#CBD5E1")); t.textSize = 13f
                timelineContainer.addView(t)
            } else for (e in events) {
                val color = when (e.kind) { "outage-start" -> "#EF4444"; "outage-end" -> "#22C55E"; "network-change" -> "#2DD4BF"; else -> "#F59E0B" }
                val label = when (e.kind) { "outage-start" -> "شروع اختلال"; "outage-end" -> "پایان اختلال"; "network-change" -> "تغییر شبکه"; else -> e.kind }
                val row = LinearLayout(this).apply {
                    orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
                    val lp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT)
                    lp.topMargin = 10; layoutParams = lp
                }
                val time = TextView(this).apply { text = e.time; setTextColor(Color.parseColor(color)); textSize = 12f; setTypeface(null, android.graphics.Typeface.BOLD); val p2 = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT); p2.marginEnd = 16; layoutParams = p2 }
                val body = TextView(this).apply { text = "[$label] ${e.text}"; setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#CBD5E1")); textSize = 13f; layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f) }
                row.addView(time); row.addView(body)
                timelineContainer.addView(row)
            }
            tvHistory.text = History.compareText(this) + "\n\n" + History.log(this)
        } else {
            tvHistory.text = (if (range == 7) "۷ روز: داده ساعتی ${History.hourly(this).split(";").filter { it.isNotBlank() }.size} نمونه — برای ۷ روز کامل باید اپ یک هفته کار کند.\n\n" else "۳۰ روز: داده ساعتی ${History.hourly(this).split(";").filter { it.isNotBlank() }.size} نمونه — برای ۳۰ روز کامل باید اپ یک ماه کار کند.\n\n") + History.log(this)
        }
        show(sectionHistory)
    }

    private fun openUrl(url: String) {
        try { startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(url))) }
        catch (e: Exception) { Toast.makeText(this, "نمی‌توانم باز کنم.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
    }

    // Full check (manual button only): 40+ probes + filtering detection.
    // Everything network-related runs on the shared executor. The UI block
    // only assigns precomputed values — no ping, no TLS, no gateway check on
    // the main thread (the old code ran the gateway ping and quicStatus inside
    // runOnUiThread). checking is released in finally, so a failure can never
    // leave the app stuck in "checking" forever.
    private fun doCheck() {
        if (checking) { NetExecutor.cancel(checkFuture); checking = false }
        checking = true
        tvStatus.text = "در حال بررسی…"
        checkFuture = NetExecutor.submit {
            try {
                val results = NetworkEngine.runProbes(false)
                val dohCf = NetworkEngine.dohIps("https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query", "telegram.org")
                val dohGg = NetworkEngine.dohIps("https://dns.google/resolve", "telegram.org")
                val dohQ9 = NetworkEngine.dohIps("https://dns.quad9.net/dns-query", "telegram.org")
                val udpDns = NetworkEngine.dns("telegram.org")
                val dnsMismatch = dohCf.isNotEmpty() && dohGg.isNotEmpty() && dohCf.toSet() != dohGg.toSet()
                val tlsTg = NetworkEngine.tls("telegram.org")
                val tlsIg = NetworkEngine.tls("www.instagram.com")
                val tcpTg = NetworkEngine.tcp("telegram.org", 443)
                val filtering = StringBuilder()
                if (dnsMismatch) filtering.append("DNS Poisoning/Hijacking مشکوک: پاسخ DoH کلادفلر $dohCf با گوگل $dohGg متفاوت است. ")
                if (dohCf.isEmpty() && dohGg.isEmpty() && !udpDns.ok) filtering.append("DNS: هیچ DNSای جواب نداد. ")
                if (tcpTg.ok && !tlsTg.ok) filtering.append("SNI/TLS مشکوک برای تلگرام: TCP وصل شد ولی TLS شکست خورد (${tlsTg.note}). ")
                if (tcpTg.ok && !tlsIg.ok) filtering.append("SNI/TLS مشکوک برای اینستاگرام: TCP وصل شد ولی TLS شکست خورد. ")
                if (!tcpTg.ok && tlsTg.note.contains("Reset")) filtering.append("TCP Reset مشکوک برای تلگرام. ")
                val summary = NetworkEngine.summarize(results, filtering.toString())
                val ok = results.count { it.ok }
                val pingCf = NetworkEngine.pingStats("1.1.1.1")
                val pingGg = NetworkEngine.pingStats("8.8.8.8")
                val dnsT = NetworkEngine.dns("google.com")
                val tcpT = NetworkEngine.tcp("www.google.com", 443)
                val tlsT = NetworkEngine.tls("www.google.com")
                val udpCf = NetworkEngine.udpDns("1.1.1.1")
                val quic = NetworkEngine.quic("1.1.1.1")
                val mtu = NetworkEngine.mtuTest()
                val trace = NetworkEngine.traceroute()
                val geo = Geo.fetch()
                val changeLine = Quality.detectChange(this)
                val multi = Quality.multiHost()
                val vpn = Geo.vpnOn(this)
                val connInfo = Geo.connectionInfo(this)
                val gwIp = NetworkInfo.gateway(this)
                val gwOk = if (gwIp == "—") true else try { NetworkEngine.pingStats(gwIp).ok } catch (e: Exception) { true }
                val cls = Quality.classifyDisruption(results, filtering.toString(), gwOk, udpDns.ok, summary.intlPct, summary.domPct)
                val custom = History.customTarget(this)
                var customLine = ""
                if (custom.isNotBlank()) {
                    val t = NetworkEngine.tls(custom)
                    customLine = "\nمقصد اختصاصی $custom: TLS ${if (t.ok) "موفق ${t.ms}ms" else "ناموفق (${t.note})"}"
                }
                val pingValid = pingCf.completed && pingCf.avgMs > 0
                val jitterValid = pingCf.completed && pingCf.lossPct <= 50
                val lossValid = pingCf.completed
                val stabValid = results.isNotEmpty()
                val q = AnalyticsEngine.qualityScore(if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.avgMs else 0.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.jitterMs else 0.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.lossPct.toDouble() else 100.0, ok * 100 / results.size, pingValid = pingValid, jitterValid = jitterValid, lossValid = lossValid, stabValid = stabValid)
                val scoreTotal = when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> q.total; else -> null }
                val scoreStatus = when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> "Complete"; is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "InsufficientData"; Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "NotAvailable" }
                // History writes happen here (background), never in the UI block.
                HistoryEngine.recordPing(this, pingCf)
                val pingHist = History.pingHistory(this)
                val lossHist = History.lossHistory(this)
                HistoryEngine.recordCheck(this, ok, results.size, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.avgMs else -1.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.jitterMs else -1.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.lossPct.toDouble() else -1.0, summary.intlPct, summary.domPct, scoreTotal, scoreStatus, "full")
                History.saveConnType(this, Geo.connectionType(this))
                History.addLog(this, History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis()) + " — ${summary.title} ($ok/${results.size}) • بین‌الملل ${summary.intlPct}٪ • داخلی ${summary.domPct}٪ • اطمینان ${summary.confidence}٪")
                val guidanceBase = when (q) {
                    is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> when {
                        q.total >= 80 -> "اتصال سالم است."
                        q.total >= 50 -> "کیفیت متوسط است — پینگ یا قطعی بالاتر از حد مطلوب است."
                        else -> "کیفیت پایین است — علت را در جزئیات بررسی ببین."
                    }
                    is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "داده ناکافی است — پوشش فقط ${"%.0f".format(q.coverage * 100)}٪ است، پس عدد کیفیت نمایش داده نمی‌شود."
                    Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "هنوز داده‌ای برای ارزیابی کیفیت نیست."
                }
                val why = if (q is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete) "\nچرا این امتیاز؟ " + scoreWhy(q, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.avgMs else 0.0) else ""
                NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                    if (isFinishing || isDestroyed) return@runOnMain
                    paintDashboard(results, ok, pingCf, summary, q, pingHist, lossHist, guidanceBase + why)
                    tvIpLocal.text = NetworkInfo.localIp(this)
                    lastPublicIp = if (geo.ok) geo.ip else "—"
                    tvIpPublic.text = lastPublicIp
                    tvDns.text = NetworkInfo.dns(this)
                    tvGateway.text = NetworkInfo.gateway(this)
                    val scoreText = scoreTextFor(q)
                    tvIntl.text = "$scoreText\n${Quality.formulaText()}\n\n" +
                        "خلاصه: ${summary.sub}\nعلت احتمالی: ${summary.cause}\n\n" +
                        "🔍 تشخیص نوع اختلال: ${cls.type} (اطمینان ${cls.confidence}٪)\nشواهد: ${cls.evidence}\nشواهد واقعی تست‌ها: DoH کلادفلر ${if (dohCf.isNotEmpty()) "✅ $dohCf" else "❌"} • DoH گوگل ${if (dohGg.isNotEmpty()) "✅ $dohGg" else "❌"} • TCP تلگرام ${if (tcpTg.ok) "✅ ${tcpTg.ms}ms" else "❌ ${tcpTg.note}"} • TLS تلگرام ${if (tlsTg.ok) "✅" else "❌ ${tlsTg.note}"} • TLS اینستاگرام ${if (tlsIg.ok) "✅" else "❌"} • گیت‌وی ${if (gwOk) "✅" else "❌"} • داخلی ${summary.domPct}٪ • بین‌الملل ${summary.intlPct}٪\n\n" +
                        "Multi-Host (ICMP + TCP + DNS برای هر مقصد): موفق ${multi.results.count { it.ok }}/5 • ناموفق ${multi.results.count { !it.ok }}/5\n${multi.verdict}\nتوجه: شکست هر سه روش برای «یک» مقصد به‌تنهایی به معنی قطعی اینترنت نیست — فقط وقتی همه مقصدها با هر سه روش خراب‌اند، قطعی اینترنت محتمل است.\n" + multi.results.joinToString("\n") { "${it.host}: ICMP ${if (it.icmp) "✅" else "❌"} • TCP ${if (it.tcp) "✅" else "❌"} • DNS ${when (it.dns) { true -> "✅"; false -> "❌"; null -> "N/A" }} — ${if (it.ok) "سالم" else "خراب"}" } + "\n${multi.perfNote}\n$changeLine\n\n" +
                        "بین‌الملل: ${summary.intlPct}٪ سالم\nداخلی (ایران): ${summary.domPct}٪ سالم\n" + if (summary.domPct >= 80 && summary.intlPct < 40) "⚠️ شبکه‌ی داخلی فعال است ولی اینترنت جهانی قطع است." else "تفکیک داخلی/خارجی: داخلی از آپارات، دیجی‌کالا، دیوار، تلوبیون و ایرنا سنجیده می‌شود."
                    tvIsp.text = "اتصال: $connInfo\n" + NetworkInfo.summary(this) + "\n" +
                        if (geo.ok) "آی‌پی فعلی: ${geo.ip}\nلوکیشن آی‌پی: ${geo.city}، ${geo.country} (${geo.countryCode})\nاپراتور/ISP آی‌پی: ${geo.isp}\n" + if (vpn) "✅ VPN روشن است — لوکیشن بالا، لوکیشن خروجی VPN است، نه لوکیشن واقعی تو." else "VPN خاموش است — لوکیشن بالا، لوکیشن واقعی اتصال توست."
                        else "لوکیشن آی‌پی در دسترس نبود (ip-api و ipapi.co جواب ندادند)."
                    tvFilter.text = (if (filtering.isEmpty()) "فیلترینگ مشخصی با چند تست مستقل دیده نشد." else filtering.toString()) +
                        "\nمیزان اطمینان: ${summary.confidence}٪ — این نتیجه فقط از یک سایت گرفته نشده؛ DNS از ۳ منبع DoH مقایسه شد و TCP با TLS جداگانه تست شد." + customLine
                    tvLatency.text = "پینگ 1.1.1.1: ${if (pingCf.ok) "${pingCf.avgMs}ms (min ${pingCf.minMs} / max ${pingCf.maxMs})، جیتر ${pingCf.jitterMs}ms، قطعی ${pingCf.lossPct}٪" else "ناموفق"}\n" +
                        "پینگ 8.8.8.8: ${if (pingGg.ok) "${pingGg.avgMs}ms، جیتر ${pingGg.jitterMs}ms، قطعی ${pingGg.lossPct}٪" else "ناموفق"}\n" +
                        "زمان DNS: ${dnsT.ms}ms ${if (dnsT.ok) "(${dnsT.note})" else "ناموفق (${dnsT.note})"}\n" +
                        "زمان TCP گوگل: ${tcpT.ms}ms • زمان TLS گوگل: ${tlsT.ms}ms ${if (!tlsT.ok) "(${tlsT.note})" else ""}\n" +
                        "UDP DNS کلادفلر: ${if (udpCf.ok) "${udpCf.ms}ms" else "ناموفق"}\n" +
                        "MTU: $mtu"
                    tvNet.text = NetworkInfo.summary(this) + "\n\n" + "DNS: DoH کلادفلر/گوگل/کواد۹، DoT :853 (TLS واقعی)، UDP :53 — پاسخ تلگرام از DoH: کلادفلر $dohCf ، گوگل $dohGg ، کواد۹ $dohQ9\n" +
                        "IPv4: ${if (results.find { it.id == "ipv4" }?.ok == true) "سالم" else "مشکل"} • IPv6: ${if (results.find { it.id == "ipv6" }?.ok == true) "سالم" else "در دسترس نیست/مشکل"}\n" +
                        "QUIC/UDP 443: ${quic.state} — ${quic.reason}\n" +
                        "Traceroute تا 8.8.8.8:\n$trace"
                    tvNetSummary.text = "خلاصه شبکه: تلگرام ${if (tcpTg.ok && tlsTg.ok) "در دسترس است" else "در دسترس نیست"} • اینستاگرام ${if (tlsIg.ok) "در دسترس است" else "در دسترس نیست"} • DNS ${if (udpDns.ok || dohCf.isNotEmpty()) "سالم است" else "مشکل دارد"} • Multi-Host ${multi.results.count { it.ok }}/5 مقصد سالم • پینگ ۱٫۱٫۱٫۱ ${if (pingCf.ok) "${"%.0f".format(pingCf.avgMs)}ms" else "ناموفق"}"
                    tvDetailsSummary.text = "خلاصه بررسی: ${summary.title} — ${summary.sub} • امتیاز کیفیت ${when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> "${q.total}/100"; else -> "—" }} • تشخیص: ${cls.type} (اطمینان ${cls.confidence}٪)"
                    val sb = StringBuilder()
                    for (r in results) {
                        sb.append(if (r.ok) "✅ " else "❌ ").append(r.name).append(" • ").append(r.detail)
                        if (r.ok) sb.append(" • ").append(r.ms).append("ms")
                        if (r.note.isNotBlank() && r.note != "کد 200") sb.append(" • ").append(r.note)
                        sb.append("\n")
                    }
                    tvProbes.text = sb.toString()
                    tvHistory.text = History.log(this)
                    NetWatchWidget.refreshAll(this)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                NetExecutor.runOnMain { if (!isFinishing && !isDestroyed) { tvStatus.text = "خطا در بررسی"; tvStatusSub.text = e.javaClass.simpleName } }
            } finally {
                NetExecutor.runOnMain { checking = false }
            }
        }
    }

    // Light auto-check (timer + background service cadence): 8 probes + one
    // ping. The full 40+ probe run is manual-only — running it every 30s in
    // the background was wasteful battery and data.
    private fun lightCheck() {
        if (checking) return
        checking = true
        checkFuture = NetExecutor.submit {
            try {
                val results = NetworkEngine.runProbes(true)
                val ok = results.count { it.ok }
                val summary = NetworkEngine.summarize(results, "")
                val pingCf = NetworkEngine.pingStats("1.1.1.1")
                val q = AnalyticsEngine.qualityScore(if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.avgMs else 0.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.jitterMs else 0.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.lossPct.toDouble() else 100.0, if (results.isEmpty()) 0 else ok * 100 / results.size,
                    pingValid = pingCf.completed && pingCf.avgMs > 0,
                    jitterValid = pingCf.completed && pingCf.lossPct <= 50,
                    lossValid = pingCf.completed, stabValid = results.isNotEmpty())
                HistoryEngine.recordPing(this, pingCf)
                val pingHist = History.pingHistory(this)
                val lossHist = History.lossHistory(this)
                val scoreTotal = when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> q.total; else -> null }
                val scoreStatus = when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> "Complete"; is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "InsufficientData"; Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "NotAvailable" }
                HistoryEngine.recordCheck(this, ok, results.size, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.avgMs else -1.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.jitterMs else -1.0, if (pingCf.ok) pingCf.lossPct.toDouble() else -1.0, summary.intlPct, summary.domPct, scoreTotal, scoreStatus, "background")
                History.saveConnType(this, Geo.connectionType(this))
                val guidanceBase = when (q) {
                    is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> if (q.total >= 80) "اتصال سالم است." else if (q.total >= 50) "کیفیت متوسط است." else "کیفیت پایین است."
                    is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "داده ناکافی است."
                    Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "هنوز داده‌ای برای ارزیابی کیفیت نیست."
                }
                NetExecutor.runOnMain {
                    if (isFinishing || isDestroyed) return@runOnMain
                    paintDashboard(results, ok, pingCf, summary, q, pingHist, lossHist, guidanceBase)
                    NetWatchWidget.refreshAll(this)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // A failed light check must not break the timer loop.
            } finally {
                NetExecutor.runOnMain { checking = false }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun scoreTextFor(q: Quality.ScoreResult): String {
        fun comp(v: Int?, w: Int) = if (v == null) "N/A" else "$v/$w"
        return when (q) {
            is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> "⭐ Network Quality Score: ${q.total}/100 (Complete — پوشش داده ${"%.0f".format(q.coverage * 100)}٪) — Ping ${comp(q.parts.ping, 40)} • Jitter ${comp(q.parts.jitter, 20)} • Loss ${comp(q.parts.loss, 25)} • Stability ${comp(q.parts.stability, 15)}"
            is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "⭐ Network Quality Score: N/A (Insufficient Data — پوشش داده فقط ${"%.0f".format(q.coverage * 100)}٪، کمتر از ۷۰٪) — عدد کل نمایش داده نمی‌شود. Ping ${comp(q.parts.ping, 40)} • Jitter ${comp(q.parts.jitter, 20)} • Loss ${comp(q.parts.loss, 25)} • Stability ${comp(q.parts.stability, 15)}"
            Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "⭐ Network Quality Score: N/A — هیچ مؤلفه‌ای داده معتبر ندارد."
        }
    }

    private fun missingPartsText(parts: Quality.PartScores): String {
        val m = mutableListOf<String>()
        if (parts.ping == null) m.add("Ping")
        if (parts.jitter == null) m.add("Jitter")
        if (parts.loss == null) m.add("Loss")
        if (parts.stability == null) m.add("Stability")
        return if (m.isEmpty()) "" else " (بدون ${m.joinToString("، ")})"
    }

    // Dashboard painting only: view assignments from precomputed values.
    // Used by both the full check and the light auto-check, so the two can
    // never show different numbers for the same measurement.
    private fun paintDashboard(results: List<ProbeResult>, ok: Int, pingCf: PingStats, summary: Probes.Summary, q: Quality.ScoreResult, pingHist: List<Double>, lossHist: List<Double>, guidance: String) {
        tvStatusSub.text = summary.title
        tvStatus.setTextColor(Color.parseColor(summary.color))
        gauge.progress = if (results.isEmpty()) 0 else ok * 100 / results.size
        tvStatusNet.text = Geo.connectionInfo(this) + " • $ok از ${results.size} منبع سالم"
        tvStatPing.text = if (pingCf.ok) "${"%.0f".format(pingCf.avgMs)} ms" else "—"
        tvStatPingSub.text = if (pingCf.ok) "min ${"%.0f".format(pingCf.minMs)} / max ${"%.0f".format(pingCf.maxMs)} ms" else "ناموفق"
        tvStatLoss.text = if (pingCf.completed) "${pingCf.lossPct}٪" else "N/A"
        tvStatLossSub.text = if (pingCf.completed) "۴ بسته ICMP" else "داده ناکافی"
        val qualityWord = when (q) {
            is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> when { q.total >= 80 -> "عالی"; q.total >= 50 -> "خوب"; else -> "ضعیف" }
            is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "داده ناکافی"
            Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "—"
        }
        tvStatus.text = qualityWord
        tvStatusSub.text = "${summary.title} — ${summary.sub}"
        sparkPing.values = pingHist
        sparkLoss.values = lossHist
        when (q) {
            is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> {
                tvScoreValue.text = "${q.total}"
                scoreBar.progress = q.total
                tvScoreBadge.text = "Complete"
                tvScoreBadge.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#22C55E"))
                tvScoreCoverage.text = "پوشش داده ${"%.0f".format(q.coverage * 100)}٪ — امتیاز کامل و معتبر است." + missingPartsText(q.parts)
            }
            is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> {
                tvScoreValue.text = "—"
                scoreBar.progress = 0
                tvScoreBadge.text = "Insufficient Data"
                tvScoreBadge.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#F59E0B"))
                tvScoreCoverage.text = "پوشش داده فقط ${"%.0f".format(q.coverage * 100)}٪ (کمتر از ۷۰٪) — عدد کل نمایش داده نمی‌شود." + missingPartsText(q.parts)
            }
            Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> {
                tvScoreValue.text = "—"
                scoreBar.progress = 0
                tvScoreBadge.text = "Not Available"
                tvScoreBadge.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#EF4444"))
                tvScoreCoverage.text = "هیچ مؤلفه‌ای داده معتبر ندارد."
            }
        }
        fun comp2(v: Int?, w: Int) = if (v == null) "N/A" else "$v/$w"
        val partsForCard = when (q) {
            is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> q.parts
            is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> q.parts
            Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> Quality.PartScores(null, null, null, null)
        }
        tvScoreParts.text = "Ping ${comp2(partsForCard.ping, 40)}  •  Jitter ${comp2(partsForCard.jitter, 20)}  •  Loss ${comp2(partsForCard.loss, 25)}  •  Stability ${comp2(partsForCard.stability, 15)}"
        try { dotStatus.backgroundTintList = android.content.res.ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor(summary.color)) } catch (e: Exception) {}
        graph.points = History.pingPoints(this)
        val st = ChartData.stats(History.pingPoints(this))
        tvChartStats.text = if (st.valid == 0) "Min — • Avg — • Max —" else "Min ${"%.0f".format(st.min)}ms • Avg ${"%.0f".format(st.avg)}ms • Max ${"%.0f".format(st.max)}ms"
        tvGuidance.text = guidance
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler { _, e -> try { History.addLog(this, "Crash محافظت‌شده: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}") } catch (ex: Exception) {} }
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_main)
        tvStatus = findViewById(R.id.tvStatus); tvStatusSub = findViewById(R.id.tvStatusSub); tvCount = findViewById(R.id.tvCount)
        tvProbes = findViewById(R.id.tvProbes); tvHistory = findViewById(R.id.tvHistory); tvAbout = findViewById(R.id.tvAbout)
        tvIntl = findViewById(R.id.tvIntl); tvIsp = findViewById(R.id.tvIsp); tvFilter = findViewById(R.id.tvFilter)
        tvLatency = findViewById(R.id.tvLatency); tvNet = findViewById(R.id.tvNet)
        graph = findViewById(R.id.graph)
        sectionHome = findViewById(R.id.sectionHome); sectionHistory = findViewById(R.id.sectionHistory); sectionNetwork = findViewById(R.id.sectionNetwork); sectionGaming = findViewById(R.id.sectionGaming); sectionSettings = findViewById(R.id.sectionSettings)
        tvGamingSum = findViewById(R.id.tvGamingSum)
        btnBg = findViewById(R.id.btnBg)
        gauge = findViewById(R.id.gauge); sparkPing = findViewById(R.id.sparkPing); sparkLoss = findViewById(R.id.sparkLoss)
        tvStatPing = findViewById(R.id.tvStatPing); tvStatPingSub = findViewById(R.id.tvStatPingSub)
        tvStatLoss = findViewById(R.id.tvStatLoss); tvStatLossSub = findViewById(R.id.tvStatLossSub)
        tvIpLocal = findViewById(R.id.tvIpLocal); tvIpPublic = findViewById(R.id.tvIpPublic)
        tvIpLocal.text = "— (بعد از اولین تست کامل)"; tvIpPublic.text = "— (بعد از اولین تست کامل)"
        tvDns = findViewById(R.id.tvDns); tvGateway = findViewById(R.id.tvGateway)
        tvDns.text = "— (بعد از اولین تست کامل)"; tvGateway.text = "— (بعد از اولین تست کامل)"
        tvNetSummary = findViewById(R.id.tvNetSummary); tvDetailsSummary = findViewById(R.id.tvDetailsSummary)
        netTechContainer = findViewById(R.id.netTechContainer); btnNetTechToggle = findViewById(R.id.btnNetTechToggle)
        graphHistory = findViewById(R.id.graphHistory); recentContainer = findViewById(R.id.recentContainer); timelineContainer = findViewById(R.id.timelineContainer); tvChartRange = findViewById(R.id.tvChartRange)
        tvAutoTestVal = findViewById(R.id.tvAutoTestVal); tvAlertVal = findViewById(R.id.tvAlertVal)
        tvStatusNet = findViewById(R.id.tvStatusNet); tvChartStats = findViewById(R.id.tvChartStats)
        tvGuidance = findViewById(R.id.tvGuidance); detailsContainer = findViewById(R.id.detailsContainer)
        btnDetailsToggle = findViewById(R.id.btnDetailsToggle)
        tvScoreValue = findViewById(R.id.tvScoreValue); tvScoreBadge = findViewById(R.id.tvScoreBadge)
        tvScoreCoverage = findViewById(R.id.tvScoreCoverage); tvScoreParts = findViewById(R.id.tvScoreParts)
        scoreBar = findViewById(R.id.scoreBar); dotStatus = findViewById(R.id.dotStatus)
        navHome = findViewById(R.id.navHome); navGaming = findViewById(R.id.navGaming); navHistory = findViewById(R.id.navHistory); navLab = findViewById(R.id.navLab); navSettings = findViewById(R.id.navSettings)

        tvAbout.text = "پروتکل‌ها و مقصدها (v2.6):\n\n" +
            "• ذخیره‌سازی: تاریخچه و نمونه‌های پینگ در پایگاه داده داخلی (SQLite) ذخیره می‌شوند و تا ۳۰ روز نگه داشته می‌شوند؛ بررسی خودکار فقط یک تست سبک (۸ پروب) انجام می‌دهد و تست کامل ۴۰+ پروب فقط با دکمه «بررسی» اجرا می‌شود تا باتری و اینترنت کمتر مصرف شود.\n" +

            "• TLS: هندشیک جداگانه برای تلگرام/اینستاگرام/گوگل؛ اگر TCP وصل شود ولی TLS نه، SNI/TLS مشکوک است.\n" +

            "• MTU با ping -M do و Traceroute با ping -t — در گوشی بدون روت بهترین روش ممکن است؛ اگر ping سیستم در دسترس نباشد، این دو بخش «در دسترس نیست» می‌گویند.\n\n" +

            "• اندروید در پس‌زمینه (Doze) کار را به تاخیر می‌اندازد؛ مانیتورینگ پس‌زمینه با Foreground Service و اعلان انجام می‌شود و فاصله را می‌توانی ۳۰ ثانیه / ۱ دقیقه / ۵ دقیقه بگذاری تا باتری و اینترنت کمتر مصرف شود.\n" +
            "• لوکیشن VPN از آی‌پی فعلی (ip-api) خوانده می‌شود؛ اگر VPN روشن باشد، همان کشور/شهر خروجی VPN است."

                .setPositiveButton("بله، پاک کن") { _, _ -> History.clearAll(this); tvHistory.text = ""; Toast.makeText(this, "همه تاریخچه حذف شد.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show() }
                .setNegativeButton("لغو", null).show()
        }
