        tvIpPublic.text = lastPublicIp
        tvDns.text = NetworkInfo.dns(this)
        tvGateway.text = NetworkInfo.gateway(this)
        val lastSample = History.samples(this).lastOrNull()
        if (lastSample != null && lastSample.third > 0) gauge.progress = lastSample.second * 100 / lastSample.third
        tvHistory.text = History.log(this)
        show(sectionHome)
        handler.post(tick)
        doCheck()
    }

    override fun onActivityResult(requestCode: Int, resultCode: Int, data: Intent?) {
        super.onActivityResult(requestCode, resultCode, data)
        if (resultCode != Activity.RESULT_OK || data?.data == null) return
        if (requestCode == 1001) Toast.makeText(this, if (History.backupToUri(this, data.data!!)) "پشتیبان در محل انتخاب‌شده ذخیره شد." else "ذخیره پشتیبان ناموفق بود.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        if (requestCode == 1002) { val reason = History.restoreFromUriReason(this, data.data!!); Toast.makeText(this, if (reason == "OK") "بازیابی شد." else "بازیابی ناموفق بود — $reason", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show() }
    }
    override fun onDestroy() { handler.removeCallbacks(tick); NetExecutor.cancel(checkFuture); super.onDestroy() }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/MonitorService.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import java.util.concurrent.Future

// Background monitoring (v2.6): foreground service so Android does not kill
// it immediately. NOTE (honest Android limit): on Android 6+ Doze/App Standby
// can still delay background work; foreground service + notification is the
// reliable route, and the widget updates on each check.
//
// v2.6 changes:
// - Background cycles run a LIGHT check (8 probes, ~2-3s) instead of the full
//   40+ probe run every 30s — far less battery and data.
// - The user-set interval is re-read on EVERY cycle, so changing it in
//   Settings applies to the running service immediately.
// - Live Ping shares the one managed executor (no new Thread per 5s tick)
//   and is skipped while a check is running — no duplicate ping measurements.
// - The widget's Live state is based on a heartbeat timestamp (written on
//   every successful refresh), not on a static boolean.
class MonitorService : Service() {
    companion object { @Volatile var running = false }
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    // Scheduling guards live in MonitoringEngine now (single-flight shared
    // with the Activity); this service only owns its Handler loops, which
    // Android requires to live here.
    private var checkFuture: Future<*>? = null
    private var liveFuture: Future<*>? = null

    private val loop = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            val iv = History.intervalSec(this@MonitorService)
            if (!MonitoringEngine.tryAcquireCheck()) { handler.postDelayed(this, iv * 1000L); return }
            HistoryEngine.setLastAttempt(this@MonitorService)
            checkFuture = NetExecutor.submit {
                try {
                    val results = NetworkEngine.runProbes(true)
                    val ok = results.count { it.ok }
                    val pct = if (results.isEmpty()) 0 else ok * 100 / results.size
                    val pingBg = NetworkEngine.pingStats("1.1.1.1")
                    HistoryEngine.recordPing(this@MonitorService, pingBg)
                    val intl = results.filter { !it.domestic }; val dom = results.filter { it.domestic }
                    val intlPct = if (intl.isEmpty()) 0 else intl.count { it.ok } * 100 / intl.size
                    val domPct = if (dom.isEmpty()) 0 else dom.count { it.ok } * 100 / dom.size
                    History.addHourly(this@MonitorService, ok, results.size)
                    // The quality score is computed once, and only when the ping
                    // burst completed; a failed cycle still records its sample
                    // but does not overwrite the "last check" summary.
                    val q = if (pingBg.completed) AnalyticsEngine.qualityScore(pingBg.avgMs, pingBg.jitterMs, pingBg.lossPct.toDouble(), pct,
                        pingValid = pingBg.ok && pingBg.avgMs > 0,
                        jitterValid = pingBg.completed && pingBg.lossPct <= 50,
                        lossValid = pingBg.completed, stabValid = results.isNotEmpty()) else null
                    val scoreTotal = when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> q.total; else -> null }
                    val scoreStatus = when (q) { is Quality.ScoreResult.Complete -> "Complete"; is Quality.ScoreResult.InsufficientData -> "InsufficientData"; Quality.ScoreResult.NotAvailable -> "NotAvailable"; null -> "NotAvailable" }
                    if (pingBg.completed) History.saveConnType(this@MonitorService, Geo.connectionType(this@MonitorService))
                    HistoryEngine.recordCheck(this@MonitorService, ok, results.size,
                        if (pingBg.ok) pingBg.avgMs else -1.0,
                        if (pingBg.ok) pingBg.jitterMs else -1.0,
                        if (pingBg.ok) pingBg.lossPct.toDouble() else -1.0,
                        intlPct, domPct, scoreTotal, scoreStatus, "background", saveSummary = pingBg.completed)
                    History.blackbox(this@MonitorService, "$ok/${results.size} ok ($pct%) — light check")
                    val prev = this@MonitorService.getSharedPreferences("netwatch", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).getInt("last_pct", 100)
                    val th = History.alertThreshold(this@MonitorService)
                    if (pct < th && prev >= th) alert("⚠️ قطعی اینترنت", "دسترسی به $pct٪ مقصدها قطع شد (آستانه هشدار $th٪) — جزئیات در Blackbox ثبت شد.")
                    if (pct >= 70 && prev < th) alert("✅ اتصال برگشت", "اینترنت دوباره سالم شد ($pct٪ مقصدها).")
                    this@MonitorService.getSharedPreferences("netwatch", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("last_pct", pct).apply()
                    HistoryEngine.setLastSuccess(this@MonitorService)
                    NetWatchWidget.refreshAll(this@MonitorService)
                    HistoryEngine.setHeartbeat(this@MonitorService)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Crash protection: a failed check must never kill the service
                    History.addLog(this@MonitorService, "خطا در بررسی پس‌زمینه: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}")
                } finally { MonitoringEngine.releaseCheck() }
            }
            handler.postDelayed(this, iv * 1000L)
        }
    }

    // Live Ping: one ICMP ping every 5s while this service runs, only when the
    // user enabled it in Settings. A 1-second loop is not used: Android throttles
    // widget updates and it would burn battery for no visible benefit.
    // Skipped while a background check is running (no duplicate measurement).
    private val liveLoop = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            // One live ping at a time: the guard is atomic, so a slow ping can
            // never stack a second one on top of it. Skipped while a check runs.
            if (MonitoringEngine.livePingOn(this@MonitorService) && !MonitoringEngine.isCheckRunning() && MonitoringEngine.tryAcquireLive()) {
                liveFuture = NetExecutor.submit {
                    try {
                        val ms = NetworkEngine.pingOnce("1.1.1.1")
                        HistoryEngine.recordPingMs(this@MonitorService, ms)
                        if (ms != null) {
                            NetWatchWidget.refreshAll(this@MonitorService)
                            HistoryEngine.setHeartbeat(this@MonitorService)
                        }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {} finally { MonitoringEngine.releaseLive() }
                }
            }
            handler.postDelayed(this, 5000)
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        running = true
        MonitoringEngine.setServiceRunning(true)
        val iv = History.intervalSec(this)
        handler.post(liveLoop)
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
            val ch = NotificationChannel("netwatch", "NetWatch monitoring", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
            (getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager).createNotificationChannel(ch)
        }
        val notif = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) Notification.Builder(this, "netwatch").setContentTitle("NetWatch در حال بررسی اینترنت است").setContentText("بررسی سبک هر $iv ثانیه — برای توقف، از داخل اپ خاموش کن.").setSmallIcon(R.drawable.netwatch_icon).build()
        else Notification.Builder(this).setContentTitle("NetWatch در حال بررسی اینترنت است").setSmallIcon(R.drawable.netwatch_icon).build()
        startForeground(1, notif)
        handler.post(loop)
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        if (intent?.action == "STOP") { stopSelf(); return START_NOT_STICKY }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun alert(title: String, text: String) {
        if (!getSharedPreferences("netwatch", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).getBoolean("alerts_on", true)) return
        try {
            val nm = getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) nm.createNotificationChannel(NotificationChannel("netwatch-alert", "NetWatch alerts", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT))
            val n = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) Notification.Builder(this, "netwatch-alert").setContentTitle(title).setContentText(text).setSmallIcon(R.drawable.netwatch_icon).build()
            else Notification.Builder(this).setContentTitle(title).setContentText(text).setSmallIcon(R.drawable.netwatch_icon).build()
            nm.notify(2, n)
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }
    override fun onDestroy() {
        handler.removeCallbacks(loop); handler.removeCallbacks(liveLoop)
        NetExecutor.cancel(checkFuture); NetExecutor.cancel(liveFuture)
        MonitoringEngine.releaseCheck(); MonitoringEngine.releaseLive()
        MonitoringEngine.setServiceRunning(false)
        running = false; super.onDestroy()
    }
    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/MonitoringEngine.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

// Monitoring Engine — owns the *policy* of when measurements run. The
// Android mechanics stay where Android requires them (MainActivity's 1s tick
// Handler, MonitorService's Foreground Service loops), but every decision —
// "should a check run now?", "is one already running?", "is the Live state
// fresh?" — is answered here, in one place, with pure testable functions.
// Before v2.7 the same guards were duplicated in MainActivity, MonitorService
// and GamingActivity and could disagree; now there is one source of truth.
//
// Cadence policy (battery/data):
// - Full check (40+ probes): manual only, or an explicit user schedule.
// - Light check (8 probes): timer + background service, at the user interval.
// - Live ping: one real ICMP ping every 5s, only while monitoring runs and
//   the user switched it on. Never two live pings on top of each other.
object MonitoringEngine {

    // Single-flight guards shared by the Activity and the Service. An
    // AtomicBoolean (not a plain static boolean) so a check started from one
    // place is visible to the other.
    private val checkRunning = AtomicBoolean(false)
    private val liveRunning = AtomicBoolean(false)

    // Returns true only if no check is running right now; the caller owns
    // the matching releaseCheck() in a finally block.
    fun tryAcquireCheck(): Boolean = checkRunning.compareAndSet(false, true)
    fun releaseCheck() { checkRunning.set(false) }
    fun isCheckRunning(): Boolean = checkRunning.get()

    fun tryAcquireLive(): Boolean = liveRunning.compareAndSet(false, true)
    fun releaseLive() { liveRunning.set(false) }
    fun isLiveRunning(): Boolean = liveRunning.get()

    // Pure policy: enough time passed since the last cycle AND nothing is
    // running AND monitoring is wanted. JVM-testable, no Context needed.
    fun shouldRunCycle(lastCycleMs: Long, nowMs: Long, intervalSec: Int, wantMonitoring: Boolean): Boolean {
        if (!wantMonitoring) return false
        if (checkRunning.get()) return false
        if (intervalSec <= 0) return false
        return nowMs - lastCycleMs >= intervalSec * 1000L
    }

    // Pure policy: a live ping is due only when monitoring runs, the user
    // switched live ping on, the interval elapsed, and no live ping is in
    // flight (a slow ping must never stack another one on top of it).
    fun livePingDue(lastLiveMs: Long, nowMs: Long, monitoringOn: Boolean, liveOn: Boolean): Boolean {
        if (!monitoringOn || !liveOn) return false
        if (liveRunning.get()) return false
        return nowMs - lastLiveMs >= 5000L
    }

    // The "Live" pill is honest only when the service's heartbeat is fresh:
    // a real measurement landed in the last 15 seconds. A switch left on
    // after the service died never shows Live.
    fun heartbeatFresh(ctx: Context): Boolean =
        History.livePingOn(ctx) && System.currentTimeMillis() - History.lastHeartbeat(ctx) < 15_000

    fun livePingOn(ctx: Context): Boolean = History.livePingOn(ctx)
    fun setLivePing(ctx: Context, on: Boolean) = History.setLivePing(ctx, on)

    // The background service reports its state here (set in onCreate /
    // onDestroy). The engine never imports the Service class — the dependency
    // goes one way only: Service -> Engine. The widget and the UI read the
    // state through here, not through a static flag of their own.
    @Volatile private var serviceRunningFlag = false
    fun setServiceRunning(v: Boolean) { serviceRunningFlag = v }
    fun serviceRunning(): Boolean = serviceRunningFlag
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/NetExecutor.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import java.util.concurrent.Callable
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.Future

// One managed executor for ALL network and heavy work in the app (v2.6).
// - Fixed small pool (4 threads): no unbounded thread creation and no new
//   Thread per 5-second tick anywhere in the app.
// - Every task runs off the main thread; results come back via runOnMain.
// - Callers keep the returned Future to cancel a running check (Stop button,
//   leaving a screen, starting a new check while one runs).
object NetExecutor {
    private val pool: ExecutorService = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4) { r ->
        Thread(r, "netwatch-net").apply { isDaemon = true }
    }
    // Lazy: creating the main-thread Handler touches Looper.getMainLooper(),
    // which must only happen on a real device. Background-only callers
    // (submit/cancel) never pay for it, and JVM unit tests can use the pool.
    private val mainHandler by lazy { Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()) }

    fun submit(task: () -> Unit): Future<*> = pool.submit(task)
    fun <T> submitCallable(task: () -> T): Future<T> = pool.submit(Callable(task))
    fun runOnMain(task: () -> Unit) { mainHandler.post(task) }
    fun cancel(f: Future<*>?) { try { f?.cancel(true) } catch (e: Exception) {} }
}

package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.LinearGradient
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.graphics.Shader
import android.widget.RemoteViews

// NetWatch widget (v2.2) — matches the home-screen mockup: header + status card +
// 4 metric tiles + latency chart + rows card + tip bar. Every value is real data
// from the last full check (History.lastCheck), the ping history, or the last
// gaming test. Nothing is invented: missing data shows "—", never a sample number.
class NetWatchWidget : AppWidgetProvider() {
    override fun onUpdate(ctx: Context, mgr: AppWidgetManager, ids: IntArray) {
        for (id in ids) updateOne(ctx, mgr, id)
    }

    companion object {
        fun refreshAll(ctx: Context) {
            val mgr = AppWidgetManager.getInstance(ctx)
            val ids = mgr.getAppWidgetIds(android.content.ComponentName(ctx, NetWatchWidget::class.java))
            for (id in ids) updateOne(ctx, mgr, id)
        }

        private fun ageText(ts: Long): String {
            if (ts <= 0) return "time unknown"
            val m = (System.currentTimeMillis() - ts) / 60000
            return when { m < 1 -> "just now"; m < 60 -> "$m min ago"; m < 1440 -> "${m / 60} h ago"; else -> "${m / 1440} d ago" }
        }

        fun updateOne(ctx: Context, mgr: AppWidgetManager, id: Int) {
            val views = RemoteViews(ctx.packageName, R.layout.widget_netwatch)
            try {
                val vn = ctx.packageManager.getPackageInfo(ctx.packageName, 0).versionName
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wVersion, "Internet Monitor v$vn")
            } catch (e: Exception) { }
            val lc = HistoryEngine.lastCheck(ctx)
            val samples = HistoryEngine.samples(ctx)
            val last = samples.lastOrNull()
            // One status source of truth: the widget shows the same word as the app
            // headline (Quality.widgetStatus) for the last full check, with the check
            // age next to it. Only when no full check exists yet does it fall back
            // to the last background sample.
            val (status, sub, statusColor) = when {
                lc != null && lc.scoreTotal != null -> {
                    val w = AnalyticsEngine.widgetStatus(lc.scoreTotal, lc.scoreStatus)
                    val c = when (w) { "Connected" -> "#22C55E"; "Degraded" -> "#F59E0B"; else -> "#EF4444" }
                    Triple(w, "Quality ${lc.scoreTotal}/100 • ${ageText(lc.checkedAt)}", c)
                }
                lc != null && lc.scoreStatus == "InsufficientData" -> Triple("Degraded", "Incomplete data • ${ageText(lc.checkedAt)}", "#F59E0B")
                last == null -> Triple("Not checked", "Open the app to run a check", "#94A3B8")
                else -> {
                    val ok = last.second; val total = last.third
                    val okPct = if (total == 0) -1 else ok * 100 / total
                    when {
                        ok == total -> Triple("Connected", "Background check • ${ageText(last.first)}", "#22C55E")
                        okPct >= 70 -> Triple("Degraded", "Background check • ${ageText(last.first)}", "#F59E0B")
                        else -> Triple("Offline", "Background check • ${ageText(last.first)}", "#EF4444")
                    }
                }
            }
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wStatus, status)
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wStatusSub, sub)
            views.setTextColor(R.id.wStatus, Color.parseColor(statusColor))

            val intl = lc?.intlPct ?: -1
            val (intlText, intlColor) = when {
                intl < 0 -> "—" to "#94A3B8"
                intl >= 70 -> "Active" to "#22C55E"
                intl >= 40 -> "Degraded" to "#F59E0B"
                else -> "Down" to "#EF4444"
            }
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wIntl, intlText)
            views.setTextColor(R.id.wIntl, Color.parseColor(intlColor))
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wOperator, Geo.operatorName(ctx))

            // Metric tiles. If the last full check's Cloudflare ping failed, the
            // tiles used to show "—" even though real ping data exists in history.
            // Fall back to the last successful value instead of a bare dash.
            val histAll = HistoryEngine.pingHistory(ctx)
            val lastPing = HistoryEngine.lastPing(ctx)
            val liveOn = MonitoringEngine.livePingOn(ctx)
            val freshLive = liveOn && lastPing != null && System.currentTimeMillis() - lastPing.first < 10_000
            if (freshLive && lastPing != null) {
                // Live Ping is on: show the newest measurement, labelled as live.
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPing, "${"%.0f".format(lastPing.second)} ms")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPingSub, "زنده")
            } else if (lc != null && lc.pingAvg >= 0 && lc.checkedAt >= (lastPing?.first ?: 0)) {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPing, "${"%.0f".format(lc.pingAvg)} ms")
                val prev = if (histAll.size >= 2) histAll[histAll.size - 2] else null
                val change = if (prev != null && prev > 0) ((prev - lc.pingAvg) / prev * 100).toInt() else null
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPingSub, if (change == null) "Avg" else if (change >= 0) "↓ $change%" else "↑ ${-change}%")
            } else if (histAll.isNotEmpty()) {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPing, "${"%.0f".format(histAll.last())} ms")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPingSub, "آخرین موفق")
            } else {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPing, "—")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wPingSub, "Avg")
            }
            // The "Live" pill must mean something: it says Live only while the
            // service's heartbeat is fresh (a real measurement landed in the
            // last 15 seconds). A switch left on after the service died, or a
            // stale ping, never shows Live.
            val pillLive = MonitoringEngine.heartbeatFresh(ctx)
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLive, if (pillLive) "Live" else "Paused")
            views.setTextColor(R.id.wLive, Color.parseColor(if (pillLive) "#22C55E" else "#94A3B8"))
            if (lc != null && lc.jitterMs >= 0) {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wJitter, "${"%.1f".format(lc.jitterMs)} ms")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wJitterSub, "mdev")
            } else if (histAll.size >= 2) {
                val tail = histAll.takeLast(10)
                val approx = (1 until tail.size).map { kotlin.math.abs(tail[it] - tail[it - 1]) }.average()
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wJitter, "${"%.1f".format(approx)} ms")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wJitterSub, "تقریبی")
            } else {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wJitter, "—")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wJitterSub, "mdev")
            }
            val lastLoss = HistoryEngine.lossHistory(ctx).lastOrNull()
            if (lc != null && lc.lossPct >= 0) {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLoss, "${"%.1f".format(lc.lossPct)}%")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLossSub, when { lc.lossPct == 0.0 -> "Good"; lc.lossPct < 3 -> "Fair"; else -> "Poor" })
            } else if (lastLoss != null) {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLoss, "${"%.1f".format(lastLoss)}%")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLossSub, when { lastLoss == 0.0 -> "Good"; lastLoss < 3 -> "Fair"; else -> "Poor" })
            } else {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLoss, "—")
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wLossSub, "—")
            }
            val g = Gaming.lastSummary(ctx)
            var grade = "-"
            var profile = "Call of Duty Mobile"
            var region = "Middle East"
            if (g.isNotBlank()) {
                val a = g.split("|")
                if (a.size > 8) {
                    grade = a[5]
                    if (a[7].isNotBlank()) profile = a[7]
                    if (a[8].isNotBlank()) region = a[8]
                }
            }
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wBuffer, grade)
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wBufferSub, when (grade) {
                "A" -> "Excellent"; "B" -> "Good"; "C" -> "Fair"; "D", "F" -> "Poor"; else -> "Not measured"
            })

            // Latency chart: timestamped points from the same data source as the
            // app (failed pings are gaps, not zeros). The stats line comes from
            // the same points, and names the age of the last measurement so an
            // old chart is never mistaken for a live one.
            val pts = HistoryEngine.pingPoints(ctx)
            views.setImageViewBitmap(R.id.wGraph, drawLatencyChart(pts))
            val wst = ChartData.stats(pts)
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wChartStats, if (wst.valid == 0) "Min: —  Avg: —  Max: —"
                else "Min: ${"%.0f".format(wst.min)}  Avg: ${"%.0f".format(wst.avg)}  Max: ${"%.0f".format(wst.max)} ms • last ${ageText(pts.last().first)}")

            // Rows card
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wGameProfile, profile)
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wRegion, region)
            val liveCt = Geo.connectionType(ctx)
            if (liveCt != "—") History.saveConnType(ctx, liveCt)
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wConnType, if (liveCt != "—") liveCt else (History.connType(ctx) ?: "—"))
            val score = lc?.scoreTotal
            if (score != null) {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wScore, "$score / 100")
                views.setProgressBar(R.id.wScoreBar, 100, score, false)
            } else {
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.wScore, if (lc?.scoreStatus == "InsufficientData") "Insufficient data" else "—")
                views.setProgressBar(R.id.wScoreBar, 100, 0, false)
            }
            val tip = when {
                lc == null -> "No data yet — open the app to run a check."
                lc.scoreStatus == "InsufficientData" -> "Not enough data yet — run a check."
                score != null && score >= 80 -> "Excellent connection! Enjoy your game."
                score != null && score >= 50 -> "Connection is fair — check details."
                score != null -> "Connection is poor — see details."
                else -> "No data yet — open the app to run a check."
            }
            views.setTextViewText(R.id.wTip, tip)

            val intent = Intent(ctx, MainActivity::class.java)
            val pi = PendingIntent.getActivity(ctx, 0, intent, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
            views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.wRoot, pi)
            views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.wOverflow, pi)
            views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.wTipBar, pi)
            val gi = Intent(ctx, GamingActivity::class.java)
            val gpi = PendingIntent.getActivity(ctx, 1, gi, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
            views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.wRowGame, gpi)
            mgr.updateAppWidget(id, views)
        }

        // Widget latency chart, rewritten on the same data layer as the app:
        // x is real time (first..last sample), y is ms scaled by ChartData.yMax
        // (P95-based, so one 986 ms outlier does not squash the line), failed
        // pings break the line (a gap + a red tick at the bottom) instead of
        // being drawn as 0 ms. The bitmap is built only when a widget exists —
        // refreshAll() is only called with real widget ids.
        private fun drawLatencyChart(points: List<Pair<Long, Double>>): Bitmap {
            val w = 800; val h = 260
            val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(w, h, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
            val c = Canvas(bmp)
            c.drawColor(Color.parseColor("#101A2E"))
            val grid = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#1E293B"); strokeWidth = 2f }
            val axis = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#7C8AA0"); textSize = 30f; isAntiAlias = true }
            val valid = points.filter { it.second >= 0 }.map { it.second }
            val maxV = ChartData.yMax(valid)
            val padL = 70f; val padR = 16f; val padT = 16f; val padB = 34f
            val plotW = w - padL - padR; val plotH = h - padT - padB
            for (i in 0..4) {
                val y = padT + plotH * i / 4
                c.drawLine(padL, y, w - padR, y, grid)
                c.drawText("${"%.0f".format(maxV * (4 - i) / 4)}", 8f, y + 10f, axis)
            }
            if (points.isEmpty()) {
                val empty = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#94A3B8"); textSize = 32f; isAntiAlias = true; textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER }
                c.drawText("No data yet", w / 2f, h / 2f, empty)
                return bmp
            }
            val t0 = points.first().first; val t1 = points.last().first
            val span = (t1 - t0).coerceAtLeast(1L)
            val xOf: (Long) -> Float = { t -> padL + plotW * (t - t0).toFloat() / span }
            val yOf: (Double) -> Float = { v -> padT + plotH - (v / maxV * plotH).toFloat() }
            // Time labels: first and last sample time (real timestamps).
            val fmt = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", java.util.Locale.US)
            c.drawText(fmt.format(java.util.Date(t0)), padL, h - 6f, axis)
            val lastLabel = fmt.format(java.util.Date(t1))
            c.drawText(lastLabel, w - padR - axis.measureText(lastLabel), h - 6f, axis)
            val line = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#22C55E"); strokeWidth = 5f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE }
            val fillPaint = Paint().apply {
                style = Paint.Style.FILL
                shader = LinearGradient(0f, padT, 0f, padT + plotH, Color.parseColor("#1B4332"), Color.parseColor("#101A2E"), Shader.TileMode.CLAMP)
            }
            val failPaint = Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#EF4444"); strokeWidth = 4f; isAntiAlias = true }
            for (seg in ChartData.segments(points)) {
                if (seg.size == 1) {
                    c.drawCircle(xOf(seg[0].first), yOf(seg[0].second), 7f, line)
                    continue
                }
                val path = Path(); val fp = Path()
                for ((i, pt) in seg.withIndex()) {
                    val x = xOf(pt.first); val y = yOf(pt.second)
                    if (i == 0) { path.moveTo(x, y); fp.moveTo(x, padT + plotH); fp.lineTo(x, y) } else { path.lineTo(x, y); fp.lineTo(x, y) }
                }
                fp.lineTo(xOf(seg.last().first), padT + plotH); fp.close()
                c.drawPath(fp, fillPaint); c.drawPath(path, line)
            }
            // Red ticks at the bottom for failed pings — a visible outage, not a zero.
            for (pt in points) if (pt.second < 0) {
                val x = xOf(pt.first)
                c.drawLine(x, padT + plotH - 18f, x, padT + plotH, failPaint)
            }
            val lastValid = points.lastOrNull { it.second >= 0 }
            if (lastValid != null) {
                val lx = xOf(lastValid.first); val ly = yOf(lastValid.second)
                c.drawCircle(lx, ly, 10f, Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#22C55E"); isAntiAlias = true })
                c.drawCircle(lx, ly, 16f, Paint().apply { color = Color.parseColor("#22C55E"); alpha = 60; isAntiAlias = true })
            }
            return bmp
        }
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/NetworkEngine.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.Future

// Network Engine — the single public API for every network measurement in the
// app. The actual measurement code lives in its workers (Diagnostics for
// sockets/processes, Probes for probe definitions, Gaming for ping bursts);
// this object is the boundary: one place for method labels, timeouts and
// single-flight guards, so no caller can run the same measurement twice or
// mix ICMP and TCP results.
//
// Design decisions (deliberate, see the v2.7 architecture report):
// - No coroutines dependency: NetExecutor (fixed pool + Future cancellation)
//   already provides the required timeout/cancellation semantics. Adding
//   kotlinx-coroutines would grow the APK for no functional gain.
// - No new threads are created here: everything runs on NetExecutor.
// - Every function is bounded: the workers kill processes and close sockets
//   on timeout; nothing here can block forever.
object NetworkEngine {

    // How a ping was measured. ICMP and TCP results are never mixed: the
    // method travels with the result (PingResult.host carries "(TCP)").
    enum class Method { ICMP, TCP }

    // Single-flight registry: one in-flight measurement per key. A second
    // request for the same key is refused instead of stacking a duplicate.
    private val inFlight = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Future<*>>()

    fun submitSingle(key: String, task: () -> Unit): Boolean {
        val existing = inFlight[key]
        if (existing != null && !existing.isDone) return false
        val f = NetExecutor.submit {
            try { task() } finally { inFlight.remove(key) }
        }
        inFlight[key] = f
        return true
    }

    fun cancelSingle(key: String) {
        NetExecutor.cancel(inFlight.remove(key))
    }

    fun isRunning(key: String): Boolean = inFlight[key]?.isDone == false

    // True when the system's ping binary is missing (not the same as "no
    // internet" — callers must report it honestly).
    fun pingMissing(): Boolean = Diagnostics.pingMissing

    // Real ICMP ping burst. Returns an empty result (received=0, loss=100%)
    // when nothing answers — never a fake 0ms.
    fun ping(host: String, count: Int = 20): PingResult = Gaming.pingHost(host, count)

    // TCP connect-time burst, used ONLY when ICMP is blocked. The result is
    // labelled "(TCP)" in PingResult.host so the UI can never present it as
    // an ICMP ping.
    fun tcpPing(host: String, port: Int = 443, count: Int = 10): PingResult =
        Gaming.tcpPing(host, port, count)

    // One-shot ping statistics (avg/min/max/jitter/loss) for the dashboard.
    // Bounded by Diagnostics.pingLines; never returns a fake 0ms.
    fun pingStats(host: String): PingStats = Diagnostics.pingStats(host)

    // Single ping, null when unanswered (used by the live-ping loop).
    fun pingOnce(host: String = "1.1.1.1"): Double? = Diagnostics.pingOnce(host)

    fun tcp(host: String, port: Int): Timed = Diagnostics.tcpTime(host, port)
    fun tls(host: String, port: Int = 443): Timed = Diagnostics.tlsTime(host, port)

    // DNS the honest way: a real query to a resolver. An IP literal needs no
    // resolution and returns ok=false with an "N/A" note — never 0ms success.
    fun dns(host: String, resolver: String = "1.1.1.1"): Timed = Diagnostics.dnsTime(host, resolver)
    fun udpDns(serverIp: String, hostname: String = "google.com"): Timed = Diagnostics.udpDns(serverIp, hostname)

    // Real DNS-over-TLS: TLS handshake to :853, then a DNS query over the TLS
    // stream with the response validated. Not a bare TCP connect.
    fun dot(serverIp: String, hostname: String = "google.com"): Timed = Diagnostics.dotQuery(serverIp, hostname)

    // QUIC: no valid handshake exists in this app, so the status stays
    // Unknown with a reason — never a fake Success.
    fun quic(host: String): Diagnostics.QuicResult = Diagnostics.quicStatus(host)

    // Full probe set (40+, manual only) or the light set (8 probes, for the
    // timer and the background service). One call, one policy.
    fun runProbes(light: Boolean): List<ProbeResult> =
        if (light) Probes.runLight() else Probes.runAll()

    // Route diagnostics (best-effort on a non-root phone; "unavailable" is
    // reported honestly when the ping binary cannot do it).
    fun traceroute(host: String = "8.8.8.8"): String = Diagnostics.traceroute(host)
    fun mtuTest(host: String = "1.1.1.1"): String = Diagnostics.mtuTest(host)

    // DNS-over-HTTPS answers for one hostname, from one DoH endpoint.
    fun dohIps(dohUrl: String, host: String): List<String> = Diagnostics.dohIps(dohUrl, host)

    fun summarize(results: List<ProbeResult>, filtering: String): Probes.Summary =
        Probes.summarize(results, filtering)

    fun networkInfo(ctx: android.content.Context): String = NetworkInfo.summary(ctx)
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/NetworkInfo.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.LinkProperties
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.net.wifi.WifiManager
import android.os.Build
import android.telephony.TelephonyManager
import java.net.Inet4Address
import java.net.Inet6Address
import java.net.NetworkInterface

// NetworkInfo (v2.6): local addresses come from ConnectivityManager /
// LinkProperties of the ACTIVE network — not from WifiManager.dhcpInfo,
// which is Wi-Fi-only and returns "—" on mobile data. Wi-Fi and mobile
// details are reported separately. API 21-34 compatible.
object NetworkInfo {
    private fun linkProps(ctx: Context): LinkProperties? {
        return try {
            val cm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
            val net = cm.activeNetwork ?: return null
            cm.getLinkProperties(net)
        } catch (e: Exception) { null }
    }

    private fun dhcpIp(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val wm = ctx.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
            val n = wm.dhcpInfo.ipAddress
            if (n == 0) "—" else "${n and 0xff}.${(n shr 8) and 0xff}.${(n shr 16) and 0xff}.${(n shr 24) and 0xff}"
        } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
    }

    fun localIp(ctx: Context): String {
        val lp = linkProps(ctx)
        val v4 = lp?.linkAddresses?.firstOrNull { it.address is Inet4Address && !it.address.isLoopbackAddress }?.address?.hostAddress
        if (v4 != null) return v4
        val v6 = lp?.linkAddresses?.firstOrNull { it.address is Inet6Address && !it.address.isLoopbackAddress }?.address?.hostAddress
        if (v6 != null) return v6
        // Fallback: network interfaces (works without an active network object).
        try {
            val ips = NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces()?.toList()
                ?.flatMap { it.inetAddresses.toList() }
                ?.filter { !it.isLoopbackAddress && it is Inet4Address }
                ?.map { it.hostAddress ?: "" } ?: emptyList()
            if (ips.isNotEmpty()) return ips.first()
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
        return dhcpIp(ctx)
    }

    fun gateway(ctx: Context): String {
        val lp = linkProps(ctx)
        val gw = lp?.routes?.firstOrNull { it.isDefaultRoute }?.gateway?.hostAddress
        if (gw != null) return gw
        return try {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val wm = ctx.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
            val n = wm.dhcpInfo.gateway
            if (n == 0) "—" else "${n and 0xff}.${(n shr 8) and 0xff}.${(n shr 16) and 0xff}.${(n shr 24) and 0xff}"
        } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
    }

    fun dns(ctx: Context): String {
        val lp = linkProps(ctx)
        val list = lp?.dnsServers?.mapNotNull { it.hostAddress } ?: emptyList()
        if (list.isNotEmpty()) return list.joinToString(" ، ")
        return try {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val wm = ctx.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
            val n = wm.dhcpInfo.dns1
            if (n != 0) "${n and 0xff}.${(n shr 8) and 0xff}.${(n shr 16) and 0xff}.${(n shr 24) and 0xff}" else "—"
        } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
    }

    fun wifiInfo(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val wm = ctx.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val info = wm.connectionInfo ?: return "Wi-Fi متصل نیست"
            val freq = info.frequency
            val band = when { freq in 2400..2500 -> "2.4GHz"; freq in 4900..5900 -> "5GHz"; freq in 5900..7200 -> "6GHz"; else -> "$freq MHz" }
            "Wi-Fi: ${info.ssid} • باند $band • سیگنال ${info.rssi} dBm • سرعت لینک ${info.linkSpeed} Mbps"
        } catch (e: Exception) { "Wi-Fi در دسترس نیست" }
    }

    fun mobileInfo(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            val tm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager ?: return "موبایل در دسترس نیست"
            val gen = when (tm.networkType) {
                TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_LTE -> "4G LTE"
                TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_NR -> "5G"
                TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPAP, TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_HSPA -> "3G"
                TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_GPRS, TelephonyManager.NETWORK_TYPE_EDGE -> "2G"
                else -> "نامشخص (${tm.networkType})"
            }
            "موبایل: $gen • اپراتور: ${try { tm.networkOperatorName } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }}"
        } catch (e: Exception) { "موبایل در دسترس نیست" }
    }

    fun summary(ctx: Context): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val lp = linkProps(ctx)
        val v4 = lp?.linkAddresses?.filter { it.address is Inet4Address && !it.address.isLoopbackAddress }?.map { it.address.hostAddress ?: "" } ?: emptyList()
        val v6 = lp?.linkAddresses?.filter { it.address is Inet6Address && !it.address.isLoopbackAddress }?.map { it.address.hostAddress ?: "" } ?: emptyList()
        if (v4.isNotEmpty()) sb.append("آی‌پی خصوصی (IPv4): ${v4.joinToString(", ")}\n")
        if (v6.isNotEmpty()) sb.append("آی‌پی خصوصی (IPv6): ${v6.joinToString(", ")}\n")
        val gw = gateway(ctx)
        if (gw != "—") sb.append("گیت‌وی: $gw\n")
        val dns = dns(ctx)
        if (dns != "—") sb.append("DNS فعال: $dns\n")
        val type = Geo.connectionType(ctx)
        sb.append("نوع اتصال: $type\n")
        if (type.startsWith("Wi-Fi")) sb.append(wifiInfo(ctx)).append("\n")
        if (type.contains("4G") || type.contains("5G") || type.contains("Mobile") || type.contains("داده")) sb.append(mobileInfo(ctx)).append("\n")
        val vpn = Geo.vpnOn(ctx)
        sb.append(if (vpn) "VPN: روشن" else "VPN: خاموش")
        if (sb.isEmpty()) sb.append("اطلاعات شبکه محلی در دسترس نیست.")
        return sb.toString().trim()
    }
}

package com.aksell.netwatch

import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.Socket
import java.net.URL
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

data class ProbeDef(val id: String, val name: String, val detail: String, val kind: String, val target: String, val domestic: Boolean = false, val category: String = "intl")
data class ProbeResult(val id: String, val name: String, val detail: String, val ok: Boolean, val ms: Long, val note: String, val domestic: Boolean = false, val category: String = "intl")

object Probes {
    val defs = listOf(
        // DNS / CDN
        ProbeDef("cf", "کلادفلر", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace", false, "cdn"),
        ProbeDef("cfdns", "DNS کلادفلر 1.1.1.1", "DoH", "DoH", "https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query?name=google.com&type=A", false, "dns"),

        ProbeDef("dot-gg", "DoT گوگل :853", "DoT", "DOT", "8.8.8.8:853", false, "dns"),

        ProbeDef("udp-gg", "UDP DNS گوگل", "UDP :53", "UDPDNS", "8.8.8.8", false, "dns"),
        ProbeDef("akamai", "Akamai", "HTTPS CDN", "HTTPS", "https://www.akamai.com/", false, "cdn"),
        ProbeDef("fastly", "Fastly", "HTTPS CDN", "HTTPS", "https://www.fastly.com/", false, "cdn"),
        // Search / general intl
        ProbeDef("google", "گوگل", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://www.google.com/generate_204", false, "intl"),
        ProbeDef("tcp-gg", "TCP گوگل :443", "TCP", "TCP", "www.google.com:443", false, "intl"),
        ProbeDef("icmp-cf", "پینگ ICMP کلادفلر", "ICMP 1.1.1.1", "ICMP", "1.1.1.1", false, "intl"),
        ProbeDef("icmp-gg", "پینگ ICMP گوگل", "ICMP 8.8.8.8", "ICMP", "8.8.8.8", false, "intl"),
        ProbeDef("tcp-cf", "TCP کلادفلر :443", "TCP", "TCP", "www.cloudflare.com:443", false, "intl"),
        ProbeDef("http", "HTTP ساده", "HTTP example.com", "HTTP", "http://example.com/", false, "intl"),
        ProbeDef("ipv4", "IPv4", "آی‌پی نسخه ۴", "HTTPS", "https://api.ipify.org?format=json", false, "ip"),
        ProbeDef("ipv6", "IPv6", "آی‌پی نسخه ۶", "HTTPS", "https://ipv6.google.com/", false, "ip"),

        ProbeDef("telegram", "تلگرام", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://telegram.org/", false, "social"),

        ProbeDef("tls-tg", "TLS تلگرام", "TLS Handshake", "TLS", "telegram.org", false, "social"),
        ProbeDef("instagram", "اینستاگرام", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://www.instagram.com/", false, "social"),
        ProbeDef("tls-ig", "TLS اینستاگرام", "TLS Handshake", "TLS", "www.instagram.com", false, "social"),

        ProbeDef("signal", "سیگنال", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://signal.org/", false, "social"),
        ProbeDef("discord", "دیسکورد", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://discord.com/", false, "social"),

        ProbeDef("youtube", "یوتیوب", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://www.youtube.com/generate_204", false, "video"),
        ProbeDef("github", "گیت‌هاب", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://api.github.com/", false, "dev"),
        ProbeDef("gitlab", "گیت‌لب", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://gitlab.com/", false, "dev"),
        ProbeDef("cfmeta", "کلادفلر Meta", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://speed.cloudflare.com/meta", false, "cdn"),
        // Domestic Iran
        ProbeDef("aparat", "آپارات", "HTTPS • داخلی", "HTTPS", "https://www.aparat.com/", true, "domestic"),
        ProbeDef("digikala", "دیجی‌کالا", "HTTPS • داخلی", "HTTPS", "https://www.digikala.com/", true, "domestic"),
        ProbeDef("divar", "دیوار", "HTTPS • داخلی", "HTTPS", "https://divar.ir/", true, "domestic"),
        ProbeDef("telewebion", "تلوبیون", "HTTPS • داخلی", "HTTPS", "https://telewebion.com/", true, "domestic"),
        ProbeDef("irna", "ایرنا", "HTTPS • داخلی", "HTTPS", "https://www.irna.ir/", true, "domestic"),
        ProbeDef("tcp-aparat", "TCP آپارات :443", "TCP • داخلی", "TCP", "www.aparat.com:443", true, "domestic"),
        // Status pages
        ProbeDef("radar", "کلادفلر رادار ایران", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://radar.cloudflare.com/ir", false, "status"),
        ProbeDef("netblocks", "نت‌بلاکس", "HTTPS", "HTTPS", "https://netblocks.org/", false, "status"),
        ProbeDef("ooni", "OONI", "HTTPS API", "HTTPS", "https://api.ooni.io/api/v1/measurements?limit=1", false, "status")
    )

    private fun httpProbe(def: ProbeDef): ProbeResult {
        val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
        var conn: HttpURLConnection? = null
        return try {
            conn = URL(def.target).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
            conn.connectTimeout = 5000; conn.readTimeout = 5000
            conn.instanceFollowRedirects = true
            conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "NetWatch/1.1")
            if (def.kind == "DoH") conn.setRequestProperty("Accept", "application/dns-json")
            conn.requestMethod = "GET"
            val code = conn.responseCode
            var body = ""
            try {
                val br = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.inputStream))
                val sb = StringBuilder(); var line: String?; var n = 0
                while (br.readLine().also { line = it } != null && n < 5) { sb.append(line); n++ }
                br.close(); body = sb.toString()
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
            val ms = System.currentTimeMillis() - start
            // Captive portal: a generate_204 endpoint MUST answer 204. A 200
            // means a portal or proxy answered instead of the real service —
            // that is NOT a healthy result.
            val is204Endpoint = def.target.contains("generate_204")
            if (is204Endpoint && code == 200) return ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, ms, "Captive Portal مشکوک (200 به‌جای 204)", def.domestic, def.category)
            if (code in 200..399) ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, true, ms, when (def.id) { "ipv4" -> body.take(60); "ipv6" -> "IPv6 OK"; "cf" -> body.lines().firstOrNull { it.startsWith("ip=") } ?: ""; else -> "کد $code" }, def.domestic, def.category)
            else ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, ms, "کد $code", def.domestic, def.category)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, e.javaClass.simpleName, def.domestic, def.category)
        } finally { try { conn?.disconnect() } catch (e: Exception) {} }
    }

    private fun tcpProbe(def: ProbeDef): ProbeResult {
        val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
        return try {
            val parts = def.target.split(":")
            val sock = Socket()
            sock.connect(InetSocketAddress(parts[0], parts[1].toInt()), 5000)
            sock.close()
            ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, true, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, "پورت باز", def.domestic, def.category)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val msg = e.message ?: ""
            val note = if (msg.contains("reset", true)) "TCP Reset مشکوک" else if (msg.contains("refused", true)) "Connection refused" else e.javaClass.simpleName
            ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, note, def.domestic, def.category)
        }
    }

    private fun icmpProbe(def: ProbeDef): ProbeResult {
        val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
        return try {
            val ms = Diagnostics.pingOnce(def.target)
            if (ms != null) ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, true, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, "ICMP واقعی (system ping) ${"%.0f".format(ms)}ms", def.domestic, def.category)
            else if (Diagnostics.pingMissing) ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, "دستور ping در دسترس نیست — نه قطعی اینترنت", def.domestic, def.category)
            else ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, "بدون پاسخ ICMP (مسدود یا قطع)", def.domestic, def.category)
        } catch (e: Exception) { ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, e.javaClass.simpleName, def.domestic, def.category) }
    }

    private fun dnsProbe(def: ProbeDef): ProbeResult {
        val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
        return try {
            val a = InetAddress.getByName(def.target)
            ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, true, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, a.hostAddress ?: "", def.domestic, def.category)
        } catch (e: Exception) { ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, System.currentTimeMillis() - start, e.javaClass.simpleName, def.domestic, def.category) }
    }

    fun runOne(def: ProbeDef): ProbeResult = when (def.kind) {
        "TCP" -> tcpProbe(def)
        "ICMP" -> icmpProbe(def)
        "DNS" -> dnsProbe(def)
        "DOT" -> { val host = def.target.substringBefore(":"); val t = Diagnostics.dotQuery(host); ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, t.ok, t.ms, t.note, def.domestic, def.category) }
        "TLS" -> { val t = Diagnostics.tlsTime(def.target); ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, t.ok, t.ms, t.note, def.domestic, def.category) }
        "UDPDNS" -> { val t = Diagnostics.udpDns(def.target); ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, t.ok, t.ms, t.note, def.domestic, def.category) }
        else -> httpProbe(def)
    }

    fun runAll(): List<ProbeResult> {
        val results = arrayOfNulls<ProbeResult>(defs.size)
        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8)
        val latch = CountDownLatch(defs.size)
        defs.forEachIndexed { i, def ->
            pool.execute {
                try { results[i] = runOne(def) } catch (e: Exception) {
                    results[i] = ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, 0, e.javaClass.simpleName, def.domestic, def.category)
                } finally { latch.countDown() }
            }
        }
        latch.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        pool.shutdownNow()
        return results.mapIndexed { i, r -> r ?: ProbeResult(defs[i].id, defs[i].name, defs[i].detail, false, 0, "تایم‌اوت", defs[i].domestic, defs[i].category) }
    }

    // Light background check: 8 probes that answer "is the internet up and
    // is international traffic flowing" — ICMP, TCP, DoH, HTTPS intl + domestic,
    // UDP DNS, IPv4, TLS. ~2-3s instead of the full 40+ probe run.
    private val lightIds = listOf("icmp-cf", "tcp-gg", "cfdns", "google", "aparat", "udp-cf", "ipv4", "tls-tg")
    fun runLight(): List<ProbeResult> {
        val light = defs.filter { it.id in lightIds }
        val results = arrayOfNulls<ProbeResult>(light.size)
        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4)
        val latch = CountDownLatch(light.size)
        light.forEachIndexed { i, def ->
            pool.execute {
                try { results[i] = runOne(def) } catch (e: Exception) {
                    results[i] = ProbeResult(def.id, def.name, def.detail, false, 0, e.javaClass.simpleName, def.domestic, def.category)
                } finally { latch.countDown() }
            }
        }
        latch.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        pool.shutdownNow()
        return results.mapIndexed { i, r -> r ?: ProbeResult(light[i].id, light[i].name, light[i].detail, false, 0, "تایم‌اوت", light[i].domestic, light[i].category) }
    }

    data class Summary(val title: String, val sub: String, val color: String, val intlPct: Int, val domPct: Int, val confidence: Int, val cause: String)

    fun summarize(results: List<ProbeResult>, filtering: String): Summary {
        val ok = results.count { it.ok }; val total = results.size
        val intl = results.filter { !it.domestic }; val dom = results.filter { it.domestic }
        val intlPct = if (intl.isEmpty()) 0 else intl.count { it.ok } * 100 / intl.size
        val domPct = if (dom.isEmpty()) 0 else dom.count { it.ok } * 100 / dom.size
        val socialFail = results.filter { it.category == "social" && !it.ok }.size
        val intlOk = results.filter { it.id in listOf("cf", "cfdns", "gdns", "google", "github") }.count { it.ok }
        // confidence: filtering only if multiple independent methods fail while controls pass
        val conf = when {
            domPct >= 80 && intlPct < 40 -> 85
            socialFail >= 2 && intlOk >= 4 -> 80
            filtering.contains("TLS") -> 75
            filtering.contains("DNS") -> 70
            ok == total -> 95
            else -> 50
        }
        val cause = when {
            ok == total -> "همه‌ی تست‌ها موفق؛ اختلالی دیده نشد."
            domPct >= 80 && intlPct < 40 -> "علت احتمالی: قطعی اینترنت بین‌الملل در حالی که شبکه‌ی داخلی فعال است (اینترانت). اعتماد $conf٪."
            socialFail >= 2 && intlOk >= 4 -> "علت احتمالی: فیلترینگ پیام‌رسان‌ها/شبکه‌های اجتماعی؛ اینترنت بین‌المللی عمومی وصل است. اعتماد $conf٪."
            filtering.contains("DNS") -> "علت احتمالی: اختلال DNS — پاسخ DNS معمولی با DoH متفاوت است. اعتماد $conf٪."
            filtering.contains("TLS") -> "علت احتمالی: فیلتر SNI/TLS — اتصال TCP برقرار می‌شود ولی هندشیک TLS شکست می‌خورد. اعتماد $conf٪."
            ok == 0 -> "علت احتمالی: قطعی کامل یا بدون اتصال؛ وای‌فای/داده را چک کن."
            else -> "علت احتمالی: اختلال عمومی اینترنت؛ چند منبع هم‌زمان جواب نمی‌دهند."
        }
        val (title, sub, color) = when {
            ok == total -> Triple("سالم", "همه‌ی منبع‌ها جواب دادند.", "#22C55E")
            domPct >= 80 && intlPct < 40 -> Triple("اینترنت داخلی سالم — بین‌الملل قطع", "شبکه‌ی داخلی فعال است ولی دسترسی جهانی قطع شده.", "#F97316")
            ok * 100 / total >= 70 -> Triple("اختلال خفیف", "$ok از $total منبع جواب دادند.", "#F59E0B")
            ok * 100 / total >= 40 -> Triple("اختلال شدید", "فقط $ok از $total منبع جواب دادند.", "#F97316")
            else -> Triple("قطع یا بدون اتصال", "هیچ/کمتر منبعی جواب داد.", "#EF4444")
        }
        return Summary(title, sub, color, intlPct, domPct, conf, cause)
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/Quality.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.os.BatteryManager
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

// Network Quality Score 0-100, weights explained in UI. NOT the same as Gaming Score:
// Gaming Score is benchmark-ping based; Quality Score uses the last full probe run.
object Quality {
    // Quality Score — Phase 1 (v1.9): sealed ScoreResult, Coverage Gate 70%.
    // - A missing component is N/A, never 0. avgMs <= 0 means "no valid ping data" -> ping N/A.
    // - total exists ONLY in Complete. If coverage < 0.7, no total number is shown at all.
    // - Stability stays in PartScores (per the finalized Phase-1 plan); making it an
    //   independent metric (out of the total) is an open decision for after Phase-1 testing.
    sealed interface ScoreResult {
        data class Complete(val total: Int, val coverage: Double, val parts: PartScores) : ScoreResult
        data class InsufficientData(val coverage: Double, val parts: PartScores) : ScoreResult
        object NotAvailable : ScoreResult
    }
    data class PartScores(val ping: Int?, val jitter: Int?, val loss: Int?, val stability: Int?)

    // Pure gate, separately unit-testable at the exact 70% boundary
    // (70 is not reachable with weights 40/20/25/15, so the boundary is tested here directly).
    // One status vocabulary for the app headline, the gauge and the widget:
    // the same score always gives the same word, so the app can never say
    // "Degraded" while the widget says "Offline" for the same check.
    fun widgetStatus(scoreTotal: Int?, scoreStatus: String): String = when {
        scoreTotal != null -> when { scoreTotal >= 80 -> "Connected"; scoreTotal >= 50 -> "Degraded"; else -> "Offline" }
        scoreStatus == "InsufficientData" -> "Degraded"
        else -> "Unknown"
    }

    fun coverageGate(validWeight: Int, totalWeight: Int): Boolean {
        if (totalWeight <= 0) return false
        return validWeight.toDouble() / totalWeight >= 0.7
    }

    // Shared ping table: the same cutoffs feed Quality.compute (max 40) and
    // Gaming.score (max 35), so one ping value always earns the same relative
    // ping score in both. Fractions: <50ms=1.0, <80=0.85, <120=0.65, <180=0.4, else 0.2.
    fun pingPoints(avgMs: Double, max: Int): Int {
        val frac = when {
            avgMs <= 0 -> 0.0
            avgMs < 50 -> 1.0
            avgMs < 80 -> 0.85
            avgMs < 120 -> 0.65
            avgMs < 180 -> 0.4
            else -> 0.2
        }
        return kotlin.math.round(max * frac).toInt()
    }

    fun compute(avgMs: Double, jitterMs: Double, lossPct: Double, okPct: Int, pingValid: Boolean = true, jitterValid: Boolean = true, lossValid: Boolean = true, stabValid: Boolean = true): ScoreResult {
        // Fix 1: avgMs <= 0 is "no valid measurement", not "score 0".
        // Jitter/Loss are judged on their own validity flags, not on ping's flag.
        val ping: Int? = if (!pingValid || avgMs <= 0) null else pingPoints(avgMs, 40)
        val jit: Int? = if (!jitterValid) null else when { jitterMs < 5 -> 20; jitterMs < 10 -> 16; jitterMs < 20 -> 11; jitterMs < 40 -> 6; else -> 2 }
        val loss: Int? = if (!lossValid) null else when { lossPct == 0.0 -> 25; lossPct < 1 -> 20; lossPct < 3 -> 14; lossPct < 5 -> 8; else -> 2 }
        val stab: Int? = if (!stabValid) null else when { okPct >= 95 -> 15; okPct >= 80 -> 12; okPct >= 60 -> 8; okPct >= 40 -> 4; else -> 1 }
        val parts = PartScores(ping, jit, loss, stab)
        val weights = listOf(ping to 40, jit to 20, loss to 25, stab to 15)
        val totalWeight = weights.sumOf { it.second }
        val validWeight = weights.filter { it.first != null }.sumOf { it.second }
        val validScore = weights.filter { it.first != null }.sumOf { it.first!! }
        if (validWeight == 0) return ScoreResult.NotAvailable
        val coverage = validWeight.toDouble() / totalWeight
        if (!coverageGate(validWeight, totalWeight)) return ScoreResult.InsufficientData(coverage, parts)
        val total = ((validScore.toDouble() / validWeight) * 100).roundToInt().coerceIn(0, 100)
        return ScoreResult.Complete(total, coverage, parts)
    }

    fun formulaText(): String = "وزن‌ها: Ping 40 • Jitter 20 • Packet Loss 25 • Stability 15. " +
        "Ping: <50ms=40، <80=34، <120=26، <180=16، بیشتر=8. " +
        "Jitter: <5ms=20، <10=16، <20=11، <40=6. " +
        "Loss: 0٪=25، <1٪=20، <3٪=14، <5٪=8. " +
        "Stability (درصد منبع‌های سالم): ≥95٪=15، ≥80٪=12، ≥60٪=8، ≥40٪=4. " +
        "Coverage Gate: امتیاز کل فقط وقتی نمایش داده می‌شود که پوشش داده ≥70٪ باشد؛ کمتر از آن، فقط «داده ناقص» نمایش داده می‌شود و عدد کل وجود ندارد. " +
        "آستانه‌های Ping تجربی بر اساس RTT هستند (الهام‌گرفته از راهنمای ITU-T G.114 که تأخیر یک‌طرفه را بررسی می‌کند؛ RTT تقریباً دو برابر تأخیر یک‌طرفه است)؛ آستانه‌های Jitter قاعده تجربی هستند، نه استاندارد."

    // DNS Benchmark: response time + success, per resolver. DoH via HTTPS, UDP via real query.
    // (No "System DNS" row: it used InetAddress, whose cache fakes 0ms repeats,
    // and the UDP-based dnsTime would just duplicate the Cloudflare UDP row.)
    data class DnsBench(val name: String, val ms: Long, val ok: Boolean, val method: String)
    fun dnsBenchmark(): List<DnsBench> {
        val out = mutableListOf<DnsBench>()
        val doh = listOf("Cloudflare DoH" to "https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query", "Google DoH" to "https://dns.google/resolve", "Quad9 DoH" to "https://dns.quad9.net/dns-query")
        for ((name, url) in doh) {
            val s = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val ips = Diagnostics.dohIps(url, "google.com")
            out.add(DnsBench(name, System.currentTimeMillis() - s, ips.isNotEmpty(), "DoH"))
        }
        for ((name, ip) in listOf("Cloudflare UDP" to "1.1.1.1", "Google UDP" to "8.8.8.8", "Quad9 UDP" to "9.9.9.9")) {
            val t = Diagnostics.udpDns(ip)
            out.add(DnsBench(name, t.ms, t.ok, "UDP :53"))
        }
        return out
    }

    // Multi-Host Monitoring v2.6: per host, THREE independent methods — ICMP ping, TCP :443, DNS resolution.
    // A host is "down" only if ALL APPLICABLE methods fail; ICMP silence alone is NOT an outage (many hosts block ICMP).
    // DNS is TRI-STATE: for IP literals DNS is N/A (an IP needs no resolution — the old
    // code counted dnsTime's 0ms "success" for IPs, so IP hosts could never fail DNS).
    // For domain hosts DNS is a real UDP resolution against 1.1.1.1.
    // ICMP uses one real ping (pingOnce) instead of a 4-packet pingStats — this
    // cuts a multi-host run from ~40s to a few seconds.
    data class HostCheck(val host: String, val icmp: Boolean, val tcp: Boolean, val dns: Boolean?, val ok: Boolean)
    data class MultiHost(val results: List<HostCheck>, val verdict: String, val durationMs: Long, val perfNote: String)
    fun multiHost(): MultiHost {
        val start = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val rt = Runtime.getRuntime()
        val memBefore = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val hosts = listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8", "9.9.9.9", "www.google.com", "www.aparat.com")
        val isIp = Regex("^\\d{1,3}(\\.\\d{1,3}){3}$")
        val res = hosts.map { h ->
            val icmp = try { Diagnostics.pingOnce(h) != null } catch (e: Exception) { false }
            val tcp = try { Diagnostics.tcpTime(h, 443).ok } catch (e: Exception) { false }
            val dns: Boolean? = if (isIp.matches(h)) null else try { Diagnostics.dnsTime(h).ok } catch (e: Exception) { false }
            HostCheck(h, icmp, tcp, dns, icmp || tcp || (dns == true))
        }
        val okCount = res.count { it.ok }
        val verdict = when {
            okCount == res.size -> "همه ۵ مقصد مستقل سالم‌اند (حداقل یک روش از ICMP/TCP/DNS برای هر کدام جواب داد) — اینترنت و مسیر سالم است."
            okCount == 0 -> "هیچ‌کدام از ۵ مقصد با هیچ‌کدام از سه روش جواب ندادند — اختلال از اینترنت/اتصال خودت است، نه یک مقصد خاص."
            res.last().ok && okCount < 3 -> "مقصد داخلی (آپارات) سالم ولی مقصدهای بین‌المللی خراب‌اند — اختلال بین‌الملل، نه کل اینترنت."
            else -> "بعضی مقصدها سالم، بعضی خراب‌اند ($okCount/${res.size}) — اختلال از مسیر یا مقصد خاص است، نه کل اینترنت."
        }
        val memAfter = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val dur = System.currentTimeMillis() - start
        val perf = "زمان اجرا: ${dur}ms (ICMP تک‌پینگ + TCP + DNS واقعی؛ برای IPها DNS = N/A) • RAM: $memBefore → $memAfter MB • باتری: در این تست کوتاه قابل اندازه‌گیری نیست."
        return MultiHost(res, verdict, dur, perf)
    }

    // DNS Benchmark v1.7: 3 rounds per resolver, Median + P95 + success rate. Cache note: round 1 is cold,
    // rounds 2-3 may be faster due to DNS cache — both are shown, median is reported.
    data class DnsBenchRound(val name: String, val medianMs: Long, val p95Ms: Long, val successPct: Int, val rounds: List<Long>, val cacheNote: String)
    // rounds: configurable, default 20 (minimum recommended for P95). If n < 20, P95 gets a statistical warning.
    fun dnsBenchmarkRounds(rounds: Int = 20): List<DnsBenchRound> {
        val out = mutableListOf<DnsBenchRound>()
        val targets = listOf("Cloudflare" to "1.1.1.1", "Google" to "8.8.8.8", "Quad9" to "9.9.9.9")
        for ((name, ip) in targets) {
            val times = mutableListOf<Long>(); var ok = 0
            repeat(rounds) { val t = Diagnostics.udpDns(ip); if (t.ok) { ok++; times.add(t.ms) } }
            val sorted = times.sorted()
            val median = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[sorted.size / 2]
            val p95 = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[(kotlin.math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.size).toInt().coerceIn(1, sorted.size)) - 1]
            val warn = if (times.size < 20) " ⚠️ هشدار آماری: نمونه کمتر از ۲۰ است، P95 اعتبار پایینی دارد." else ""
            out.add(DnsBenchRound(name, median, p95, if (rounds == 0) 0 else ok * 100 / rounds, times, "دور ۱ سرد (بدون Cache)، دورهای بعد ممکن است به‌خاطر DNS Cache سریع‌تر باشند؛ Median از همه دورها حساب می‌شود.$warn"))
        }
        // DoH rounds
        for ((name, url) in listOf("Cloudflare DoH" to "https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query", "Google DoH" to "https://dns.google/resolve")) {
            val times = mutableListOf<Long>(); var ok = 0
            repeat(rounds) { val s = System.currentTimeMillis(); val ips = Diagnostics.dohIps(url, "google.com"); if (ips.isNotEmpty()) { ok++; times.add(System.currentTimeMillis() - s) } }
            val sorted = times.sorted()
            val median = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[sorted.size / 2]
            val p95 = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[(kotlin.math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.size).toInt().coerceIn(1, sorted.size)) - 1]
            val warn = if (times.size < 20) " ⚠️ هشدار آماری: نمونه کمتر از ۲۰ است، P95 اعتبار پایینی دارد." else ""
            out.add(DnsBenchRound(name, median, p95, if (rounds == 0) 0 else ok * 100 / rounds, times, "DoH روی HTTPS است؛ Cache مرورگر/سیستم روی آن تأثیر کمتری دارد.$warn"))
        }
        return out
    }

    // Disruption classifier: only with sufficient evidence; otherwise Probable / Unknown.
    data class Classification(val type: String, val confidence: Int, val evidence: String)
    fun classifyDisruption(results: List<ProbeResult>, filtering: String, gatewayOk: Boolean, dnsOk: Boolean, intlPct: Int, domPct: Int): Classification {
        val socialFail = results.filter { it.category == "social" && !it.ok }.size
        val intlOk = results.filter { it.id in listOf("cf", "cfdns", "gdns", "google", "github") }.count { it.ok }
        return when {
            results.all { it.ok } -> Classification("سالم", 95, "همه مقصدها با چند روش جواب دادند.")
            !gatewayOk -> Classification("احتمالی: مشکل Gateway / وای‌فای محلی", 60, "پینگ گیت‌وی مودم ناموفق است؛ مشکل احتمالاً بین گوشی و مودم است، نه اینترنت.")
            !dnsOk && results.any { it.category == "dns" && it.ok } -> Classification("احتمالی: اختلال DNS", 70, "DNS معمولی خراب ولی DoH سالم است — پاسخ DNS با DoH متفاوت است.")
            filtering.contains("TLS") -> Classification("احتمالی: فیلتر SNI/TLS", 75, "TCP وصل می‌شود ولی TLS Handshake شکست می‌خورد.")
            domPct >= 80 && intlPct < 40 -> Classification("احتمالی: اختلال مسیر بین‌المللی / ISP", 85, "داخلی سالم (≥80٪) ولی بین‌الملل قطع (<40٪) — الگوی مشاهده‌شده با محدودیت مسیر سازگار است؛ علت قطعی (ISP یا مسیر بین‌الملل) از این داده به‌تنهایی مشخص نیست.")
            socialFail >= 2 && intlOk >= 4 -> Classification("احتمالی: فیلترینگ مقصد خاص", 80, "شبکه‌های اجتماعی خراب‌اند ولی اینترنت عمومی سالم است — مشکل از سرور/فیلتر مقصد است، نه کل اینترنت.")
            results.count { it.ok } == 0 -> Classification("Unknown — قطعی کامل یا بدون اتصال", 50, "هیچ مقصدی جواب نداد؛ نمی‌توان گفت مشکل از ISP است یا دستگاه — وای‌فای/داده را چک کن.")
            else -> Classification("Unknown — شواهد کافی نیست", 50, "الگو با هیچ دسته‌بندی قطعی نمی‌خواند؛ نتیجه «احتمالی» اعلام نمی‌شود.")
        }
    }

    // Network Change Detection: compare current network fingerprint with last stored one.
    fun detectChange(ctx: Context): String {
        val conn = Geo.connectionInfo(ctx)
        val geo = Geo.fetch()
        val net = NetworkInfo.summary(ctx)
        val fingerprint = "$conn | ${geo.ip} | $net"
        val p = ctx.getSharedPreferences("netwatch", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val old = p.getString("last_fingerprint", "") ?: ""
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        return if (old.isBlank()) {
            p.edit().putString("last_fingerprint", fingerprint).apply()
            "اولین ثبت شبکه در $now — $conn"
        } else if (old == fingerprint) {
            "بدون تغییر شبکه از آخرین بررسی."
        } else {
            p.edit().putString("last_fingerprint", fingerprint).apply()
            val line = "تغییر شبکه در $now: $conn • IP ${geo.ip}"
            History.addLog(ctx, line)
            History.addTimeline(ctx, "network-change", line)
            line
        }
    }

    // Stability Timeline: events recorded with exact time.
    fun perfSnapshot(ctx: Context): String {
        val rt = Runtime.getRuntime()
        val usedMb = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val bm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.BATTERY_SERVICE) as BatteryManager
        val level = try { bm.getIntProperty(BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_CAPACITY) } catch (e: Exception) { -1 }
        return "RAM اپ: ${usedMb}MB (Heap) • باتری: ${if (level >= 0) "$level٪" else "نامشخص"} • CPU: اندازه‌گیری دقیق CPU بدون Profiler ممکن نیست — فقط زمان هر بررسی ثبت می‌شود."
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/SparklineView.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View

// Mini sparkline for the Home stat cards (v2.6).
// - Invalid values (NaN, negative) are filtered out before drawing — they
//   used to corrupt the scale.
// - Points spread across the full width (the old code divided by a fixed 59,
//   so fewer points were squeezed into the left corner).
// - Flat data (all values equal) draws a flat line instead of a broken scale.
class SparklineView @JvmOverloads constructor(ctx: Context, attrs: AttributeSet? = null) : View(ctx, attrs) {
    var values: List<Double> = emptyList()
        set(v) { field = v.filter { !it.isNaN() && it >= 0 }; invalidate() }
    var lineColor: Int = Color.parseColor("#2DD4BF")
    var fillColor: Int = Color.parseColor("#142E2B")

    private val linePaint = Paint().apply { strokeWidth = 4f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE }
    private val fillPaint = Paint().apply { style = Paint.Style.FILL; alpha = 140 }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        if (values.size < 2) return
        val w = width.toFloat(); val h = height.toFloat()
        val maxV = values.maxOrNull() ?: 0.0
        val minV = values.minOrNull() ?: 0.0
        val span = (maxV - minV).coerceAtLeast(1.0)
        linePaint.color = lineColor; fillPaint.color = fillColor
        val path = Path(); val fill = Path()
        val n = values.size
        val stepX = if (n > 1) w / (n - 1) else w
        for (i in values.indices) {
            val x = i * stepX
            val y = h - 4f - ((values[i] - minV) / span * (h - 8f)).toFloat()
            if (i == 0) { path.moveTo(x, y); fill.moveTo(x, h); fill.lineTo(x, y) } else { path.lineTo(x, y); fill.lineTo(x, y) }
        }
        fill.lineTo((n - 1) * stepX, h); fill.close()
        canvas.drawPath(fill, fillPaint)
        canvas.drawPath(path, linePaint)
    }
}
